
Monolithic to Microservices Architecture with Patterns & Best Practices

Link:

https://github.com/mehmetozkaya/Design-Microservices-Architecture-with-Patterns-Principles/

https://medium.com/design-microservices-architecture-with-patterns/monolithic-to-microservices-architecture-with-patterns-best-practices-a768272797b2

Java Spring Boot Kafka Microservices 3 Tier Architecture

microservices-app/
├── api-gateway/              # API Gateway Layer
├── user-service/             # Business Logic Layer
├── order-service/            # Business Logic Layer
├── product-service/          # Business Logic Layer
├── notification-service/     # Business Logic Layer
├── common-library/           # Shared DTOs, exceptions, utils
├── docker-compose.yml        # Container orchestration
└── kafka/                    # Kafka configuration

// common-library/src/main/java/com/example/common/dto/UserDTO.java
package com.example.common.dto;

import lombok.Data;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Data
public class UserDTO {
    private Long id;
    private String username;
    private String email;
    private String fullName;
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;
}

// common-library/src/main/java/com/example/common/dto/OrderDTO.java
package com.example.common.dto;

import lombok.Data;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

@Data
public class OrderDTO {
    private Long id;
    private Long userId;
    private List<OrderItemDTO> items;
    private BigDecimal totalAmount;
    private OrderStatus status;
    private LocalDateTime orderDate;
}

@Data
public class OrderItemDTO {
    private Long productId;
    private String productName;
    private Integer quantity;
    private BigDecimal price;
}

// common-library/src/main/java/com/example/common/dto/ProductDTO.java
package com.example.common.dto;

import lombok.Data;
import java.math.BigDecimal;

@Data
public class ProductDTO {
    private Long id;
    private String name;
    private String description;
    private BigDecimal price;
    private Integer stockQuantity;
}

// common-library/src/main/java/com/example/common/enums/OrderStatus.java
package com.example.common.enums;

public enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING,
    CONFIRMED,
    PROCESSING,
    SHIPPED,
    DELIVERED,
    CANCELLED
}

// common-library/src/main/java/com/example/common/event/OrderEvent.java
package com.example.common.event;

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import com.example.common.dto.OrderDTO;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Data
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public class OrderEvent {
    private String eventId;
    private String eventType;
    private OrderDTO order;
    private LocalDateTime timestamp;
}


Project Structure Overview
text
microservices-app/
├── api-gateway/              # API Gateway Layer
├── user-service/             # Business Logic Layer
├── order-service/            # Business Logic Layer
├── product-service/          # Business Logic Layer
├── notification-service/     # Business Logic Layer
├── common-library/           # Shared DTOs, exceptions, utils
├── docker-compose.yml        # Container orchestration
└── kafka/                    # Kafka configuration

        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>

        <version>3.1.5</version>
    </parent>

    </properties>
    
    <dependencies>

            <groupId>org.springframework.cloud</groupId>
            <artifactId>spring-cloud-starter-gateway</artifactId>
        </dependency>

            <groupId>io.jsonwebtoken</groupId>
            <artifactId>jjwt-jackson</artifactId>
            <version>0.11.5</version>
            <scope>runtime</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
    
    <dependencyManagement>
        <dependencies>
            <dependency>
                <groupId>org.springframework.cloud</groupId>
                <artifactId>spring-cloud-dependencies</artifactId>
                <version>${spring-cloud.version}</version>
                <type>pom</type>
                <scope>import</scope>
            </dependency>
        </dependencies>
    </dependencyManagement>
</project>
Gateway Application
java
// api-gateway/src/main/java/com/example/gateway/ApiGatewayApplication.java
package com.example.gateway;

import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.cloud.client.discovery.EnableDiscoveryClient;

@SpringBootApplication
@EnableDiscoveryClient
public class ApiGatewayApplication {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(ApiGatewayApplication.class, args);
    }
}
Security Configuration
java
// api-gateway/src/main/java/com/example/gateway/config/SecurityConfig.java
package com.example.gateway.config;

import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.reactive.EnableWebFluxSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.web.server.ServerHttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.web.server.SecurityWebFilterChain;

@Configuration
@EnableWebFluxSecurity
public class SecurityConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public SecurityWebFilterChain springSecurityFilterChain(ServerHttpSecurity http) {
        return http
            .csrf().disable()
            .authorizeExchange()
                .pathMatchers("/api/auth/**").permitAll()
                .pathMatchers("/actuator/**").permitAll()
                .anyExchange().authenticated()
            .and()
            .oauth2ResourceServer()
                .jwt()
            .and()
            .build();
    }
}
application.yml
yaml
# api-gateway/src/main/resources/application.yml
server:
  port: 8080

spring:
  application:
    name: api-gateway
  cloud:
    gateway:
      routes:
        - id: user-service
          uri: lb://user-service
          predicates:
            - Path=/api/users/**
          filters:
            - name: CircuitBreaker
              args:
                name: userService
                fallbackUri: forward:/fallback/users
        - id: order-service
          uri: lb://order-service
          predicates:
            - Path=/api/orders/**
          filters:
            - name: CircuitBreaker
              args:
                name: orderService
                fallbackUri: forward:/fallback/orders
        - id: product-service
          uri: lb://product-service
          predicates:
            - Path=/api/products/**
          filters:
            - name: CircuitBreaker
              args:
                name: productService
                fallbackUri: forward:/fallback/products
      discovery:
        locator:
          enabled: true
          lower-case-service-id: true
  
  security:
    oauth2:
      resourceserver:
        jwt:
          jwk-set-uri: http://localhost:8081/auth/jwks

eureka:
  client:
    service-url:
      defaultZone: http://localhost:8761/eureka/
    fetch-registry: true
    register-with-eureka: true
  instance:
    hostname: localhost

resilience4j:
  circuitbreaker:
    instances:
      userService:
        slidingWindowSize: 10
        failureRateThreshold: 50
        waitDurationInOpenState: 10000
        permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState: 5

User Entity and Repository
java
// user-service/src/main/java/com/example/userservice/entity/User.java
package com.example.userservice.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Data;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Entity
@Table(name = "users")
@Data
public class User {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    
    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)
    private String username;
    
    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)
    private String email;
    
    private String fullName;
    
    private String password;
    
    @Column(name = "created_at")
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    
    @PrePersist
    protected void onCreate() {
        createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

// user-service/src/main/java/com/example/userservice/repository/UserRepository.java
package com.example.userservice.repository;

import com.example.userservice.entity.User;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import java.util.Optional;

public interface UserRepository extends JpaRepository<User, Long> {
    Optional<User> findByUsername(String username);
    Optional<User> findByEmail(String email);
}

Kafka Producer Configuration

java
// user-service/src/main/java/com/example/userservice/config/KafkaProducerConfig.java
package com.example.userservice.config;

import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.DefaultKafkaProducerFactory;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.KafkaTemplate;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.ProducerFactory;
import org.springframework.kafka.support.serializer.JsonSerializer;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

@Configuration
public class KafkaProducerConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public ProducerFactory<String, Object> producerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "localhost:9092");
        config.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, JsonSerializer.class);
        return new DefaultKafkaProducerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate() {
        return new KafkaTemplate<>(producerFactory());
    }
}
User Service Implementation
java
// user-service/src/main/java/com/example/userservice/service/UserService.java
package com.example.userservice.service;

import com.example.common.dto.UserDTO;
import com.example.userservice.entity.User;
import com.example.userservice.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.KafkaTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class UserService {
    
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    
    @Transactional
    public UserDTO createUser(UserDTO userDTO) {
        User user = new User();
        user.setUsername(userDTO.getUsername());
        user.setEmail(userDTO.getEmail());
        user.setFullName(userDTO.getFullName());
        
        User savedUser = userRepository.save(user);
        
        // Send Kafka event
        kafkaTemplate.send("user-events", "user-created", savedUser);
        
        return mapToDTO(savedUser);
    }
    
    public UserDTO getUserById(Long id) {
        User user = userRepository.findById(id)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("User not found"));
        return mapToDTO(user);
    }
    
    private UserDTO mapToDTO(User user) {
        UserDTO dto = new UserDTO();
        dto.setId(user.getId());
        dto.setUsername(user.getUsername());
        dto.setEmail(user.getEmail());
        dto.setFullName(user.getFullName());
        dto.setCreatedAt(user.getCreatedAt());
        return dto;
    }
}
User Controller
java
// user-service/src/main/java/com/example/userservice/controller/UserController.java
package com.example.userservice.controller;

import com.example.common.dto.UserDTO;
import com.example.userservice.service.UserService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/users")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class UserController {
    
    private final UserService userService;
    
    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<UserDTO> createUser(@RequestBody UserDTO userDTO) {
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
            .body(userService.createUser(userDTO));
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<UserDTO> getUserById(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(userService.getUserById(id));
    }
}
4. Order Service (with Kafka Consumer)
Order Service Configuration
java
// order-service/src/main/java/com/example/orderservice/config/KafkaConsumerConfig.java
package com.example.orderservice.config;

import com.example.common.event.OrderEvent;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringDeserializer;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.kafka.config.ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.ConsumerFactory;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.DefaultKafkaConsumerFactory;
import org.springframework.kafka.support.serializer.JsonDeserializer;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

@Configuration
public class KafkaConsumerConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public ConsumerFactory<String, OrderEvent> consumerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "localhost:9092");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.GROUP_ID_CONFIG, "order-service-group");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, JsonDeserializer.class);
        config.put(JsonDeserializer.TRUSTED_PACKAGES, "*");
        return new DefaultKafkaConsumerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<String, OrderEvent> kafkaListenerContainerFactory() {
        ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<String, OrderEvent> factory = 
            new ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<>();
        factory.setConsumerFactory(consumerFactory());
        return factory;
    }
}
Order Event Consumer
java
// order-service/src/main/java/com/example/orderservice/consumer/OrderEventConsumer.java
package com.example.orderservice.consumer;

import com.example.common.event.OrderEvent;
import com.example.orderservice.service.OrderProcessingService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.kafka.annotation.KafkaListener;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class OrderEventConsumer {
    
    private final OrderProcessingService orderProcessingService;
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = "order-events", groupId = "order-service-group")
    public void consumeOrderEvent(OrderEvent orderEvent) {
        log.info("Received order event: {}", orderEvent);
        
        switch (orderEvent.getEventType()) {
            case "ORDER_CREATED":
                orderProcessingService.processNewOrder(orderEvent.getOrder());
                break;
            case "ORDER_CONFIRMED":
                orderProcessingService.confirmOrder(orderEvent.getOrder());
                break;
            case "ORDER_CANCELLED":
                orderProcessingService.cancelOrder(orderEvent.getOrder());
                break;
            default:
                log.warn("Unknown event type: {}", orderEvent.getEventType());
        }
    }
}
5. Docker Compose Configuration
yaml
# docker-compose.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  zookeeper:
    image: confluentinc/cp-zookeeper:latest
    environment:
      ZOOKEEPER_CLIENT_PORT: 2181
      ZOOKEEPER_TICK_TIME: 2000
    ports:
      - "2181:2181"
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  kafka:
    image: confluentinc/cp-kafka:latest
    depends_on:
      - zookeeper
    ports:
      - "9092:9092"
    environment:
      KAFKA_BROKER_ID: 1
      KAFKA_ZOOKEEPER_CONNECT: zookeeper:2181
      KAFKA_ADVERTISED_LISTENERS: PLAINTEXT://localhost:9092
      KAFKA_OFFSETS_TOPIC_REPLICATION_FACTOR: 1
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  eureka-server:
    image: openjdk:17-jdk-slim
    build:
      context: ./eureka-server
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "8761:8761"
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  api-gateway:
    image: openjdk:17-jdk-slim
    build:
      context: ./api-gateway
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    depends_on:
      - eureka-server
      - kafka
    environment:
      SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE: docker
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  user-service:
    image: openjdk:17-jdk-slim
    build:
      context: ./user-service
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "8081:8081"
    depends_on:
      - eureka-server

      SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE: docker

      - microservices-network

  order-service:

    ports:
      - "8083:8083"
    depends_on:
      - eureka-server
      - kafka
    environment:
      SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE: docker
    networks:
      - microservices-network

networks:
  microservices-network:
    driver: bridge

7. Kafka Topics Configuration Script
bash
# create-topics.sh
#!/bin/bash

# Create Kafka topics
docker exec -it kafka kafka-topics --create \
  --topic user-events \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
  --partitions 3 \
  --replication-factor 1

docker exec -it kafka kafka-topics --create \
  --topic order-events \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
  --partitions 3 \
  --replication-factor 1

docker exec -it kafka kafka-topics --create \
  --topic product-events \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
  --partitions 3 \
  --replication-factor 1

docker exec -it kafka kafka-topics --create \
  --topic notification-events \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
  --partitions 3 \
  --replication-factor 1

# List topics
docker exec -it kafka kafka-topics --list --bootstrap-server localhost:9092

// eureka-server/src/main/java/com/example/eureka/EurekaServerApplication.java
package com.example.eureka;

import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.cloud.netflix.eureka.server.EnableEurekaServer;

@SpringBootApplication
@EnableEurekaServer
public class EurekaServerApplication {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(EurekaServerApplication.class, args);
    }
}

# eureka-server/src/main/resources/application.yml

9. Maven Parent POM
xml
<!-- parent-pom.xml -->
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 
         http://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>
    
    <groupId>com.example</groupId>
    <artifactId>microservices-parent</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0</version>

# Test User Service via Gateway

  }'

# Test Kafka Message
echo -e "\n\nSending Kafka Test Message..."
docker exec -it kafka kafka-console-producer \
  --topic test-topic \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
  --property "parse.key=true" \
  --property "key.separator=:" <<< "test-key:Test message"

# Consume Kafka Message
echo -e "\n\nConsuming Kafka Test Message..."
docker exec -it kafka kafka-console-consumer \
  --topic test-topic \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
  --from-beginning \
  --max-messages 1
Key Features of This Architecture:

3-Tier Architecture:

Presentation Tier: API Gateway handling routing, authentication, and load balancing

Business Logic Tier: Individual microservices (User, Order, Product, Notification)

Data Tier: Each service has its own database (H2 for development, can be replaced with PostgreSQL/MySQL)

Kafka Integration:

Event-driven communication between services

Topics for different event types

Producers and consumers for loose coupling

Spring Cloud Components:

Eureka for service discovery

API Gateway for routing and security

Resilience4j for circuit breakers

Docker Support:

Containerized services

Docker Compose for orchestration

Easy deployment and scaling

This architecture provides scalability, maintainability, and fault tolerance while maintaining clear separation of concerns across the three tiers.

1. API Gateway Patterns
1.1 Single API Gateway Pattern

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     Client Applications                      │
│  (Web App, Mobile App, Third-party Services, IoT Devices)   │
└────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                         │
                         ▼
              ┌──────────────────────┐
              │   API Gateway        │
              │  (Single Entry Point)│
              │  - Routing           │
              │  - Authentication    │
              │  - Rate Limiting     │
              │  - Caching           │
              └──────────┬───────────┘
                         │
        ┌────────────────┼────────────────┐
        ▼                ▼                ▼
┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐
│   User       │ │   Order      │ │   Product    │
│   Service    │ │   Service    │ │   Service    │
└──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

└────────┬────────┴────────┬────────┴────────────┬────────────┘
         │                 │                     │
         ▼                 ▼                     ▼
┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────────┐
│  Web API Gateway│ │  Mobile API     │ │  Partner API        │
│  (BFF for Web) │ │  Gateway (BFF)  │ │  Gateway            │
└────────┬────────┘ └────────┬────────┘ └──────────┬──────────┘
         │                   │                     │
         └───────────────────┼─────────────────────┘

              └─────────────────────────────┘
1.3 Gateway Aggregation Pattern
java
// Gateway Aggregation Service
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/aggregate")
public class AggregateController {
    
    private final WebClient.Builder webClientBuilder;
    
    @GetMapping("/user-dashboard/{userId}")
    public Mono<UserDashboardDTO> getUserDashboard(@PathVariable String userId) {
        
        Mono<UserDTO> userMono = webClientBuilder.build()
            .get()
            .uri("http://user-service/api/users/" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(UserDTO.class);
        
        Mono<List<OrderDTO>> ordersMono = webClientBuilder.build()
            .get()
            .uri("http://order-service/api/orders?userId=" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToFlux(OrderDTO.class)
            .collectList();
        
        Mono<List<ProductDTO>> recommendationsMono = webClientBuilder.build()
            .get()
            .uri("http://recommendation-service/api/recommendations/" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToFlux(ProductDTO.class)
            .collectList();
        
        return Mono.zip(userMono, ordersMono, recommendationsMono)
            .map(tuple -> new UserDashboardDTO(
                tuple.getT1(),
                tuple.getT2(),
                tuple.getT3()
            ));
    }
}
2. Advanced API Gateway Implementation
2.1 Spring Cloud Gateway with Advanced Routing
java
// Advanced Gateway Configuration
@Configuration
public class AdvancedGatewayConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public RouteLocator customRouteLocator(RouteLocatorBuilder builder) {
        return builder.routes()
            // Versioned API Routes
            .route("user_service_v1", r -> r
                .path("/api/v1/users/**")
                .filters(f -> f
                    .rewritePath("/api/v1/users/(?<segment>.*)", "/api/users/${segment}")
                    .addRequestHeader("X-API-Version", "v1")
                    .circuitBreaker(config -> config
                        .setName("userServiceV1")
                        .setFallbackUri("forward:/fallback/users")))
                .uri("lb://user-service"))
            
            .route("user_service_v2", r -> r
                .path("/api/v2/users/**")
                .filters(f -> f
                    .rewritePath("/api/v2/users/(?<segment>.*)", "/api/users/${segment}")
                    .addRequestHeader("X-API-Version", "v2")
                    .requestRateLimiter(config -> config
                        .setRateLimiter(redisRateLimiter())))
                .uri("lb://user-service-v2"))
            
            // Canary Deployment Route
            .route("canary_user_service", r -> r
                .path("/api/users/**")
                .and()
                .header("X-Canary", "true")
                .uri("lb://user-service-canary"))
            
            // WebSocket Route
            .route("websocket_route", r -> r
                .path("/ws/**")
                .uri("lb://websocket-service"))
            
            // Static Content Route
            .route("static_content", r -> r
                .path("/static/**")
                .uri("http://cdn-service:8080"))
            
            // Internationalization Route
            .route("i18n_route", r -> r
                .path("/api/**")
                .filters(f -> f
                    .rewritePath("/api/(?<segment>.*)", "/api/${segment}")
                    .addRequestHeader("Accept-Language", 
                        "#{request.headers['Accept-Language'] ?: 'en-US'}"))
                .uri("lb://i18n-service"))
            
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public RedisRateLimiter redisRateLimiter() {
        return new RedisRateLimiter(10, 20, 1);
    }
}
2.2 Custom Gateway Filters
java
// Custom Authentication Filter
@Component
public class AuthenticationFilter implements GlobalFilter, Ordered {
    
    private final JwtTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        ServerHttpRequest request = exchange.getRequest();
        
        // Skip authentication for public endpoints
        if (isPublicEndpoint(request)) {
            return chain.filter(exchange);
        }
        
        String authHeader = request.getHeaders().getFirst(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);
        
        if (authHeader == null || !authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            return onError(exchange, "No token provided", HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED);
        }
        
        String token = authHeader.substring(7);
        
        return validateToken(token)
            .flatMap(isValid -> {
                if (!isValid) {
                    return onError(exchange, "Invalid token", HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED);
                }
                
                // Add user context to downstream requests
                ServerHttpRequest mutatedRequest = request.mutate()
                    .header("X-User-Id", tokenProvider.getUserIdFromToken(token))
                    .header("X-User-Roles", String.join(",", tokenProvider.getRolesFromToken(token)))
                    .build();
                
                return chain.filter(exchange.mutate().request(mutatedRequest).build());
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<Boolean> validateToken(String token) {
        return redisTemplate.hasKey("blacklist:" + token)
            .map(isBlacklisted -> !isBlacklisted && tokenProvider.validateToken(token));
    }
    
    private boolean isPublicEndpoint(ServerHttpRequest request) {
        return request.getURI().getPath().startsWith("/api/auth/") ||
               request.getURI().getPath().startsWith("/actuator/") ||
               request.getURI().getPath().startsWith("/public/");
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> onError(ServerWebExchange exchange, String message, HttpStatus status) {
        ServerHttpResponse response = exchange.getResponse();
        response.setStatusCode(status);
        response.getHeaders().setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        
        Map<String, Object> errorResponse = Map.of(
            "timestamp", Instant.now().toString(),
            "status", status.value(),
            "error", status.getReasonPhrase(),
            "message", message,
            "path", exchange.getRequest().getURI().getPath()
        );
        
        DataBuffer buffer = response.bufferFactory()
            .wrap(JsonUtils.toJson(errorResponse).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        
        return response.writeWith(Mono.just(buffer));
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return -100; // High priority
    }
}

// Rate Limiting Filter
@Component
public class RateLimitingFilter implements GlobalFilter, Ordered {
    
    private final ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        String clientId = getClientId(exchange.getRequest());
        String key = "rate_limit:" + clientId;
        String limitKey = key + ":limit";
        String timestampKey = key + ":timestamp";
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(limitKey)
            .flatMap(currentCount -> {
                if (currentCount == 1) {
                    return redisTemplate.expire(limitKey, Duration.ofMinutes(1))
                        .then(redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(timestampKey, 
                            Instant.now().toString(), Duration.ofMinutes(1)))
                        .then(chain.filter(exchange));
                }
                
                if (currentCount > 100) { // 100 requests per minute
                    return onRateLimitExceeded(exchange);
                }
                
                return chain.filter(exchange);
            });
    }
    
    private String getClientId(ServerHttpRequest request) {
        // Try to get from API key, JWT, or IP address
        String apiKey = request.getHeaders().getFirst("X-API-Key");
        if (apiKey != null) {
            return "apikey:" + apiKey;
        }
        
        // Get from JWT
        String authHeader = request.getHeaders().getFirst(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);
        if (authHeader != null && authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            return "user:" + extractUserId(authHeader.substring(7));
        }
        
        // Fallback to IP
        return "ip:" + Objects.requireNonNull(request.getRemoteAddress()).getAddress().getHostAddress();
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> onRateLimitExceeded(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        exchange.getResponse().setStatusCode(HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS);
        exchange.getResponse().getHeaders().setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        
        Map<String, Object> error = Map.of(
            "error", "Rate limit exceeded",
            "retryAfter", 60,
            "limit", 100
        );
        
        return exchange.getResponse().writeWith(
            Mono.just(exchange.getResponse().bufferFactory()
                .wrap(JsonUtils.toJson(error).getBytes()))
        );
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return -50;
    }
}
2.3 Request/Response Transformation
java
@Component
public class ResponseTransformerFilter implements GlobalFilter {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        return chain.filter(exchange).then(Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            ServerHttpResponse response = exchange.getResponse();
            
            // Add correlation ID to response headers
            String correlationId = exchange.getRequest().getHeaders()
                .getFirst("X-Correlation-Id");
            if (correlationId != null) {
                response.getHeaders().add("X-Correlation-Id", correlationId);
            }
            
            // Add response time header
            long startTime = (long) exchange.getAttributes()
                .getOrDefault("startTime", System.currentTimeMillis());
            long responseTime = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;
            response.getHeaders().add("X-Response-Time", responseTime + "ms");
            
            // Transform response based on Accept header
            String accept = exchange.getRequest().getHeaders()
                .getFirst(HttpHeaders.ACCEPT);
            
            if (accept != null && accept.contains("application/xml")) {
                // Transform JSON to XML if needed
                transformToXml(response);
            }
        }));
    }
}

// Response Wrapper
@Component
public class ResponseWrapperFilter implements GlobalFilter {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        ServerHttpResponse originalResponse = exchange.getResponse();
        DataBufferFactory bufferFactory = originalResponse.bufferFactory();
        
        ServerHttpResponseDecorator decoratedResponse = new ServerHttpResponseDecorator(originalResponse) {
            @Override
            public Mono<Void> writeWith(Publisher<? extends DataBuffer> body) {
                if (body instanceof Flux) {
                    Flux<? extends DataBuffer> fluxBody = (Flux<? extends DataBuffer>) body;
                    
                    return super.writeWith(fluxBody.buffer().map(dataBuffers -> {
                        // Combine all data buffers
                        ByteArrayOutputStream outputStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                        dataBuffers.forEach(db -> {
                            byte[] bytes = new byte[db.readableByteCount()];
                            db.read(bytes);
                            outputStream.write(bytes, 0, bytes.length);
                        });
                        
                        String responseBody = new String(outputStream.toByteArray());
                        
                        // Wrap response
                        ApiResponse wrappedResponse = ApiResponse.success(
                            JsonUtils.parse(responseBody)
                        );
                        
                        String wrappedBody = JsonUtils.toJson(wrappedResponse);
                        
                        return bufferFactory.wrap(wrappedBody.getBytes());
                    }));
                }
                return super.writeWith(body);
            }
        };
        
        return chain.filter(exchange.mutate().response(decoratedResponse).build());
    }
}

yaml
# docker-compose-multi-gateway.yml
version: '3.8'

services:

    image: springcloud/spring-cloud-gateway:latest
    ports:
      - "80:8080"
      - "443:8443"
    environment:
      - SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=edge
    volumes:

      - ./certs:/certs

      - microservices-network

  # Internal Gateway (Service Mesh)
  internal-gateway:
    image: springcloud/spring-cloud-gateway:latest
    environment:
      - SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=internal
    volumes:
      - ./config/internal-gateway.yml:/config/application.yml

    image: springcloud/spring-cloud-gateway:latest
    ports:
      - "8443:8443"
    environment:
      - SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=partner
    volumes:
      - ./config/partner-gateway.yml:/config/application.yml
      - ./certs/partner:/certs

      - microservices-network

  admin-gateway:
    image: springcloud/spring-cloud-gateway:latest
    ports:
      - "9090:9090"
    environment:
      - SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=admin
    volumes:
      - ./config/admin-gateway.yml:/config/application.yml
    networks:
      - microservices-network
3.2 Edge Gateway Configuration
yaml
# edge-gateway.yml
spring:
  cloud:
    gateway:
      globalcors:
        cors-configurations:
          '[/**]':
            allowedOrigins: 
              - "https://*.example.com"
              - "https://app.example.com"
            allowedMethods:
              - GET
              - POST
              - PUT
              - DELETE
              - OPTIONS
            allowedHeaders:
              - "*"
            allowCredentials: true
            maxAge: 3600
      
      routes:
        # Public API Routes
        - id: public-api
          uri: lb://public-service
          predicates:
            - Path=/api/public/**
          filters:
            - name: RequestRateLimiter
              args:
                key-resolver: "#{@ipKeyResolver}"
                redis-rate-limiter.replenishRate: 10
                redis-rate-limiter.burstCapacity: 20
        
        # Authenticated API Routes
        - id: authenticated-api
          uri: lb://api-gateway
          predicates:
            - Path=/api/**
          filters:
            - name: JwtAuthenticationFilter
            - name: CircuitBreaker
              args:
                name: authenticatedAPI
                fallbackUri: forward:/fallback/authenticated
          
        # WebSocket Routes
        - id: websocket
          uri: lb:ws://websocket-service
          predicates:
            - Path=/ws/**
          
        # Static Assets (CDN)
        - id: static-assets
          uri: http://cdn-service:8080
          predicates:
            - Path=/assets/**
          filters:
            - name: Cache
              args:
                cacheSeconds: 31536000
                cacheControl: "public, max-age=31536000, immutable"
                
        # File Upload Routes
        - id: file-upload
          uri: lb://upload-service
          predicates:
            - Path=/api/upload/**
          filters:
            - name: StripPrefix
              args:
                parts: 1
            - name: RequestSize
              args:
                maxSize: 10MB
                
        # GraphQL Route
        - id: graphql
          uri: lb://graphql-service
          predicates:
            - Path=/graphql
          filters:
            - name: DedupeResponseHeader
              args:
                name: Access-Control-Allow-Credentials Access-Control-Allow-Origin
                strategy: RETAIN_UNIQUE
                
        # Fallback Route
        - id: fallback
          uri: forward:/fallback
          predicates:
            - Path=/fallback/**

            - Path=/db/**

            - name: StripPrefix
              args:
                parts: 1

4. Security & Authentication
4.1 JWT Authentication with Multiple Providers
java
@Component
public class JwtAuthenticationManager implements ReactiveAuthenticationManager {
    
    private final Map<String, JwtTokenProvider> tokenProviders = new HashMap<>();
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        tokenProviders.put("internal", new InternalJwtProvider());
        tokenProviders.put("oauth2", new OAuth2JwtProvider());
        tokenProviders.put("custom", new CustomJwtProvider());
    }
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Authentication> authenticate(Authentication authentication) {
        String token = authentication.getCredentials().toString();
        
        // Detect token type
        String tokenType = detectTokenType(token);
        JwtTokenProvider provider = tokenProviders.get(tokenType);
        
        if (provider == null) {
            return Mono.error(new AuthenticationException("Unsupported token type"));
        }
        
        return provider.validateToken(token)
            .filter(valid -> valid)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.error(new AuthenticationException("Invalid token")))
            .map(valid -> {
                UserDetails userDetails = provider.getUserDetails(token);
                return new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                    userDetails,
                    token,
                    userDetails.getAuthorities()
                );
            });
    }
    
    private String detectTokenType(String token) {
        if (token.startsWith("eyJ")) {
            return "internal";
        }
        if (token.startsWith("oauth2_")) {
            return "oauth2";
        }
        return "custom";
    }
}

4.3 API Key Management
java
@Component
public class ApiKeyManager {
    
    private final ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, ApiKeyInfo> redisTemplate;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    
    public Mono<ApiKeyInfo> validateApiKey(String apiKey) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get("apikey:" + apiKey)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> {
                meterRegistry.counter("apikey.invalid", "key", apiKey).increment();
                return Mono.error(new ApiKeyInvalidException("Invalid API key"));
            }))
            .flatMap(apiKeyInfo -> {
                if (apiKeyInfo.isExpired()) {
                    meterRegistry.counter("apikey.expired", "key", apiKey).increment();
                    return Mono.error(new ApiKeyExpiredException("API key expired"));
                }
                
                // Update last used timestamp
                apiKeyInfo.setLastUsed(Instant.now());
                return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                    .set("apikey:" + apiKey, apiKeyInfo)
                    .then(Mono.just(apiKeyInfo));
            });
    }
    
    public Mono<ApiKeyInfo> createApiKey(CreateApiKeyRequest request) {
        String apiKey = generateApiKey();
        ApiKeyInfo info = ApiKeyInfo.builder()
            .key(apiKey)
            .name(request.getName())
            .permissions(request.getPermissions())
            .rateLimit(request.getRateLimit())
            .createdAt(Instant.now())
            .expiresAt(request.getExpiresAt())
            .build();
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .set("apikey:" + apiKey, info, Duration.between(Instant.now(), request.getExpiresAt()))
            .thenReturn(info);
    }
    
    private String generateApiKey() {
        return "ak_" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "");
    }
}
5. Performance Optimization
5.1 Caching Strategy
java
@Configuration
public class GatewayCacheConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public ReactiveRedisConnectionFactory reactiveRedisConnectionFactory() {
        RedisStandaloneConfiguration config = new RedisStandaloneConfiguration();
        config.setHostName("redis-cluster");
        config.setPort(6379);
        return new LettuceConnectionFactory(config);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, Object> reactiveRedisTemplate(
            ReactiveRedisConnectionFactory factory) {
        
        Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer<Object> serializer = 
            new Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer<>(Object.class);
        
        RedisSerializationContext<String, Object> serializationContext = 
            RedisSerializationContext.<String, Object>newSerializationContext()
                .key(new StringRedisSerializer())
                .value(serializer)
                .hashKey(new StringRedisSerializer())
                .hashValue(serializer)
                .build();
        
        return new ReactiveRedisTemplate<>(factory, serializationContext);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public CacheKeyGenerator cacheKeyGenerator() {
        return exchange -> {
            ServerHttpRequest request = exchange.getRequest();
            String path = request.getURI().getPath();
            String query = request.getURI().getQuery();
            String method = request.getMethod().name();
            
            return DigestUtils.md5DigestAsHex(
                (method + ":" + path + ":" + query).getBytes()
            );
        };
    }
}

@Component
public class ResponseCachingFilter implements GlobalFilter {
    
    private final ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final CacheKeyGenerator keyGenerator;
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Void> filter(ServerWebExchange exchange, GatewayFilterChain chain) {
        ServerHttpRequest request = exchange.getRequest();
        
        // Only cache GET requests
        if (!request.getMethod().equals(HttpMethod.GET)) {
            return chain.filter(exchange);
        }
        
        String cacheKey = keyGenerator.generateKey(exchange);
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(cacheKey)
            .flatMap(cachedResponse -> {
                if (cachedResponse != null) {
                    // Serve from cache
                    return serveFromCache(exchange, (CachedResponse) cachedResponse);
                }
                
                // Cache the response
                return chain.filter(exchange).then(Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
                    CachedResponse response = captureResponse(exchange);
                    redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                        .set(cacheKey, response, Duration.ofMinutes(5))
                        .subscribe();
                }));
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> serveFromCache(ServerWebExchange exchange, CachedResponse cachedResponse) {
        ServerHttpResponse response = exchange.getResponse();
        response.setStatusCode(cachedResponse.getStatus());
        
        cachedResponse.getHeaders().forEach((name, values) -> {
            response.getHeaders().addAll(name, values);
        });
        
        DataBuffer buffer = response.bufferFactory()
            .wrap(cachedResponse.getBody().getBytes());
        
        return response.writeWith(Mono.just(buffer));
    }
}

6. Monitoring & Observability
6.1 Distributed Tracing with OpenTelemetry
java
@Configuration
public class TracingConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public OpenTelemetry openTelemetry() {
        Resource resource = Resource.getDefault()
            .merge(Resource.create(
                Attributes.of(ResourceAttributes.SERVICE_NAME, "api-gateway")));
        
        JaegerGrpcSpanExporter jaegerExporter = 
            JaegerGrpcSpanExporter.builder()
                .setEndpoint("http://jaeger:14250")
                .build();
        
        SdkTracerProvider sdkTracerProvider = SdkTracerProvider.builder()
            .addSpanProcessor(BatchSpanProcessor.builder(jaegerExporter).build())
            .setResource(resource)
            .build();
        
        return OpenTelemetrySdk.builder()
            .setTracerProvider(sdkTracerProvider)
            .buildAndRegisterGlobal();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public GlobalFilter tracingFilter(OpenTelemetry openTelemetry) {
        Tracer tracer = openTelemetry.getTracer("gateway-tracer");
        
        return (exchange, chain) -> {
            Span span = tracer.spanBuilder(exchange.getRequest().getURI().getPath())
                .setSpanKind(SpanKind.SERVER)
                .startSpan();
            
            try (Scope scope = span.makeCurrent()) {
                span.setAttribute("http.method", exchange.getRequest().getMethod().name());
                span.setAttribute("http.url", exchange.getRequest().getURI().toString());
                span.setAttribute("http.client.ip", 
                    exchange.getRequest().getRemoteAddress().getAddress().getHostAddress());
                
                return chain.filter(exchange)
                    .doFinally(signalType -> {
                        span.setAttribute("http.status_code", 
                            exchange.getResponse().getStatusCode().value());
                        span.end();
                    });
            }
        };
    }
}
6.2 Metrics Collection
java
@Component
public class MetricsCollector {
    
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    private final DistributionSummary requestSize;
    private final DistributionSummary responseSize;
    private final Timer requestTimer;
    private final Counter errorCounter;
    
    public MetricsCollector(MeterRegistry meterRegistry) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        
        this.requestSize = DistributionSummary.builder("gateway.request.size")
            .description("Size of incoming requests")
            .baseUnit("bytes")
            .publishPercentiles(0.5, 0.95, 0.99)
            .register(meterRegistry);
        
        this.responseSize = DistributionSummary.builder("gateway.response.size")
            .description("Size of outgoing responses")
            .baseUnit("bytes")
            .publishPercentiles(0.5, 0.95, 0.99)
            .register(meterRegistry);
        
        this.requestTimer = Timer.builder("gateway.request.duration")
            .description("Request processing time")
            .publishPercentiles(0.5, 0.95, 0.99)
            .register(meterRegistry);
        
        this.errorCounter = Counter.builder("gateway.errors.total")
            .description("Total number of errors")
            .register(meterRegistry);
    }
    
    @EventListener
    public void handleRequestMetrics(RequestEvent event) {
        requestSize.record(event.getRequestSize());
        responseSize.record(event.getResponseSize());
        requestTimer.record(event.getDuration(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        
        if (event.isError()) {
            errorCounter.increment();
        }
        
        // Record per-endpoint metrics
        meterRegistry.counter("gateway.requests.total",
            "endpoint", event.getEndpoint(),
            "method", event.getMethod(),
            "status", String.valueOf(event.getStatus()))
            .increment();
        
        meterRegistry.timer("gateway.requests.duration",
            "endpoint", event.getEndpoint(),
            "method", event.getMethod())
            .record(event.getDuration(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
    }
}
6.3 Health Check and Readiness Probes
java
@Component
public class GatewayHealthIndicator implements ReactiveHealthIndicator {
    
    private final Map<String, HealthIndicator> healthIndicators;
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Health> health() {
        return checkServices()
            .map(health -> {
                Health.Builder builder = Health.up();
                
                health.forEach((service, status) -> {
                    if (status.equals("UP")) {
                        builder.withDetail(service, "available");
                    } else {
                        builder.withDetail(service, "unavailable");
                        builder.down();
                    }
                });
                
                return builder.build();
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<Map<String, String>> checkServices() {
        List<Mono<Tuple2<String, String>>> checks = serviceUrls.entrySet().stream()
            .map(entry -> webClient.get()
                .uri(entry.getValue() + "/actuator/health")
                .retrieve()
                .bodyToMono(Map.class)
                .map(response -> Tuples.of(entry.getKey(), 
                    response.getOrDefault("status", "DOWN").toString()))
                .onErrorReturn(Tuples.of(entry.getKey(), "DOWN")))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        return Mono.zip(checks, results -> {
            Map<String, String> statusMap = new HashMap<>();
            for (Object result : results) {
                Tuple2<String, String> tuple = (Tuple2<String, String>) result;
                statusMap.put(tuple.getT1(), tuple.getT2());
            }
            return statusMap;
        });
    }
}

// Kubernetes probes
@Configuration
public class KubernetesProbesConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public ApplicationAvailability applicationAvailability() {
        return new ApplicationAvailability();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ReactiveHealthContributor livenessProbe(ApplicationAvailability availability) {
        return () -> availability.getLivenessState().map(state -> {
            if (state == LivenessState.CORRECT) {
                return Health.up().build();
            }
            return Health.down().withDetail("reason", "Liveness probe failed").build();
        });
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ReactiveHealthContributor readinessProbe(ApplicationAvailability availability) {
        return () -> availability.getReadinessState().map(state -> {
            if (state == ReadinessState.ACCEPTING_TRAFFIC) {
                return Health.up().build();
            }
            return Health.down().withDetail("reason", "Not ready to accept traffic").build();
        });
    }
}

Best Practices Summary
Gateway Design:

Use BFF pattern for different client types

Implement circuit breakers for resilience

Cache frequently accessed responses

Use connection pooling for performance

Security:

Implement multiple authentication methods

Use JWT with short expiration times

Rate limit by client/IP

Validate all inputs at gateway level

Observability:

Implement distributed tracing

Collect detailed metrics

Use structured logging

Set up proper health checks

Performance:

Implement response caching

Use async non-blocking calls

Optimize connection pools

Enable compression

Deployment:

Use Kubernetes for orchestration

Implement auto-scaling

Use ConfigMaps for configuration

Separate secrets management

This comprehensive guide provides a production-ready API Gateway implementation with multiple patterns, advanced security, performance optimization, and deployment strategies for microservices architecture.
