Microservices Architectures API Gws — BFF

Comprehensive BFF (Backend for Frontend) Pattern in Microservices Architecture

Table of Contents:

BFF Pattern Overview

BFF Implementation Strategies

Multi-Platform BFF Architecture

Advanced BFF Patterns

Performance Optimization for BFF

Security in BFF

Testing Strategies

Real-world Examples

1. BFF Pattern Overview
1.1 What is BFF?
The Backend for Frontend (BFF) pattern creates dedicated backend services for each specific client interface (web, mobile, IoT, etc.), addressing the unique requirements of each client type.

text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        Client Layer                             │
├──────────────┬─────────────────┬────────────────┬───────────────┤
│   Web App    │   Mobile App    │   TV App       │   IoT Device  │
│  (React)     │  (iOS/Android)  │  (Smart TV)    │  (Sensors)    │
└──────┬───────┴────────┬────────┴────────┬───────┴───────┬───────┘
       │                │                  │               │
       ▼                ▼                  ▼               ▼
┌─────────────┐ ┌─────────────┐ ┌─────────────┐ ┌─────────────┐
│ Web BFF     │ │ Mobile BFF  │ │ TV BFF      │ │ IoT BFF     │
│ - SEO       │ │ - Offline   │ │ - Remote    │ │ - Real-time │
│ - SSR       │ │ - Push      │ │   Control   │ │ - Low      │
│ - Caching   │ │   Notif     │ │ - Voice     │ │   Latency  │
└──────┬──────┘ └──────┬──────┘ └──────┬──────┘ └──────┬──────┘
       │                │                  │               │
       └────────────────┴──────────────────┴───────────────┘
                                    │
                                    ▼
                    ┌───────────────────────────────────┐
                    │     Core Microservices Layer      │
                    │  ┌─────┐ ┌─────┐ ┌─────┐ ┌─────┐ │
                    │  │User │ │Order│ │Prod │ │Pay  │ │
                    │  └─────┘ └─────┘ └─────┘ └─────┘ │
                    └───────────────────────────────────┘
1.2 Why BFF?
Problems Without BFF:

Over-fetching/under-fetching data

Multiple round trips

Client-specific logic in core services

Different performance requirements

Security concerns

Benefits of BFF:

Optimized APIs per client

Reduced network calls

Client-specific business logic

Better security isolation

Independent scaling

2. BFF Implementation Strategies
2.1 Web BFF with Server-Side Rendering
java
// Web BFF Controller with SSR Support
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/web/api")
public class WebBFFController {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final ThymeleafRenderer thymeleafRenderer;
    
    @GetMapping("/dashboard/{userId}")
    public Mono<ServerResponse> getDashboard(@PathVariable String userId, 
                                              ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        // Check for SEO crawlers
        boolean isCrawler = isSearchEngineCrawler(exchange);
        
        if (isCrawler) {
            // Return SSR version for SEO
            return getSSRDashboard(userId);
        } else {
            // Return JSON for SPA
            return getJSONDashboard(userId);
        }
    }
    
    private Mono<ServerResponse> getSSRDashboard(String userId) {
        return Mono.zip(
            getUserData(userId),
            getUserOrders(userId),
            getRecommendations(userId),
            getUserStats(userId)
        ).flatMap(tuple -> {
            WebDashboardModel model = new WebDashboardModel();
            model.setUser(tuple.getT1());
            model.setOrders(tuple.getT2());
            model.setRecommendations(tuple.getT3());
            model.setStats(tuple.getT4());
            
            // Render HTML with Thymeleaf
            return thymeleafRenderer.render("dashboard", model)
                .flatMap(html -> ServerResponse.ok()
                    .contentType(MediaType.TEXT_HTML)
                    .bodyValue(html));
        });
    }
    
    private Mono<ServerResponse> getJSONDashboard(String userId) {
        return Mono.zip(
            getUserData(userId),
            getUserOrders(userId),
            getRecommendations(userId),
            getUserStats(userId)
        ).flatMap(tuple -> {
            Map<String, Object> response = new HashMap<>();
            response.put("user", tuple.getT1());
            response.put("orders", tuple.getT2());
            response.put("recommendations", tuple.getT3());
            response.put("stats", tuple.getT4());
            
            // Add web-specific data
            response.put("seoMetadata", getSeoMetadata());
            response.put("analytics", getAnalyticsData());
            
            return ServerResponse.ok()
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .bodyValue(response);
        });
    }
    
    private boolean isSearchEngineCrawler(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        String userAgent = exchange.getRequest()
            .getHeaders()
            .getFirst(HttpHeaders.USER_AGENT);
        
        return userAgent != null && (
            userAgent.contains("Googlebot") ||
            userAgent.contains("Bingbot") ||
            userAgent.contains("Slurp") ||
            userAgent.contains("DuckDuckBot")
        );
    }
}
2.2 Mobile BFF with Offline Support
java
// Mobile BFF with Offline Sync
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/mobile/api")
public class MobileBFFController {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final MongoTemplate mongoTemplate;
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, SyncEvent> kafkaTemplate;
    
    @PostMapping("/sync")
    public Mono<SyncResponse> syncData(@RequestBody SyncRequest syncRequest) {
        String deviceId = syncRequest.getDeviceId();
        long lastSyncTimestamp = syncRequest.getLastSyncTimestamp();
        
        return Mono.zip(
            getChangedData(deviceId, lastSyncTimestamp),
            processPendingOperations(syncRequest.getPendingOperations())
        ).flatMap(tuple -> {
            SyncResponse response = new SyncResponse();
            response.setChanges(tuple.getT1());
            response.setNewTimestamp(System.currentTimeMillis());
            response.setConflicts(tuple.getT2());
            
            // Store sync metadata
            saveSyncMetadata(deviceId, response);
            
            return Mono.just(response);
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/offline-queue")
    public Mono<Void> queueOfflineOperation(@RequestBody OfflineOperation operation) {
        // Store operation for later sync
        return mongoTemplate.save(operation)
            .doOnSuccess(op -> {
                // Notify through push notification if needed
                if (operation.isUrgent()) {
                    sendPushNotification(operation.getDeviceId());
                }
            })
            .then();
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/delta/{deviceId}")
    public Flux<DeltaChange> getDeltaChanges(@PathVariable String deviceId, 
                                              @RequestParam long since) {
        // Return only changes since last sync for mobile efficiency
        return mongoTemplate.find(
            Query.query(Criteria.where("deviceId").is(deviceId)
                .and("timestamp").gt(since)),
            DeltaChange.class
        );
    }
    
    private Mono<List<Change>> getChangedData(String deviceId, long since) {
        // Aggregate changes from multiple services
        return Flux.merge(
            getUserChanges(deviceId, since),
            getOrderChanges(deviceId, since),
            getProductChanges(deviceId, since)
        ).collectList();
    }
    
    private Flux<Change> getUserChanges(String deviceId, long since) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://user-service/api/users/changes?deviceId=" + deviceId + "&since=" + since)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToFlux(Change.class);
    }
}
2.3 TV/OTT BFF with Voice Control
java
// TV BFF for Smart TV Applications
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/tv/api")
public class TVBFFController {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final VoiceAssistantService voiceService;
    private final RecommendationEngine recommendationEngine;
    
    @GetMapping("/content/{userId}")
    public Mono<TVContentResponse> getTVContent(@PathVariable String userId) {
        // TV apps need larger thumbnails, different layout, and voice-friendly metadata
        return Mono.zip(
            getPersonalizedContent(userId),
            getContinueWatching(userId),
            getRecommendations(userId),
            getVoiceCommands(userId)
        ).map(tuple -> {
            TVContentResponse response = new TVContentResponse();
            response.setPersonalizedContent(tuple.getT1());
            response.setContinueWatching(tuple.getT2());
            response.setRecommendations(tuple.getT3());
            response.setVoiceCommands(tuple.getT4());
            
            // TV-specific optimizations
            response.setImageQuality("4K");
            response.setLayoutType("grid");
            response.setVoiceSearchEnabled(true);
            
            return response;
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/voice-command")
    public Mono<VoiceCommandResponse> processVoiceCommand(@RequestBody VoiceCommand command) {
        return voiceService.processCommand(command)
            .flatMap(result -> {
                switch (result.getAction()) {
                    case "SEARCH":
                        return searchContent(result.getQuery());
                    case "PLAY":
                        return playContent(result.getContentId());
                    case "NAVIGATE":
                        return navigateTo(result.getDestination());
                    default:
                        return Mono.just(VoiceCommandResponse.error("Unknown command"));
                }
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<PersonalizedContent> getPersonalizedContent(String userId) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://recommendation-service/api/personalized/" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(PersonalizedContent.class)
            .map(content -> {
                // TV-specific enrichment
                content.setThumbnailSize("large");
                content.setBackgroundVideo(true);
                return content;
            });
    }
}
3. Multi-Platform BFF Architecture
3.1 Complete BFF Layer Implementation
java
// BFF Base Configuration
@Configuration
public class BFFConfiguration {
    
    @Bean
    @Qualifier("webWebClient")
    public WebClient webWebClient() {
        return WebClient.builder()
            .baseUrl("http://web-bff-service")
            .defaultHeader(HttpHeaders.USER_AGENT, "Web-BFF/1.0")
            .clientConnector(new ReactorClientHttpConnector(
                HttpClient.create()
                    .option(ChannelOption.CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MILLIS, 5000)
                    .doOnConnected(conn -> 
                        conn.addHandlerLast(new ReadTimeoutHandler(10))
                            .addHandlerLast(new WriteTimeoutHandler(10)))
            ))
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    @Qualifier("mobileWebClient")
    public WebClient mobileWebClient() {
        return WebClient.builder()
            .baseUrl("http://mobile-bff-service")
            .defaultHeader(HttpHeaders.USER_AGENT, "Mobile-BFF/1.0")
            .codecs(configurer -> configurer
                .defaultCodecs()
                .maxInMemorySize(2 * 1024 * 1024)) // 2MB limit for mobile
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    @Qualifier("tvWebClient")
    public WebClient tvWebClient() {
        return WebClient.builder()
            .baseUrl("http://tv-bff-service")
            .defaultHeader(HttpHeaders.USER_AGENT, "TV-BFF/1.0")
            .build();
    }
}

// BFF Router for Different Clients
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api")
public class BFFRouter {
    
    @Autowired
    @Qualifier("webWebClient")
    private WebClient webWebClient;
    
    @Autowired
    @Qualifier("mobileWebClient")
    private WebClient mobileWebClient;
    
    @GetMapping("/**")
    public Mono<ResponseEntity<?>> routeRequest(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        String clientType = detectClientType(exchange);
        
        switch (clientType) {
            case "web":
                return forwardToWebBFF(exchange);
            case "mobile":
                return forwardToMobileBFF(exchange);
            case "tv":
                return forwardToTVBFF(exchange);
            default:
                return forwardToDefaultBFF(exchange);
        }
    }
    
    private String detectClientType(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        String userAgent = exchange.getRequest()
            .getHeaders()
            .getFirst(HttpHeaders.USER_AGENT);
        
        if (userAgent == null) return "default";
        
        if (userAgent.contains("Mobile") || userAgent.contains("Android") || 
            userAgent.contains("iPhone") || userAgent.contains("iPad")) {
            return "mobile";
        }
        
        if (userAgent.contains("SmartTV") || userAgent.contains("AppleTV") ||
            userAgent.contains("Roku") || userAgent.contains("Chromecast")) {
            return "tv";
        }
        
        return "web";
    }
}
3.2 BFF with GraphQL Federation
java
// GraphQL BFF Implementation
@Controller
public class GraphQLBFFController {
    
    private final GraphQLService graphQLService;
    private final DataLoaderRegistry dataLoaderRegistry;
    
    @SchemaMapping(typeName = "Query", field = "userDashboard")
    public CompletableFuture<UserDashboard> getUserDashboard(
            @Argument String userId,
            @ContextValue DataLoaderRegistry registry) {
        
        DataLoader<String, User> userLoader = registry
            .getDataLoader("userLoader", 
                () -> new UserBatchLoader(webClient));
        
        DataLoader<String, List<Order>> orderLoader = registry
            .getDataLoader("orderLoader",
                () -> new OrderBatchLoader(webClient));
        
        return userLoader.load(userId)
            .thenCombine(orderLoader.load(userId), (user, orders) -> {
                UserDashboard dashboard = new UserDashboard();
                dashboard.setUser(user);
                dashboard.setRecentOrders(orders.stream()
                    .limit(5)
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()));
                dashboard.setOrderCount(orders.size());
                
                // Web-specific fields
                dashboard.setSeoTitle(user.getUsername() + "'s Dashboard");
                dashboard.setMetaDescription("View your orders and account");
                
                return dashboard;
            });
    }
    
    @SchemaMapping(typeName = "UserDashboard", field = "recommendations")
    public CompletableFuture<List<Product>> getRecommendations(
            UserDashboard dashboard,
            @ContextValue DataLoaderRegistry registry) {
        
        DataLoader<String, List<Product>> recommendationLoader = registry
            .getDataLoader("recommendationLoader",
                () -> new RecommendationBatchLoader(webClient));
        
        return recommendationLoader.load(dashboard.getUser().getId());
    }
}

// Batch Loader for Performance
public class UserBatchLoader implements BatchLoader<String, User> {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    
    @Override
    public CompletionStage<List<User>> load(List<String> userIds) {
        return webClient.post()
            .uri("http://user-service/api/users/batch")
            .bodyValue(userIds)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<User>>() {})
            .toFuture();
    }
}
4. Advanced BFF Patterns
4.1 Composable BFF with Reactive Streams
java
// Composable BFF Pattern
@Component
public class ComposableBFF {
    
    @Autowired
    private Map<String, BFFComponent> components;
    
    public Mono<BFFResponse> compose(String clientType, 
                                      String userId, 
                                      Map<String, Object> params) {
        // Get composition strategy for client type
        CompositionStrategy strategy = getStrategy(clientType);
        
        // Create reactive pipeline
        return Flux.fromIterable(strategy.getComponents())
            .flatMap(component -> {
                BFFComponent bffComponent = components.get(component);
                return bffComponent.execute(userId, params)
                    .onErrorResume(error -> 
                        bffComponent.getFallback(userId, params)
                    );
            })
            .collectList()
            .map(results -> assembleResponse(results, strategy));
    }
}

// BFF Component Interface
public interface BFFComponent {
    Mono<ComponentResult> execute(String userId, Map<String, Object> params);
    Mono<ComponentResult> getFallback(String userId, Map<String, Object> params);
    String getName();
    int getPriority();
}

// Specific Components
@Component
public class UserProfileComponent implements BFFComponent {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<ComponentResult> execute(String userId, Map<String, Object> params) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://user-service/api/users/" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(User.class)
            .map(user -> ComponentResult.success("userProfile", user))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
            .onErrorResume(e -> getFallback(userId, params));
    }
    
    @Override
    public Mono<ComponentResult> getFallback(String userId, Map<String, Object> params) {
        return Mono.just(ComponentResult.fallback("userProfile", 
            new User(userId, "Guest", "default-avatar.png")));
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return "userProfile";
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getPriority() {
        return 1;
    }
}

// Composition Strategies
public interface CompositionStrategy {
    List<String> getComponents();
    String getClientType();
}

@Component
public class WebCompositionStrategy implements CompositionStrategy {
    
    @Override
    public List<String> getComponents() {
        return Arrays.asList(
            "userProfile",
            "userOrders",
            "recommendations",
            "cartSummary",
            "notifications",
            "seoMetadata"
        );
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getClientType() {
        return "web";
    }
}

@Component
public class MobileCompositionStrategy implements CompositionStrategy {
    
    @Override
    public List<String> getComponents() {
        return Arrays.asList(
            "userProfile",
            "recentOrders",
            "cartSummary",
            "pushNotifications"
        );
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getClientType() {
        return "mobile";
    }
}
4.2 Intelligent Caching Strategy for BFF
java
@Component
public class IntelligentCacheManager {
    
    private final ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final Map<String, CacheStrategy> cacheStrategies;
    
    public Mono<CachedResponse> getOrFetch(String cacheKey, 
                                           String clientType,
                                           Supplier<Mono<Object>> fetcher) {
        CacheStrategy strategy = cacheStrategies.get(clientType);
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(cacheKey)
            .flatMap(cached -> {
                CachedResponse response = (CachedResponse) cached;
                
                // Check if cache is stale but can be served while refreshing
                if (response.isStale() && strategy.isStaleWhileRevalidate()) {
                    // Serve stale content and refresh in background
                    refreshCache(cacheKey, fetcher, strategy);
                    return Mono.just(response);
                }
                
                return Mono.just(response);
            })
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> 
                fetcher.get()
                    .flatMap(data -> {
                        CachedResponse response = new CachedResponse(data);
                        response.setTtl(strategy.getTtl());
                        response.setStaleTtl(strategy.getStaleTtl());
                        
                        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                            .set(cacheKey, response, Duration.ofSeconds(strategy.getTtl()))
                            .thenReturn(response);
                    })
            ));
    }
    
    private void refreshCache(String key, 
                             Supplier<Mono<Object>> fetcher, 
                             CacheStrategy strategy) {
        fetcher.get()
            .flatMap(data -> {
                CachedResponse response = new CachedResponse(data);
                response.setTtl(strategy.getTtl());
                response.setStaleTtl(strategy.getStaleTtl());
                
                return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                    .set(key, response, Duration.ofSeconds(strategy.getTtl()));
            })
            .subscribe();
    }
}

// Client-specific cache strategies
@Component
public class WebCacheStrategy implements CacheStrategy {
    
    @Override
    public long getTtl() {
        return 300; // 5 minutes for web
    }
    
    @Override
    public long getStaleTtl() {
        return 3600; // Serve stale for 1 hour while refreshing
    }
    
    @Override
    public boolean isStaleWhileRevalidate() {
        return true;
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getClientType() {
        return "web";
    }
}

@Component
public class MobileCacheStrategy implements CacheStrategy {
    
    @Override
    public long getTtl() {
        return 3600; // 1 hour for mobile (better battery life)
    }
    
    @Override
    public long getStaleTtl() {
        return 86400; // Serve stale for 24 hours
    }
    
    @Override
    public boolean isStaleWhileRevalidate() {
        return true;
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getClientType() {
        return "mobile";
    }
}
5. Performance Optimization for BFF
5.1 Connection Pooling and Parallel Fetching
java
@Component
public class OptimizedDataFetcher {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = 
        Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(10);
    
    public Mono<AggregatedData> fetchWithTimeoutAndFallback(String userId) {
        // Parallel fetching with timeout and fallback
        return Mono.zip(
            fetchWithTimeout("user-service", "/api/users/" + userId, 
                User.class, Duration.ofSeconds(2))
                .onErrorReturn(getDefaultUser()),
            
            fetchWithTimeout("order-service", "/api/orders?userId=" + userId,
                new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<Order>>() {},
                Duration.ofSeconds(3))
                .onErrorReturn(Collections.emptyList()),
            
            fetchWithTimeout("recommendation-service", 
                "/api/recommendations/" + userId,
                new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<Product>>() {},
                Duration.ofSeconds(2))
                .onErrorReturn(Collections.emptyList())
        ).map(tuple -> new AggregatedData(tuple.getT1(), tuple.getT2(), tuple.getT3()));
    }
    
    private <T> Mono<T> fetchWithTimeout(String service, 
                                         String uri,
                                         Class<T> responseType,
                                         Duration timeout) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri(service + uri)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(responseType)
            .timeout(timeout, 
                Mono.defer(() -> {
                    log.warn("Timeout fetching from {}: {}", service, uri);
                    return Mono.empty();
                }));
    }
    
    // Smart prefetching based on user behavior
    @EventListener
    public void onUserNavigation(UserNavigationEvent event) {
        // Prefetch data for likely next actions
        CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
            String userId = event.getUserId();
            String currentPage = event.getCurrentPage();
            
            switch (currentPage) {
                case "home":
                    prefetchRecommendations(userId);
                    break;
                case "product":
                    prefetchRelatedProducts(event.getProductId());
                    break;
                case "cart":
                    prefetchCheckoutData(userId);
                    break;
            }
        }, scheduler);
    }
}
5.2 Response Compression and Optimization
java
@Component
public class ResponseOptimizer {
    
    public Mono<ServerResponse> optimizeResponse(ServerResponse response, 
                                                  ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        return response
            .flatMap(entity -> {
                ServerHttpResponse httpResponse = exchange.getResponse();
                String acceptEncoding = exchange.getRequest()
                    .getHeaders()
                    .getFirst(HttpHeaders.ACCEPT_ENCODING);
                
                // Apply compression
                if (acceptEncoding != null && acceptEncoding.contains("gzip")) {
                    httpResponse.getHeaders().add(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_ENCODING, "gzip");
                    return compressResponse(entity);
                }
                
                // Apply field filtering for mobile
                String clientType = detectClientType(exchange);
                if ("mobile".equals(clientType)) {
                    return filterMobileFields(entity);
                }
                
                return Mono.just(entity);
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<DataBuffer> compressResponse(ResponseEntity<?> entity) {
        try {
            ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            try (GZIPOutputStream gzip = new GZIPOutputStream(baos)) {
                ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();
                byte[] jsonBytes = mapper.writeValueAsBytes(entity.getBody());
                gzip.write(jsonBytes);
            }
            
            return Mono.just(DefaultDataBufferFactory.sharedInstance
                .wrap(baos.toByteArray()));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return Mono.error(e);
        }
    }
    
    private Mono<ResponseEntity<?>> filterMobileFields(ResponseEntity<?> entity) {
        Object body = entity.getBody();
        if (body instanceof Map) {
            Map<String, Object> filtered = ((Map<String, Object>) body).entrySet().stream()
                .filter(entry -> !isHeavyField(entry.getKey()))
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(Map.Entry::getKey, Map.Entry::getValue));
            return Mono.just(new ResponseEntity<>(filtered, entity.getHeaders(), 
                entity.getStatusCode()));
        }
        return Mono.just(entity);
    }
    
    private boolean isHeavyField(String fieldName) {
        return fieldName.equals("fullDescription") ||
               fieldName.equals("largeImages") ||
               fieldName.equals("videoUrl");
    }
}
6. Security in BFF
6.1 Client-Specific Authentication
java
@Component
public class BFSSecurityManager {
    
    private final JwtTokenProvider jwtTokenProvider;
    private final Map<String, AuthProvider> authProviders;
    
    public Mono<Authentication> authenticate(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        String clientType = detectClientType(exchange);
        AuthProvider provider = authProviders.get(clientType);
        
        return provider.authenticate(exchange)
            .flatMap(auth -> {
                // Add client-specific claims
                Map<String, Object> additionalClaims = new HashMap<>();
                additionalClaims.put("client_type", clientType);
                additionalClaims.put("device_id", getDeviceId(exchange));
                
                if ("mobile".equals(clientType)) {
                    additionalClaims.put("app_version", getAppVersion(exchange));
                    additionalClaims.put("os_version", getOSVersion(exchange));
                }
                
                String token = jwtTokenProvider.createToken(auth, additionalClaims);
                
                return Mono.just(new Authentication(token, auth));
            });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/web/auth")
    public Mono<AuthResponse> webAuth(@RequestBody WebAuthRequest request) {
        // Web authentication with CSRF protection
        return validateCSRFToken(request.getCsrfToken())
            .then(authenticateUser(request.getUsername(), request.getPassword()))
            .map(user -> {
                String token = jwtTokenProvider.generateWebToken(user);
                return AuthResponse.builder()
                    .token(token)
                    .refreshToken(generateRefreshToken(user))
                    .expiresIn(3600)
                    .csrfToken(generateCSRFToken())
                    .build();
            });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/mobile/auth")
    public Mono<AuthResponse> mobileAuth(@RequestBody MobileAuthRequest request) {
        // Mobile authentication with device registration
        return authenticateDevice(request.getDeviceId())
            .then(authenticateUser(request.getUsername(), request.getPassword()))
            .map(user -> {
                String token = jwtTokenProvider.generateMobileToken(user);
                return AuthResponse.builder()
                    .token(token)
                    .refreshToken(generateLongLivedRefreshToken(user))
                    .expiresIn(604800) // 7 days for mobile
                    .build();
            });
    }
}
6.2 Rate Limiting per Client Type
java
@Component
public class ClientSpecificRateLimiter {
    
    private final ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    private final Map<String, RateLimitConfig> rateLimitConfigs;
    
    public Mono<Void> checkRateLimit(ServerWebExchange exchange) {
        String clientType = detectClientType(exchange);
        RateLimitConfig config = rateLimitConfigs.getOrDefault(clientType, 
            new RateLimitConfig(100, 60));
        
        String key = generateRateLimitKey(exchange, clientType);
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key)
            .flatMap(count -> {
                if (count == 1) {
                    return redisTemplate.expire(key, Duration.ofSeconds(config.getWindowSeconds()))
                        .then(Mono.just(count));
                }
                return Mono.just(count);
            })
            .flatMap(count -> {
                if (count > config.getLimit()) {
                    return Mono.error(new RateLimitExceededException(
                        "Rate limit exceeded for " + clientType + " client"));
                }
                return Mono.empty();
            });
    }
    
    private String generateRateLimitKey(ServerWebExchange exchange, String clientType) {
        String identifier = getIdentifier(exchange, clientType);
        return String.format("rate_limit:%s:%s:%s", 
            clientType, identifier, 
            Instant.now().truncatedTo(ChronoUnit.MINUTES).toString());
    }
    
    private String getIdentifier(ServerWebExchange exchange, String clientType) {
        if ("web".equals(clientType)) {
            // Use IP for web
            return exchange.getRequest().getRemoteAddress().getAddress().getHostAddress();
        } else if ("mobile".equals(clientType)) {
            // Use device ID for mobile
            return exchange.getRequest().getHeaders().getFirst("X-Device-ID");
        }
        return exchange.getRequest().getHeaders().getFirst("X-API-Key");
    }
}

// Rate limit configurations per client
@Component
public class WebRateLimitConfig implements RateLimitConfig {
    @Override
    public int getLimit() {
        return 500; // 500 requests
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getWindowSeconds() {
        return 60; // per minute
    }
}

@Component
public class MobileRateLimitConfig implements RateLimitConfig {
    @Override
    public int getLimit() {
        return 200; // 200 requests
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getWindowSeconds() {
        return 60; // per minute
    }
}
7. Testing Strategies
7.1 BFF Integration Tests
java
@SpringBootTest(webEnvironment = SpringBootTest.WebEnvironment.RANDOM_PORT)
@AutoConfigureMockMvc
public class WebBFFIntegrationTest {
    
    @Autowired
    private MockMvc mockMvc;
    
    @MockBean
    private WebClient webClient;
    
    @Test
    public void testDashboardEndpointWithSEO() throws Exception {
        // Mock downstream services
        when(webClient.get()
            .any())
            .thenReturn(mockRequestHeadersUriSpec);
        
        when(mockRequestHeadersUriSpec.retrieve())
            .thenReturn(mockResponseSpec);
        
        when(mockResponseSpec.bodyToMono(User.class))
            .thenReturn(Mono.just(mockUser()));
        
        // Test with SEO crawler user agent
        mockMvc.perform(get("/web/api/dashboard/123")
                .header(HttpHeaders.USER_AGENT, "Googlebot"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.TEXT_HTML))
            .andExpect(content().string(containsString("<title>")))
            .andExpect(content().string(containsString("meta name=\"description\"")));
    }
    
    @Test
    public void testDashboardEndpointWithSPA() throws Exception {
        // Test with regular browser
        mockMvc.perform(get("/web/api/dashboard/123")
                .header(HttpHeaders.USER_AGENT, "Mozilla/5.0"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user").exists())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.orders").exists())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.seoMetadata").exists());
    }
    
    @Test
    public void testTimeoutHandling() throws Exception {
        // Simulate timeout
        when(mockResponseSpec.bodyToMono(User.class))
            .thenReturn(Mono.delay(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                .then(Mono.just(mockUser())));
        
        mockMvc.perform(get("/web/api/dashboard/123"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.user").value("default"));
    }
}
7.2 Performance Testing
java
@Component
public class BFFPerformanceTest {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 60000) // Run every minute
    public void measurePerformance() {
        // Test different endpoints for different clients
        List<String> endpoints = Arrays.asList(
            "/web/api/dashboard/123",
            "/mobile/api/dashboard/123",
            "/tv/api/content/123"
        );
        
        endpoints.forEach(endpoint -> {
            long startTime = System.nanoTime();
            
            webClient.get()
                .uri(endpoint)
                .retrieve()
                .bodyToMono(Map.class)
                .subscribe(response -> {
                    long duration = System.nanoTime() - startTime;
                    
                    // Record metrics
                    meterRegistry.timer("bff.request.duration",
                        "endpoint", endpoint,
                        "status", "success")
                        .record(duration, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
                    
                    // Check response size
                    int size = response.toString().length();
                    meterRegistry.summary("bff.response.size",
                        "endpoint", endpoint)
                        .record(size);
                }, error -> {
                    meterRegistry.counter("bff.request.errors",
                        "endpoint", endpoint,
                        "error", error.getClass().getSimpleName())
                        .increment();
                });
        });
    }
    
    @Test
    public void loadTest() {
        int concurrentUsers = 100;
        int requestsPerUser = 10;
        
        ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(concurrentUsers);
        CountDownLatch latch = new CountDownLatch(concurrentUsers * requestsPerUser);
        
        for (int i = 0; i < concurrentUsers; i++) {
            executor.submit(() -> {
                for (int j = 0; j < requestsPerUser; j++) {
                    webClient.get()
                        .uri("/web/api/dashboard/123")
                        .retrieve()
                        .bodyToMono(Void.class)
                        .subscribe(
                            response -> latch.countDown(),
                            error -> latch.countDown()
                        );
                }
            });
        }
        
        assertTrue(latch.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS));
        
        // Verify metrics
        Timer timer = meterRegistry.find("bff.request.duration")
            .tag("endpoint", "/web/api/dashboard/123")
            .timer();
        
        assertNotNull(timer);
        assertTrue(timer.totalTime(TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS) / timer.count() < 500);
    }
}

// E-commerce BFF Implementation
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/ecommerce/api")
public class EcommerceBFFController {
    
    @GetMapping("/web/product/{productId}")
    public Mono<WebProductResponse> getWebProduct(@PathVariable String productId) {
        return Mono.zip(
            getProductDetails(productId),
            getProductReviews(productId),
            getRelatedProducts(productId),
            getSEOData(productId),
            getInventoryStatus(productId)
        ).map(tuple -> {
            WebProductResponse response = new WebProductResponse();
            response.setProduct(tuple.getT1());
            response.setReviews(tuple.getT2());
            response.setRelatedProducts(tuple.getT3());
            response.setSeoData(tuple.getT4());
            response.setInventory(tuple.getT5());
            
            // Web-specific enhancements
            response.setStructuredData(generateSchemaOrg(tuple.getT1()));
            response.setOpenGraphTags(generateOpenGraph(tuple.getT1()));
            response.setJsonLd(generateJsonLd(tuple.getT1()));
            
            return response;
        });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/mobile/product/{productId}")
    public Mono<MobileProductResponse> getMobileProduct(@PathVariable String productId) {
        return Mono.zip(
            getProductDetails(productId),
            getProductImages(productId),
            getPriceWithDiscount(productId),
            getMobileRecommendations(productId)
        ).map(tuple -> {
            MobileProductResponse response = new MobileProductResponse();
            response.setProduct(tuple.getT1());
            response.setImages(tuple.getT2());
            response.setPrice(tuple.getT3());
            response.setRecommendations(tuple.getT4());
            
            // Mobile-specific optimizations
            response.setImageCompression("webp");
            response.setLazyLoad(true);
            response.setOfflineAvailable(true);
            
            return response;
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/web/cart/checkout")
    public Mono<CheckoutResponse> webCheckout(@RequestBody CheckoutRequest request) {
        // Web checkout with full page flow
        return validateCart(request.getCartId())
            .then(validateAddress(request.getAddressId()))
            .then(validatePayment(request.getPaymentId()))
            .then(createOrder(request))
            .flatMap(order -> {
                if (request.isGuestCheckout()) {
                    return sendGuestConfirmationEmail(order);
                }
                return sendOrderConfirmationEmail(order);
            })
            .then(generateInvoice(request))
            .map(invoice -> {
                CheckoutResponse response = new CheckoutResponse();
                response.setOrderId(invoice.getOrderId());
                response.setInvoiceUrl(invoice.getUrl());
                response.setEstimatedDelivery(calculateDeliveryDate());
                response.setTrackingUrl(generateTrackingUrl(invoice.getOrderId()));
                return response;
            });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/mobile/cart/checkout")
    public Mono<MobileCheckoutResponse> mobileCheckout(@RequestBody MobileCheckoutRequest request) {
        // Mobile checkout optimized for quick completion
        return validateCart(request.getCartId())
            .then(processQuickPayment(request.getPaymentToken()))
            .then(createOrder(request))
            .map(order -> {
                MobileCheckoutResponse response = new MobileCheckoutResponse();
                response.setOrderId(order.getId());
                response.setStatus("CONFIRMED");
                response.setPushNotification(preparePushNotification(order));
                response.setWalletCredits(calculateCashback(order));
                return response;
            });
    }
}

            response.setEmbeddedVideo(true);
            
            return response;
        });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/mobile/feed/{userId}")
    public Mono<MobileFeedResponse> getMobileFeed(@PathVariable String userId,
                                                   @RequestParam String cursor) {

        return getFeedPostsCursor(userId, cursor, 20)
            .flatMap(posts -> Mono.zip(
                Mono.just(posts),
                getStoriesMobile(userId),
                getCachedProfileInfo(userId),
                getMobileOptimizedAds(userId)
            )).map(tuple -> {

                response.setStories(tuple.getT2());
                response.setProfile(tuple.getT3());
                response.setAds(tuple.getT4());
                
                // Mobile optimizations
                response.setNextCursor(generateNextCursor(tuple.getT1()));
                response.setDataSaverMode(true);
                response.setLowQualityImages(true);
                
                return response;
            });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/web/post")
    public Mono<WebPostResponse> createWebPost(@RequestBody WebPostRequest request) {
        // Web post with rich media support
        return uploadMedia(request.getMedia())
            .flatMap(mediaUrls -> {
                Post post = new Post();
                post.setContent(request.getContent());
                post.setMedia(mediaUrls);
                post.setTags(request.getTags());
                post.setLocation(request.getLocation());
                
                return createPost(post);
            })
            .flatMap(post -> {
                if (request.isSchedulePost()) {
                    return schedulePost(post, request.getScheduledTime());
                }
                return publishPost(post);
            })
            .map(post -> {
                WebPostResponse response = new WebPostResponse();
                response.setPostId(post.getId());
                response.setShareUrl(generateShareUrl(post));
                response.setEmbedCode(generateEmbedCode(post));
                return response;
            });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/mobile/story")
    public Mono<MobileStoryResponse> createMobileStory(@RequestBody MobileStoryRequest request) {
        // Mobile story with AR filters and effects
        return applyARFilters(request.getImage(), request.getFilters())
            .flatMap(filteredImage -> {
                Story story = new Story();
                story.setImage(filteredImage);
                story.setDuration(15);
                story.setBackgroundMusic(request.getMusic());
                story.setStickers(request.getStickers());
                
                return createStory(story);
            })
            .map(story -> {
                MobileStoryResponse response = new MobileStoryResponse();
                response.setStoryId(story.getId());
                response.setViewUrl(generateStoryUrl(story));
                response.setExpiresIn(86400); // 24 hours
                return response;
            });
    }
}
8.3 IoT Platform BFF
java
// IoT BFF Implementation
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/iot/api")
public class IoTBFFController {
    
    private final WebSocketHandler webSocketHandler;
    private final InfluxDBClient influxDBClient;
    
    @GetMapping("/devices/{deviceId}/telemetry")
    public Flux<TelemetryData> getDeviceTelemetry(@PathVariable String deviceId,
                                                    @RequestParam long from,
                                                    @RequestParam long to) {
        // IoT BFF with time-series data optimization
        return Flux.create(sink -> {
            Flux<TelemetryData> flux = influxDBClient.query(
                FluxQuery.builder()
                    .bucket("telemetry")
                    .range(from, to)
                    .filter("device_id", deviceId)
                    .build()
            );
            
            flux.subscribe(
                sink::next,
                sink::error,
                sink::complete
            );
        })
        .limitRate(100) // Backpressure handling
        .doOnNext(data -> {
            // Apply IoT-specific transformations
            data.setUnits(convertUnits(data.getRawValue(), data.getSensorType()));
            data.setQuality(checkDataQuality(data));
            
            // Add metadata for IoT dashboard
            data.setBatteryLevel(getBatteryLevel(deviceId));
            data.setSignalStrength(getSignalStrength(deviceId));
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/device/command")
    public Mono<CommandResponse> sendDeviceCommand(@RequestBody DeviceCommand command) {
        // IoT command handling with retry and confirmation
        return validateDeviceStatus(command.getDeviceId())
            .flatMap(status -> {
                if (!status.isOnline()) {
                    return queueCommandForLater(command);
                }
                
                return sendCommand(command)
                    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                    .retryWhen(Retry.backoff(3, Duration.ofSeconds(1))
                        .filter(throwable -> throwable instanceof TimeoutException))
                    .flatMap(response -> {
                        if (!response.isAcknowledged()) {
                            return handleCommandFailure(command, response);
                        }
                        return Mono.just(response);
                    });
            })
            .map(response -> {
                CommandResponse result = new CommandResponse();
                result.setCommandId(response.getId());
                result.setStatus(response.getStatus());
                result.setExecutionTime(response.getLatency());
                
                // IoT-specific tracking
                result.setMqttTopic(generateMqttTopic(command));
                result.setWebhookUrl(generateWebhook(command));
                
                return result;
            });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/dashboard/real-time")
    public Flux<DashboardData> getRealTimeDashboard() {
        // WebSocket streaming for real-time IoT dashboard
        return webSocketHandler.getStream()
            .window(Duration.ofSeconds(1))
            .flatMap(window -> window.collectList())
            .map(events -> {
                DashboardData data = new DashboardData();
                data.setEvents(events);
                data.setTimestamp(Instant.now());
                data.setMetrics(calculateMetrics(events));
                
                // Dashboard optimizations
                data.setChartData(optimizeForCharting(events));
                data.setAlerts(detectAnomalies(events));
                
                return data;
            });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/alert/configure")
    public Mono<AlertConfiguration> configureAlert(@RequestBody AlertConfigRequest request) {
        // IoT alert configuration with ML-based thresholds
        return analyzeHistoricalData(request.getDeviceType())
            .flatMap(analysis -> {
                AlertConfiguration config = new AlertConfiguration();
                config.setDeviceType(request.getDeviceType());
                config.setThreshold(analysis.getRecommendedThreshold());
                config.setAnomalyDetection(true);
                config.setMlModelId(analysis.getModelId());
                
                if (request.isSmartAlert()) {
                    config.setSmartAlert(true);
                    config.setLearningPeriod(7); // 7 days learning period
                    config.setFeedbackLoop(true);
                }
                
                return saveAlertConfiguration(config);
            })
            .map(config -> {
                config.setWebhookUrl(generateAlertWebhook(request));
                config.setSmsEnabled(request.isSmsEnabled());
                config.setEmailRecipients(request.getEmailRecipients());
                return config;
            });
    }
}
Best Practices Summary
Architecture Decisions
Separate BFFs for each client type - Different requirements for web, mobile, TV, IoT

Use GraphQL federation for complex data aggregation

Implement caching strategies per client type

Design for offline support in mobile BFFs

Performance Considerations
Parallel data fetching with timeout handling

Response compression based on client capabilities

Field filtering to reduce payload size

Connection pooling and keep-alive connections

Security Guidelines
Client-specific authentication with appropriate token lifetimes

Different rate limits per client type

CSRF protection for web BFF

Device registration for mobile BFF

Monitoring & Observability
Client-specific metrics for performance tracking

Distributed tracing across BFF and downstream services

Error tracking with client context

Real user monitoring (RUM) for web BFF

Deployment Strategies
Independent scaling per BFF based on traffic patterns

Canary deployments for BFF changes

A/B testing support in BFF layer

Blue-green deployments for zero downtime

This comprehensive BFF implementation provides a robust, scalable, and maintainable architecture for handling multiple client types in a microservices environment.

1.3 Service Registration with Custom Metadata
java
// Custom Service Registration
@Component
public class CustomServiceRegistration implements ApplicationListener<ApplicationReadyEvent> {
    
    private final EurekaClient eurekaClient;
    private final ApplicationInfoManager applicationInfoManager;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    
    @Override
    public void onApplicationEvent(ApplicationReadyEvent event) {
        // Register with extended metadata
        InstanceInfo instanceInfo = InstanceInfo.Builder.newBuilder()
            .setAppName("user-service")
            .setInstanceId(getInstanceId())
            .setHostName(getHostName())
            .setIPAddr(getIPAddress())
            .setPort(8081)
            .setSecurePort(8443)
            .setDataCenterInfo(new MyDataCenterInfo())
            .setMetadata(getServiceMetadata())
            .setStatus(InstanceInfo.Status.UP)
            .setLeaseInfo(getLeaseInfo())
            .build();
        
        applicationInfoManager.registerAppMetadata(instanceInfo);
        
        // Register metrics
        meterRegistry.gauge("service.registration.time", System.currentTimeMillis());
        
        // Start heartbeat thread
        startHeartbeatThread();
    }
    
    private Map<String, String> getServiceMetadata() {
        return Map.of(
            "version", getAppVersion(),
            "git-commit", getGitCommit(),
            "build-time", getBuildTime(),
            "region", System.getenv("REGION"),
            "availability-zone", System.getenv("AZ"),
            "instance-type", System.getenv("INSTANCE_TYPE"),
            "capacity", String.valueOf(getServiceCapacity()),
            "features", String.join(",", getSupportedFeatures())
        );
    }
    
    private LeaseInfo getLeaseInfo() {
        return LeaseInfo.Builder.newBuilder()
            .setRenewalIntervalInSecs(30)
            .setDurationInSecs(90)
            .setRegistrationTimestamp(System.currentTimeMillis())
            .setLastRenewalTimestamp(System.currentTimeMillis())
            .build();
    }
    
    private void startHeartbeatThread() {
        ScheduledExecutorService executor = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor();
        executor.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
            try {
                InstanceInfo currentInfo = applicationInfoManager.getInfo();
                applicationInfoManager.setInstanceStatus(InstanceInfo.Status.UP);
                
                // Report custom metrics in heartbeat
                reportCustomMetrics();
                
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Heartbeat failed", e);
                meterRegistry.counter("service.heartbeat.failures").increment();
            }
        }, 30, 30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
}
1.4 Advanced Service Discovery
java
// Service Discovery with Load Balancing
@Component
public class AdvancedServiceDiscovery {
    
    private final DiscoveryClient discoveryClient;
    private final LoadBalancerClient loadBalancerClient;
    private final Cache<String, List<ServiceInstance>> serviceCache;
    
    public AdvancedServiceDiscovery() {
        this.serviceCache = Caffeine.newBuilder()
            .expireAfterWrite(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .maximumSize(100)
            .recordStats()
            .build();
    }
    
    public Mono<ServiceInstance> getServiceInstance(String serviceName, 
                                                     RequestContext context) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            List<ServiceInstance> instances = serviceCache.get(serviceName,
                key -> discoveryClient.getInstances(key));
            
            if (instances.isEmpty()) {
                throw new ServiceNotFoundException("No instances found for: " + serviceName);
            }
            
            // Apply load balancing strategy based on context
            return selectInstance(instances, context);
        });
    }
    
    private ServiceInstance selectInstance(List<ServiceInstance> instances, 
                                           RequestContext context) {
        // Zone-aware routing
        String userZone = context.getZone();
        List<ServiceInstance> zoneInstances = instances.stream()
            .filter(instance -> userZone.equals(instance.getMetadata().get("zone")))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        if (!zoneInstances.isEmpty()) {
            instances = zoneInstances;
        }
        
        // Weighted response time routing
        if (context.isPerformanceSensitive()) {
            return selectByResponseTime(instances);
        }
        
        // Sticky session support
        if (context.hasSessionId()) {
            return selectByStickySession(instances, context.getSessionId());
        }
        
        // Default to round-robin
        return selectRoundRobin(instances, context);
    }
    
    private ServiceInstance selectByResponseTime(List<ServiceInstance> instances) {
        return instances.stream()
            .min(Comparator.comparingDouble(this::getAverageResponseTime))
            .orElseThrow();
    }
    
    private double getAverageResponseTime(ServiceInstance instance) {
        String metricKey = "service.response_time." + instance.getInstanceId();
        return meterRegistry.find(metricKey)
            .timer()
            .map(timer -> timer.totalTime(TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS) / timer.count())
            .orElse(100.0);
    }
    
    @EventListener
    public void onServiceChange(HeartbeatEvent event) {
        // Clear cache on service changes
        serviceCache.invalidateAll();
        meterRegistry.counter("service.cache.invalidations").increment();
    }
}

│                                 └──────────────┘        │
