2.2 Simple Aggregator Implementation
java
// Simple Aggregator Pattern
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/aggregator")
public class SimpleAggregatorController {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final AggregationService aggregationService;
    
    @GetMapping("/user-dashboard/{userId}")
    public Mono<UserDashboard> getUserDashboard(@PathVariable String userId) {
        // Sequential aggregation
        return aggregationService.getUserDetails(userId)
            .flatMap(user -> 
                aggregationService.getUserOrders(userId)
                    .zipWith(aggregationService.getUserPreferences(userId))
                    .map(tuple -> {
                        UserDashboard dashboard = new UserDashboard();
                        dashboard.setUser(user);
                        dashboard.setOrders(tuple.getT1());
                        dashboard.setPreferences(tuple.getT2());
                        return dashboard;
                    })
            );
    }
}

@Service
public class AggregationService {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    
    public Mono<User> getUserDetails(String userId) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://user-service/api/users/" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(User.class)
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
            .onErrorResume(e -> Mono.just(getDefaultUser(userId)));
    }
    
    public Mono<List<Order>> getUserOrders(String userId) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://order-service/api/orders?userId=" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToFlux(Order.class)
            .collectList()
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
            .onErrorReturn(Collections.emptyList());
    }
    
    public Mono<UserPreferences> getUserPreferences(String userId) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://preference-service/api/preferences/" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(UserPreferences.class)
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
            .onErrorReturn(new UserPreferences());
    }
}
2.3 Parallel Aggregator Pattern
java
// Parallel Aggregator with Reactive Streams
@Component
public class ParallelAggregator {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = 
        Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(20);
    
    public Mono<AggregatedResponse> aggregateParallel(String userId) {
        List<Mono<ComponentResponse>> components = Arrays.asList(
            fetchUserProfile(userId),
            fetchUserOrders(userId),
            fetchRecommendations(userId),
            fetchNotifications(userId),
            fetchAnalytics(userId)
        );
        
        // Parallel execution with backpressure
        return Flux.merge(components)
            .collectList()
            .map(this::combineResults)
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
            .onErrorResume(this::handlePartialFailure);
    }
    
    private Mono<ComponentResponse> fetchUserProfile(String userId) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://user-service/api/users/" + userId)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(UserProfile.class)
            .map(profile -> new ComponentResponse("profile", profile))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
            .onErrorReturn(new ComponentResponse("profile", null, true));
    }
    
    private Mono<ComponentResponse> fetchUserOrders(String userId) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://order-service/api/orders?userId=" + userId + "&limit=10")
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToFlux(Order.class)
            .collectList()
            .map(orders -> new ComponentResponse("orders", orders))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
            .onErrorReturn(new ComponentResponse("orders", Collections.emptyList(), true));
    }
    
    private AggregatedResponse combineResults(List<ComponentResponse> responses) {
        AggregatedResponse result = new AggregatedResponse();
        
        responses.forEach(response -> {
            switch (response.getComponentName()) {
                case "profile":
                    result.setUserProfile((UserProfile) response.getData());
                    break;
                case "orders":
                    result.setOrders((List<Order>) response.getData());
                    break;
                case "recommendations":
                    result.setRecommendations((List<Product>) response.getData());
                    break;
                case "notifications":
                    result.setNotifications((List<Notification>) response.getData());
                    break;
                case "analytics":
                    result.setAnalytics((Map<String, Object>) response.getData());
                    break;
            }
            
            if (response.isError()) {
                result.addPartialError(response.getComponentName());
            }
        });
        
        result.setTimestamp(Instant.now());
        result.setAggregationTime(calculateAggregationTime());
        
        return result;
    }
    
    // Smart parallel execution with priority
    public Mono<AggregatedResponse> aggregateWithPriority(String userId) {
        // Critical components first
        Mono<ComponentResponse> critical = fetchCriticalData(userId);
        
        // Non-critical components with lower priority
        List<Mono<ComponentResponse>> nonCritical = Arrays.asList(
            fetchRecommendations(userId).subscribeOn(Schedulers.parallel()),
            fetchAnalytics(userId).subscribeOn(Schedulers.parallel())
        );
        
        return critical.flatMap(criticalData ->
            Flux.merge(nonCritical)
                .collectList()
                .map(nonCriticalData -> combineWithPriority(criticalData, nonCriticalData))
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3), 
                    Mono.just(createTimeoutResponse(criticalData)))
        );
    }
}
2.4 Chain Aggregator Pattern
java
// Chain Aggregator for Sequential Processing
@Component
public class ChainAggregator {
    
    private final List<AggregatorChain> chains;
    
    @Autowired
    public ChainAggregator(List<AggregatorChain> chains) {
        this.chains = chains.stream()
            .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(AggregatorChain::getOrder))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }
    
    public Mono<AggregationContext> executeChain(AggregationContext context) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(chains)
            .reduce(Mono.just(context), (mono, chain) ->
                mono.flatMap(chain::execute))
            .flatMap(Function.identity());
    }
}

// Chain Interface
public interface AggregatorChain {
    Mono<AggregationContext> execute(AggregationContext context);
    int getOrder();
}

// Concrete Chain Implementations
@Component
@Order(1)
public class UserValidationChain implements AggregatorChain {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<AggregationContext> execute(AggregationContext context) {
        return webClient.get()
            .uri("http://user-service/api/users/validate/" + context.getUserId())
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(Boolean.class)
            .flatMap(isValid -> {
                if (!isValid) {
                    return Mono.error(new InvalidUserException());
                }
                context.setUserValidated(true);
                return Mono.just(context);
            });
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return 1;
    }
}

@Component
@Order(2)
public class DataEnrichmentChain implements AggregatorChain {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<AggregationContext> execute(AggregationContext context) {
        return Mono.zip(
            fetchUserData(context.getUserId()),
            fetchUserPermissions(context.getUserId())
        ).map(tuple -> {
            context.setUserData(tuple.getT1());
            context.setPermissions(tuple.getT2());
            return context;
        });
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return 2;
    }
}

@Component
@Order(3)
public class BusinessLogicChain implements AggregatorChain {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<AggregationContext> execute(AggregationContext context) {
        // Apply business rules
        return applyBusinessRules(context)
            .map(context::setProcessedData)
            .thenReturn(context);
    }
    
    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
        return 3;
    }
}
2.5 Scatter-Gather Pattern
java
// Scatter-Gather Pattern Implementation
@Component
public class ScatterGatherAggregator {
    
    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, ScatterMessage> kafkaTemplate;
    
    public Mono<GatheredResponse> scatterGather(ScatterRequest request) {
        // Scatter phase - broadcast to multiple services
        List<String> targets = discoverServices(request.getServiceType());
        
        List<Mono<ServiceResponse>> scattered = targets.stream()
            .map(target -> scatterToService(target, request))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        // Gather phase - collect and merge responses
        return Flux.merge(scattered)
            .collectList()
            .flatMap(responses -> gatherResponses(responses, request))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
            .onErrorResume(e -> handleGatherTimeout(request));
    }
    
    private Mono<ServiceResponse> scatterToService(String target, ScatterRequest request) {
        return webClient.post()
            .uri("http://" + target + "/api/scatter")
            .bodyValue(request)
            .retrieve()
            .bodyToMono(ServiceResponse.class)
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
            .onErrorResume(e -> Mono.just(new ServiceResponse(target, null, true)));
    }
    
    private Mono<GatheredResponse> gatherResponses(List<ServiceResponse> responses, 
                                                    ScatterRequest request) {
        GatheredResponse gathered = new GatheredResponse();
        
        // Apply different gathering strategies
        switch (request.getStrategy()) {
            case "QUORUM":
                return quorumGather(responses);
            case "BEST":
                return bestResultGather(responses);
            case "MAJORITY":
                return majorityGather(responses);
            case "ALL":
                return allResponsesGather(responses);
            default:
                return firstSuccessGather(responses);
        }
    }
    
    private Mono<GatheredResponse> quorumGather(List<ServiceResponse> responses) {
        int quorum = (int) Math.ceil(responses.size() * 0.75);
        long successCount = responses.stream()
            .filter(r -> !r.isError())
            .count();
        
        if (successCount >= quorum) {
            return Mono.just(mergeSuccessfulResponses(responses));
        } else {
            return Mono.error(new QuorumNotMetException("Quorum not met: " + 
                successCount + "/" + quorum));
        }
    }
    
    private Mono<GatheredResponse> bestResultGather(List<ServiceResponse> responses) {
        return responses.stream()
            .filter(r -> !r.isError())
            .min(Comparator.comparingDouble(r -> r.getScore()))
            .map(Mono::just)
            .orElse(Mono.error(new NoValidResponseException()));
    }
    
    private Mono<GatheredResponse> majorityGather(List<ServiceResponse> responses) {
        Map<Object, Long> results = responses.stream()
            .filter(r -> !r.isError())
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(ServiceResponse::getResult, Collectors.counting()));
        
        return results.entrySet().stream()
            .max(Map.Entry.comparingByValue())
            .map(entry -> Mono.just(new GatheredResponse(entry.getKey())))
            .orElse(Mono.error(new NoConsensusException()));
    }
}
3. Advanced Registry Implementations
3.1 Multi-Data Center Service Registry
java
// Multi-Data Center Service Registry
@Configuration
public class MultiDataCenterRegistry {
    
    @Bean
    public CompositeDiscoveryClient compositeDiscoveryClient() {
        return new CompositeDiscoveryClient(Arrays.asList(
            eurekaDiscoveryClient(),
            consulDiscoveryClient(),
            kubernetesDiscoveryClient()
        ));
    }
    
    private DiscoveryClient eurekaDiscoveryClient() {
        return new EurekaDiscoveryClient(eurekaClient, eurekaClientConfig);
    }
    
    private DiscoveryClient consulDiscoveryClient() {
        return new ConsulDiscoveryClient(consulClient, consulProperties);
    }
    
    private DiscoveryClient kubernetesDiscoveryClient() {
        return new KubernetesDiscoveryClient(kubernetesClient, kubernetesProperties);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ServiceRegistryManager serviceRegistryManager() {
        return new ServiceRegistryManager() {
            @Override
            public Mono<ServiceInstance> getService(String serviceName, 
                                                     RoutingContext context) {
                // Zone-aware routing across data centers
                String preferredZone = context.getZone();
                
                return Flux.fromIterable(discoveryClients)
                    .flatMap(client -> client.getInstances(serviceName))
                    .filter(instance -> matchesZone(instance, preferredZone))
                    .next()
                    .switchIfEmpty(Flux.fromIterable(discoveryClients)
                        .flatMap(client -> client.getInstances(serviceName))
                        .next())
                    .switchIfEmpty(Mono.error(new ServiceNotFoundException(serviceName)));
            }
            
            @Override
            public Mono<List<ServiceInstance>> getAllServices(String serviceName) {
                return Flux.fromIterable(discoveryClients)
                    .flatMap(client -> client.getInstances(serviceName))
                    .collectList();
            }
        };
    }
}

// Cross-Region Replication
@Component
public class CrossRegionRegistryReplicator {
    
    private final EurekaClient eurekaClient;
    private final List<String> peerRegions;
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 30000)
    public void replicateRegistry() {
        Application app = eurekaClient.getApplication("user-service");
        
        peerRegions.forEach(region -> {
            try {
                replicateToRegion(app, region);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Failed to replicate to region: " + region, e);
            }
        });
    }
    
    private void replicateToRegion(Application app, String region) {
        RestTemplate restTemplate = new RestTemplate();
        String peerUrl = String.format("http://eureka-%s:8761/eureka/apps/%s", 
            region, app.getName());
        
        restTemplate.put(peerUrl, app);
        
        meterRegistry.counter("registry.replication",
            "region", region,
            "status", "success")
            .increment();
    }
}
3.2 Service Mesh Integration
java
// Service Mesh Integration with Istio
@Configuration
public class ServiceMeshIntegration {
    
    @Bean
    public IstioServiceRegistry istioServiceRegistry() {
        return new IstioServiceRegistry();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public VirtualServiceConfig virtualServiceConfig() {
        return new VirtualServiceConfig() {
            @Override
            public VirtualService createVirtualService(String serviceName) {
                return VirtualService.builder()
                    .name(serviceName + "-virtual-service")
                    .hosts(Collections.singletonList(serviceName))
                    .http(Collections.singletonList(
                        HTTPRoute.builder()
                            .match(Collections.singletonList(
                                HTTPMatchRequest.builder()
                                    .uri(URIStringMatch.builder()
                                        .prefix("/api/" + serviceName)
                                        .build())
                                    .build()
                            ))
                            .route(Collections.singletonList(
                                HTTPRouteDestination.builder()
                                    .destination(Destination.builder()
                                        .host(serviceName)
                                        .subset("v1")
                                        .build())
                                    .weight(90)
                                    .build(),
                                HTTPRouteDestination.builder()
                                    .destination(Destination.builder()
                                        .host(serviceName)
                                        .subset("v2")
                                        .build())
                                    .weight(10)
                                    .build()
                            ))
                            .retries(HTTPRetry.builder()
                                .attempts(3)
                                .perTryTimeout("2s")
                                .retryOn(Collections.singletonList("5xx"))
                                .build())
                            .build()
                    ))
                    .build();
            }
        };
    }
    
    @Bean
    public DestinationRuleConfig destinationRuleConfig() {
        return new DestinationRuleConfig() {
            @Override
            public DestinationRule createDestinationRule(String serviceName) {
                return DestinationRule.builder()
                    .name(serviceName + "-destination-rule")
                    .host(serviceName)
                    .trafficPolicy(TrafficPolicy.builder()
                        .connectionPool(ConnectionPoolSettings.builder()
                            .tcp(TcpSettings.builder()
                                .maxConnections(100)
                                .connectTimeout("30ms")
                                .build())
                            .http(HttpSettings.builder()
                                .http1MaxPendingRequests(1024)
                                .http2MaxRequests(1024)
                                .maxRequestsPerConnection(10)
                                .build())
                            .build())
                        .loadBalancer(LoadBalancerSettings.builder()
                            .simple("LEAST_CONN")
                            .build())
                        .outlierDetection(OutlierDetection.builder()
                            .consecutive5xxErrors(5)
                            .interval("30s")
                            .baseEjectionTime("30s")
                            .maxEjectionPercent(50)
                            .build())
                        .build())
                    .subsets(Collections.singletonList(
                        Subset.builder()
                            .name("v1")
                            .labels(Collections.singletonMap("version", "v1"))
                            .build()
                    ))
                    .build();
            }
        };
    }
}
4. Aggregator Patterns in Depth
4.1 Dynamic Aggregator with Strategy Pattern
java
// Dynamic Aggregator with Pluggable Strategies
@Component
public class DynamicAggregator {
    
    private final Map<String, AggregationStrategy> strategies;
    private final AggregationStrategy defaultStrategy;
    
    @Autowired
    public DynamicAggregator(List<AggregationStrategy> strategyList) {
        this.strategies = strategyList.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(AggregationStrategy::getName, 
                                      Function.identity()));
        this.defaultStrategy = strategies.get("parallel");
    }
    
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregate(AggregationRequest request) {
        AggregationStrategy strategy = strategies.getOrDefault(
            request.getStrategy(), 
            defaultStrategy
        );
        
        return strategy.aggregate(request)
            .doOnSuccess(result -> recordMetrics(strategy, result))
            .doOnError(error -> handleError(strategy, error));
    }
    
    private void recordMetrics(AggregationStrategy strategy, 
                               AggregatedResult result) {
        meterRegistry.timer("aggregation.duration",
            "strategy", strategy.getName())
            .record(result.getDuration());
        
        meterRegistry.counter("aggregation.requests",
            "strategy", strategy.getName(),
            "status", "success")
            .increment();
    }
}

// Strategy Interface
public interface AggregationStrategy {
    Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregate(AggregationRequest request);
    String getName();
}

// Parallel Strategy
@Component
public class ParallelStrategy implements AggregationStrategy {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregate(AggregationRequest request) {
        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        List<Mono<ComponentResult>> components = request.getComponents().stream()
            .map(this::fetchComponent)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        return Flux.merge(components)
            .collectList()
            .map(results -> {
                AggregatedResult result = new AggregatedResult();
                result.setResults(results);
                result.setDuration(System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime);
                result.setStrategy(getName());
                return result;
            });
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return "parallel";
    }
}

// Sequential Strategy
@Component
public class SequentialStrategy implements AggregationStrategy {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregate(AggregationRequest request) {
        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        return Flux.fromIterable(request.getComponents())
            .concatMap(this::fetchComponent)
            .collectList()
            .map(results -> {
                AggregatedResult result = new AggregatedResult();
                result.setResults(results);
                result.setDuration(System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime);
                result.setStrategy(getName());
                return result;
            });
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return "sequential";
    }
}

// Priority Strategy
@Component
public class PriorityStrategy implements AggregationStrategy {
    
    @Override
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregate(AggregationRequest request) {
        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        // Separate components by priority
        List<ComponentRequest> critical = request.getComponents().stream()
            .filter(c -> c.getPriority() > 5)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        List<ComponentRequest> nonCritical = request.getComponents().stream()
            .filter(c -> c.getPriority() <= 5)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        // Execute critical components first
        return Flux.fromIterable(critical)
            .concatMap(this::fetchComponent)
            .collectList()
            .flatMap(criticalResults -> 
                Flux.fromIterable(nonCritical)
                    .flatMap(this::fetchComponent)
                    .collectList()
                    .map(nonCriticalResults -> {
                        List<ComponentResult> allResults = new ArrayList<>();
                        allResults.addAll(criticalResults);
                        allResults.addAll(nonCriticalResults);
                        
                        AggregatedResult result = new AggregatedResult();
                        result.setResults(allResults);
                        result.setDuration(System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime);
                        result.setStrategy(getName());
                        return result;
                    })
            );
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return "priority";
    }
}
4.2 Caching Aggregator
java
// Intelligent Caching Aggregator
@Component
public class CachingAggregator {
    
    private final ReactiveRedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final CacheMetrics cacheMetrics;
    
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregateWithCache(AggregationRequest request) {
        String cacheKey = generateCacheKey(request);
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(cacheKey)
            .cast(AggregatedResult.class)
            .doOnNext(result -> {
                cacheMetrics.recordHit(request.getStrategy());
                result.setFromCache(true);
            })
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> {
                cacheMetrics.recordMiss(request.getStrategy());
                return performAggregation(request)
                    .flatMap(result -> 
                        cacheResult(cacheKey, result, request.getCacheTtl())
                            .thenReturn(result)
                    );
            }));
    }
    
    private String generateCacheKey(AggregationRequest request) {
        // Smart cache key generation
        StringBuilder key = new StringBuilder("agg:")
            .append(request.getStrategy())
            .append(":");
        
        request.getComponents().stream()
            .sorted(Comparator.comparing(ComponentRequest::getName))
            .forEach(component -> {
                key.append(component.getName())
                   .append(":")
                   .append(component.getVersion())
                   .append(":");
            });
        
        key.append(request.getUserId());
        
        return DigestUtils.md5DigestAsHex(key.toString().getBytes());
    }
    
    private Mono<Boolean> cacheResult(String key, 
                                      AggregatedResult result, 
                                      Duration ttl) {
        // Add cache metadata
        result.setCachedAt(Instant.now());
        result.setCacheTtl(ttl);
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .set(key, result, ttl)
            .doOnSuccess(success -> {
                if (success) {
                    cacheMetrics.recordSet(key, result.getSize());
                }
            });
    }
    
    // Write-through cache for mutable data
    @EventListener
    public void onDataChange(DataChangeEvent event) {
        // Invalidate related cache entries
        String pattern = "agg:*:" + event.getUserId() + ":*";
        
        redisTemplate.keys(pattern)
            .flatMap(redisTemplate::delete)
            .subscribe(count -> {
                cacheMetrics.recordInvalidation(event.getUserId(), count);
                log.info("Invalidated {} cache entries for user: {}", 
                    count, event.getUserId());
            });
    }
}

// Cache Warming Service
@Component
public class CacheWarmer {
    
    private final CachingAggregator aggregator;
    private final UserActivityPredictor predictor;
    
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 */5 * * * *") // Every 5 minutes
    public void warmCache() {
        // Predict which users will be active
        List<String> predictedActiveUsers = predictor.predictActiveUsers();
        
        predictedActiveUsers.parallelStream().forEach(userId -> {
            AggregationRequest request = createRequestForUser(userId);
            
            aggregator.aggregateWithCache(request)
                .subscribe(result -> {
                    log.debug("Warmed cache for user: {}", userId);
                    meterRegistry.counter("cache.warming",
                        "user", userId,
                        "status", "success")
                        .increment();
                }, error -> {
                    log.error("Failed to warm cache for user: " + userId, error);
                    meterRegistry.counter("cache.warming",
                        "user", userId,
                        "status", "failed")
                        .increment();
                });
        });
    }
    
    private AggregationRequest createRequestForUser(String userId) {
        // Create request based on user's typical needs
        UserProfile profile = userService.getUserProfile(userId);
        
        return AggregationRequest.builder()
            .userId(userId)
            .strategy(profile.getPreferredStrategy())
            .cacheTtl(Duration.ofMinutes(5))
            .components(Arrays.asList(
                new ComponentRequest("userProfile", 1),
                new ComponentRequest("userOrders", 2),
                new ComponentRequest("recommendations", 3)
            ))
            .build();
    }
}
5. Hybrid Patterns
5.1 Registry-Aware Aggregator
java
// Registry-Aware Aggregator
@Component
public class RegistryAwareAggregator {
    
    private final ServiceRegistryManager registryManager;
    private final LoadBalancerClient loadBalancer;
    
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregateWithServiceDiscovery(
            AggregationRequest request) {
        
        return Mono.zip(
            getServiceInstances("user-service"),
            getServiceInstances("order-service"),
            getServiceInstances("product-service")
        ).flatMap(tuple -> {
            // Route to best available instances
            ServiceInstance userService = selectBestInstance(tuple.getT1(), request);
            ServiceInstance orderService = selectBestInstance(tuple.getT2(), request);
            ServiceInstance productService = selectBestInstance(tuple.getT3(), request);
            
            return performAggregation(userService, orderService, productService, request);
        });
    }
    
    private Mono<List<ServiceInstance>> getServiceInstances(String serviceName) {
        return registryManager.getServiceInstances(serviceName)
            .filter(this::isHealthy)
            .filter(this::hasCapacity)
            .collectList()
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.error(new ServiceUnavailableException(serviceName)));
    }
    
    private ServiceInstance selectBestInstance(List<ServiceInstance> instances, 
                                               AggregationRequest request) {
        // Apply weighted selection based on various factors
        return instances.stream()
            .max(Comparator.comparingDouble(instance -> 
                calculateScore(instance, request)))
            .orElseThrow();
    }
    
    private double calculateScore(ServiceInstance instance, 
                                  AggregationRequest request) {
        double score = 0.0;
        
        // Zone affinity
        if (instance.getMetadata().get("zone").equals(request.getZone())) {
            score += 0.3;
        }
        
        // Instance health
        score += getHealthScore(instance);
        
        // Instance capacity
        score += getCapacityScore(instance);
        
        // Response time history
        score += getResponseTimeScore(instance);
        
        return score;
    }
    
    private double getHealthScore(ServiceInstance instance) {
        String health = instance.getMetadata().get("health");
        return "UP".equals(health) ? 0.5 : 0.0;
    }
    
    private double getCapacityScore(ServiceInstance instance) {
        String capacity = instance.getMetadata().get("capacity");
        int currentLoad = Integer.parseInt(instance.getMetadata()
            .getOrDefault("currentLoad", "0"));
        int maxCapacity = Integer.parseInt(capacity);
        
        return (maxCapacity - currentLoad) / (double) maxCapacity * 0.2;
    }
}
5.2 Event-Driven Aggregator
java
// Event-Driven Aggregator with Kafka
@Component
public class EventDrivenAggregator {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, AggregationEvent> kafkaTemplate;
    private final ReactiveKafkaConsumerTemplate<String, AggregationEvent> consumer;
    
    public Mono<AggregationResult> aggregateAsync(AggregationRequest request) {
        String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        
        // Create aggregation event
        AggregationEvent event = AggregationEvent.builder()
            .correlationId(correlationId)
            .request(request)
            .timestamp(Instant.now())
            .build();
        
        // Send to Kafka for processing
        return Mono.fromFuture(kafkaTemplate.send("aggregation-requests", 
            correlationId, event))
            .then(waitForResponse(correlationId))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(30));
    }
    
    private Mono<AggregationResult> waitForResponse(String correlationId) {
        return consumer.receive()
            .filter(record -> correlationId.equals(record.value().getCorrelationId()))
            .next()
            .map(record -> record.value().getResult())
            .doOnNext(result -> {
                meterRegistry.timer("event.aggregation.duration",
                    "status", "success")
                    .record(Duration.between(result.getStartTime(), Instant.now()));
            });
    }
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = "aggregation-results")
    public void handleAggregationResult(AggregationResult result) {
        // Store result in cache
        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .set("agg:result:" + result.getCorrelationId(), 
                 result, 
                 Duration.ofMinutes(10))
            .subscribe();
        
        // Trigger any post-processing
        postProcessAggregation(result);
    }
}
6. Performance Optimization
6.1 Connection Management
java
// Optimized Connection Management
@Configuration
public class ConnectionPoolConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public ConnectionProvider connectionProvider() {
        return ConnectionProvider.builder("custom-pool")
            .maxConnections(500)
            .pendingAcquireTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(30))
            .maxIdleTime(Duration.ofSeconds(60))
            .maxLifeTime(Duration.ofMinutes(5))
            .evictInBackground(Duration.ofSeconds(120))
            .metrics(true)
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public HttpClient httpClient(ConnectionProvider connectionProvider) {
        return HttpClient.create(connectionProvider)
            .option(ChannelOption.CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MILLIS, 5000)
            .option(ChannelOption.SO_KEEPALIVE, true)
            .option(ChannelOption.TCP_NODELAY, true)
            .doOnConnected(connection -> 
                connection.addHandlerLast(new ReadTimeoutHandler(30))
                    .addHandlerLast(new WriteTimeoutHandler(30))
            )
            .compress(true)
            .wiretap("reactor.netty.http.client", LogLevel.DEBUG);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public WebClient webClient(HttpClient httpClient) {
        return WebClient.builder()
            .clientConnector(new ReactorClientHttpConnector(httpClient))
            .defaultHeader(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_TYPE, MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)
            .defaultHeader(HttpHeaders.ACCEPT, MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)
            .codecs(configurer -> {
                configurer.defaultCodecs().maxInMemorySize(10 * 1024 * 1024);
                configurer.defaultCodecs().enableLoggingRequestDetails(true);
            })
            .build();
    }
}
6.2 Circuit Breaker Integration
java
// Circuit Breaker for Aggregator
@Component
public class ResilientAggregator {
    
    private final Map<String, CircuitBreaker> circuitBreakers;
    
    @Autowired
    public ResilientAggregator(ServiceRegistry registry) {
        this.circuitBreakers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        
        // Initialize circuit breakers for each service
        registry.getServices().forEach(service -> {
            CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
                .failureRateThreshold(50)
                .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(60))
                .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(10)
                .slidingWindowSize(100)
                .recordExceptions(TimeoutException.class, 
                                 ServiceUnavailableException.class)
                .build();
            
            circuitBreakers.put(service, CircuitBreaker.of(service, config));
        });
    }
    
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregateWithResilience(AggregationRequest request) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(request.getComponents())
            .flatMap(component -> 
                callServiceWithBreaker(component)
                    .onErrorResume(e -> getFallback(component, e))
            )
            .collectList()
            .map(this::combineResults);
    }
    
    private Mono<ComponentResult> callServiceWithBreaker(Component component) {
        CircuitBreaker breaker = circuitBreakers.get(component.getServiceName());
        
        return Mono.defer(() -> callService(component))
            .transformDeferred(CircuitBreakerOperator.of(breaker))
            .doOnError(e -> recordBreakerMetrics(component, e));
    }
    
    private Mono<ComponentResult> getFallback(Component component, Throwable error) {
        if (error instanceof CircuitBreakerOpenException) {
            meterRegistry.counter("circuitbreaker.open",
                "service", component.getServiceName())
                .increment();
        }
        
        // Return cached or default response
        return getCachedResponse(component)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.just(ComponentResult.defaultResult(component)));
    }
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 10000)
    public void monitorCircuitBreakers() {
        circuitBreakers.forEach((service, breaker) -> {
            meterRegistry.gauge("circuitbreaker.state",
                breaker.getState().ordinal(),
                "service", service);
            
            meterRegistry.gauge("circuitbreaker.failure.rate",
                breaker.getMetrics().getFailureRate(),
                "service", service);
        });
    }
}
7. Resilience & Fault Tolerance
7.1 Retry Mechanisms
java
// Advanced Retry Mechanism
@Component
public class RetryableAggregator {
    
    private final RetryTemplate retryTemplate;
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        this.retryTemplate = RetryTemplate.builder()
            .maxAttempts(3)
            .exponentialBackoff(1000, 2, 10000)
            .retryOn(TimeoutException.class)
            .retryOn(ServiceUnavailableException.class)
            .traversingCauses()
            .build();
    }
    
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregateWithRetry(AggregationRequest request) {
        return Mono.defer(() -> performAggregation(request))
            .retryWhen(Retry.backoff(3, Duration.ofSeconds(1))
                .filter(this::isRetryableError)
                .onRetryExhaustedThrow((spec, signal) -> {
                    log.error("Retry exhausted for request: {}", request);
                    meterRegistry.counter("retry.exhausted",
                        "request", request.getType())
                        .increment();
                    return new AggregationException("Max retries exceeded");
                })
                .doBeforeRetry(retrySignal -> {
                    log.info("Retry attempt {} for request: {}", 
                        retrySignal.totalRetries(), request);
                    meterRegistry.counter("retry.attempt",
                        "request", request.getType())
                        .increment();
                })
            );
    }
    
    private boolean isRetryableError(Throwable error) {
        return error instanceof TimeoutException ||
               error instanceof ServiceUnavailableException ||
               (error instanceof WebClientResponseException && 
                ((WebClientResponseException) error).getStatusCode().is5xxServerError());
    }
    
    // Idempotent retry with idempotency key
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> idempotentRetry(AggregationRequest request) {
        String idempotencyKey = request.getIdempotencyKey();
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .get("idempotency:" + idempotencyKey)
            .cast(AggregatedResult.class)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> 
                performAggregation(request)
                    .flatMap(result -> 
                        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                            .set("idempotency:" + idempotencyKey, 
                                 result, 
                                 Duration.ofHours(24))
                            .thenReturn(result)
                    )
            ));
    }
}
7.2 Bulkhead Pattern
java
// Bulkhead Pattern Implementation
@Component
public class BulkheadAggregator {
    
    private final Map<String, Semaphore> bulkheads;
    private final Map<String, Queue<AggregationRequest>> pendingRequests;
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        // Initialize bulkheads for different service types
        bulkheads.put("critical", new Semaphore(10));
        bulkheads.put("default", new Semaphore(20));
        bulkheads.put("background", new Semaphore(5));
        
        pendingRequests.put("critical", new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>());
        pendingRequests.put("default", new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>());
        pendingRequests.put("background", new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>());
        
        // Start processing pending requests
        startPendingRequestProcessor();
    }
    
    public Mono<AggregatedResult> aggregateWithBulkhead(AggregationRequest request) {
        String priority = request.getPriority();
        Semaphore semaphore = bulkheads.get(priority);
        
        if (semaphore.tryAcquire()) {
            return performAggregation(request)
                .doFinally(signalType -> semaphore.release())
                .onErrorResume(e -> {
                    semaphore.release();
                    return Mono.error(e);
                });
        } else {
            // Queue request for later processing
            return queueRequest(request, priority);
        }
    }
    
    private Mono<AggregatedResult> queueRequest(AggregationRequest request, 
                                                  String priority) {
        pendingRequests.get(priority).add(request);
        
        meterRegistry.counter("bulkhead.queued",
            "priority", priority)
            .increment();
        
        return Mono.defer(() -> {
            if (pendingRequests.get(priority).contains(request)) {
                return Mono.error(new BulkheadException("Request queued, timeout waiting"));
            }
            return performAggregation(request);
        }).timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(30));
    }
    
    private void startPendingRequestProcessor() {
        ScheduledExecutorService executor = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(3);
        
        executor.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
            Arrays.asList("critical", "default", "background").forEach(priority -> {
                Semaphore semaphore = bulkheads.get(priority);
                Queue<AggregationRequest> queue = pendingRequests.get(priority);
                
                while (semaphore.availablePermits() > 0 && !queue.isEmpty()) {
                    AggregationRequest request = queue.poll();
                    if (request != null && semaphore.tryAcquire()) {
                        performAggregation(request)
                            .subscribe(
                                result -> {
                                    semaphore.release();
                                    meterRegistry.counter("bulkhead.processed",
                                        "priority", priority)
                                        .increment();
                                },
                                error -> {
                                    semaphore.release();
                                    meterRegistry.counter("bulkhead.failed",
                                        "priority", priority)
                                        .increment();
                                }
                            );
                    }
                }
            });
        }, 1, 1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
}
8. Real-world Implementations
8.1 E-commerce Order Aggregator
java
// Complete E-commerce Order Aggregator
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/ecommerce")
public class EcommerceAggregator {
    
    private final OrderAggregationService orderService;
    private final InventoryService inventoryService;
    private final PaymentService paymentService;
    private final ShippingService shippingService;
    
    @PostMapping("/orders")
    public Mono<OrderAggregation> createOrder(@RequestBody OrderRequest request) {
        return orderService.validateOrder(request)
            .flatMap(validatedOrder -> 
                Mono.zip(
                    inventoryService.reserveInventory(validatedOrder),
                    paymentService.processPayment(validatedOrder),
                    shippingService.calculateShipping(validatedOrder)
                )
            )
            .flatMap(tuple -> {
                OrderAggregation aggregation = new OrderAggregation();
                aggregation.setInventoryReservation(tuple.getT1());
                aggregation.setPaymentConfirmation(tuple.getT2());
                aggregation.setShippingOptions(tuple.getT3());
                
                return orderService.createOrder(aggregation);
            })
            .flatMap(order -> 
                // Parallel processing of non-critical tasks
                Flux.merge(
                    sendOrderConfirmation(order).subscribeOn(Schedulers.parallel()),
                    updateInventory(order).subscribeOn(Schedulers.parallel()),
                    notifyWarehouse(order).subscribeOn(Schedulers.parallel())
                ).collectList()
                .thenReturn(order)
            );
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/orders/{orderId}/status")
    public Mono<OrderStatusAggregation> getOrderStatus(@PathVariable String orderId) {
        return Mono.zip(
            orderService.getOrder(orderId),
            shippingService.getTrackingInfo(orderId),
            paymentService.getPaymentStatus(orderId),
            inventoryService.getFulfillmentStatus(orderId)
        ).map(tuple -> {
            OrderStatusAggregation status = new OrderStatusAggregation();
            status.setOrder(tuple.getT1());
            status.setTracking(tuple.getT2());
            status.setPayment(tuple.getT3());
            status.setFulfillment(tuple.getT4());
            
            // Calculate estimated delivery
            status.setEstimatedDelivery(calculateDeliveryDate(status));
            
            // Add customer support info
            status.setSupportInfo(getSupportInfo(status));
            
            return status;
        });
    }
}
8.2 Financial Services Aggregator
java
// Financial Services Aggregator with Transaction Management
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/financial")
public class FinancialAggregator {
    
    private final TransactionManager transactionManager;
    private final AccountService accountService;
    private final FraudDetectionService fraudService;
    private final ComplianceService complianceService;
    
    @PostMapping("/transactions")
    @Transactional
    public Mono<TransactionAggregation> processTransaction(
            @RequestBody TransactionRequest request) {
        
        return transactionManager.beginTransaction()
            .flatMap(tx -> 
                Mono.zip(
                    accountService.validateAccounts(request),
                    fraudService.analyzeTransaction(request),
                    complianceService.checkCompliance(request)
                )
                .flatMap(tuple -> {
                    if (tuple.getT2().isSuspicious() || !tuple.getT3().isCompliant()) {
                        return transactionManager.rollback(tx)
                            .then(Mono.error(new TransactionException("Validation failed")));
                    }
                    
                    return accountService.executeTransfer(request)
                        .flatMap(result -> {
                            TransactionAggregation aggregation = new TransactionAggregation();
                            aggregation.setTransactionId(result.getId());
                            aggregation.setStatus("COMPLETED");
                            aggregation.setFraudScore(tuple.getT2().getScore());
                            aggregation.setComplianceId(tuple.getT3().getId());
                            
                            return transactionManager.commit(tx)
                                .thenReturn(aggregation);
                        });
                })
            )
            .doOnError(error -> log.error("Transaction failed", error))
            .onErrorResume(error -> 
                transactionManager.rollback()
                    .then(Mono.just(createFailedTransaction(error)))
            );
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/portfolio/{userId}")
    public Mono<PortfolioAggregation> getPortfolio(@PathVariable String userId) {
        return Mono.zip(
            accountService.getAccounts(userId),
            investmentService.getHoldings(userId),
            marketDataService.getCurrentPrices(),
            riskService.calculateRisk(userId)
        ).map(tuple -> {
            PortfolioAggregation portfolio = new PortfolioAggregation();
            portfolio.setAccounts(tuple.getT1());
            portfolio.setHoldings(tuple.getT2());
            portfolio.setMarketData(tuple.getT3());
            portfolio.setRiskProfile(tuple.getT4());
            
            // Calculate portfolio metrics
            portfolio.setTotalValue(calculateTotalValue(portfolio));
            portfolio.setDailyPnL(calculateDailyPnL(portfolio));
            portfolio.setAssetAllocation(calculateAssetAllocation(portfolio));
            
            return portfolio;
        });
    }
}
8.3 Social Media Feed Aggregator
java
// Social Media Feed Aggregator with Personalization
@Component
public class SocialFeedAggregator {
    
    private final ContentService contentService;
    private final UserGraphService graphService;
    private final RecommendationEngine recommendationEngine;
    private final AdService adService;
    
    public Flux<FeedItem> getPersonalizedFeed(String userId, 
                                              FeedContext context) {
        // Fetch user's social graph
        return graphService.getUserNetwork(userId)
            .flatMapMany(network -> 
                // Fetch content from network
                contentService.getContentFromNetwork(network, context)
            )
            .mergeWith(
                // Add sponsored content
                adService.getTargetedAds(userId, context)
                    .map(ad -> new FeedItem(ad, "sponsored"))
            )
            .mergeWith(
                // Add recommendations
                recommendationEngine.getRecommendations(userId, context)
                    .map(rec -> new FeedItem(rec, "recommended"))
            )
            .transform(feed -> 
                // Apply ranking algorithm
                rankFeedItems(feed, userId)
            )
            .transform(feed -> 
                // Apply personalization filters
                personalizeFeed(feed, userId)
            )
            .transform(feed -> 
                // Apply deduplication
                deduplicateFeed(feed)
            )
            .transform(feed -> 
                // Apply caching
                cacheFeedResults(feed, userId)
            );
    }
    
    private Flux<FeedItem> rankFeedItems(Flux<FeedItem> feed, String userId) {
        return feed.buffer(100) // Batch for ranking
            .flatMap(batch -> {
                // Apply ML-based ranking model
                return rankingModel.rank(batch, userId)
                    .flatMapMany(Flux::fromIterable);
            });
    }
    
    private Flux<FeedItem> personalizeFeed(Flux<FeedItem> feed, String userId) {
        return feed.map(item -> {
            // Apply personalization based on user preferences
            double personalizationScore = calculatePersonalizationScore(item, userId);
            item.setPersonalizationScore(personalizationScore);
            
            // Apply content filtering
            if (!userPreferencesService.shouldShow(userId, item)) {
                return null;
            }
            
            return item;
        })
        .filter(Objects::nonNull);
    }
    
    // Real-time feed updates with WebSocket
    @MessageMapping("/feed/{userId}")
    public Flux<FeedItem> streamRealTimeFeed(@DestinationVariable String userId) {
        return Flux.merge(
            // Live activities from friends
            activityService.getLiveActivities(userId),
            // Breaking news
            newsService.getBreakingNews(),
            // Real-time notifications
            notificationService.getRealtimeNotifications(userId)
        )
        .share() // Share the stream for multiple subscribers
        .publishOn(Schedulers.parallel());
    }
}
Best Practices Summary
Service Registry Best Practices
Health Checks: Implement detailed health checks with custom metrics

Zone Awareness: Use zone/region metadata for optimal routing

Self-Preservation: Configure appropriate renewal thresholds

Cache Strategy: Implement client-side caching with invalidation

Security: Secure registry endpoints with authentication

Service Aggregator Best Practices
Timeouts: Always set appropriate timeouts for downstream calls

Circuit Breakers: Implement circuit breakers for fault tolerance

Caching: Use multi-level caching (Redis + local) for frequent aggregations

Bulkheads: Isolate different service types to prevent cascading failures

Retries: Implement intelligent retry with exponential backoff

Performance Optimization
Parallel Execution: Use parallel fetching where dependencies allow

Connection Pooling: Optimize HTTP client connection pools

Response Compression: Compress responses for network efficiency

Data Filtering: Filter unnecessary fields early

Async Processing: Use non-blocking I/O throughout

Monitoring & Observability
Distributed Tracing: Trace requests across service boundaries

Metrics Collection: Track aggregation times, error rates, cache hits

Health Indicators: Provide detailed health information

Audit Logging: Log critical aggregation operations

Performance Baselines: Establish and monitor performance baselines

This comprehensive implementation provides production-ready patterns for service registry and aggregation in microservices architectures, with a focus on scalability, resilience, and maintainability.

1.2 Message Broker Core Concepts
java
// Core Message Model
public class Message<T> {
    private String id;
    private String correlationId;
    private String messageType;
    private T payload;
    private Map<String, String> headers;
    private Instant timestamp;
    private String source;
    private String destination;
    private int priority;
    private long ttl;
    private DeliveryMode deliveryMode;
    
    // Builder pattern
    public static <T> MessageBuilder<T> builder() {
        return new MessageBuilder<>();
    }
    
    public static class MessageBuilder<T> {
        private Message<T> message = new Message<>();
        
        public MessageBuilder<T> id(String id) {
            message.id = id;
            return this;
        }
        
        public MessageBuilder<T> correlationId(String correlationId) {
            message.correlationId = correlationId;
            return this;
        }
        
        public MessageBuilder<T> payload(T payload) {
            message.payload = payload;
            return this;
        }
        
        public MessageBuilder<T> header(String key, String value) {
            if (message.headers == null) {
                message.headers = new HashMap<>();
            }
            message.headers.put(key, value);
            return this;
        }
        
        public Message<T> build() {
            message.timestamp = Instant.now();
            if (message.id == null) {
                message.id = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
            }
            return message;
        }
    }
}

// Message Delivery Guarantees
public enum DeliveryMode {
    AT_MOST_ONCE,    // Fire-and-forget, fastest, potential loss
    AT_LEAST_ONCE,   // Guaranteed delivery, potential duplicates
    EXACTLY_ONCE     // Guaranteed exactly-once, highest overhead
}

// Message Acknowledgment Strategies
public enum AcknowledgmentMode {
    AUTO,           // Automatic acknowledgment
    MANUAL,         // Manual acknowledgment
    CLIENT,         // Client-controlled
    TRANSACTIONAL   // Transactional acknowledgment
}
2. Broker Selection & Comparison
2.1 Broker Comparison Matrix
java
@Component
public class BrokerSelector {
    
    private final Map<String, BrokerCapabilities> brokers;
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        brokers = Map.of(
            "kafka", BrokerCapabilities.builder()
                .messageOrdering(true)
                .messageRetention(true)
                .streamProcessing(true)
                .maxThroughput("1M+ msgs/sec")
                .latency("~10ms")
                .persistence("Disk")
                .useCase("Event Streaming, Log Aggregation")
                .build(),
                
            "rabbitmq", BrokerCapabilities.builder()
                .messageOrdering(false)
                .messageRetention(false)
                .streamProcessing(false)
                .maxThroughput("~50K msgs/sec")
                .latency("~1ms")
                .persistence("Memory/Disk")
                .useCase("RPC, Task Queues, Work Distribution")
                .build(),
                
            "activemq", BrokerCapabilities.builder()
                .messageOrdering(true)
                .messageRetention(true)
                .streamProcessing(false)
                .maxThroughput("~30K msgs/sec")
                .latency("~2ms")
                .persistence("Disk")
                .useCase("JMS, Legacy Integration")
                .build(),
                
            "pulsar", BrokerCapabilities.builder()
                .messageOrdering(true)
                .messageRetention(true)
                .streamProcessing(true)
                .maxThroughput("~800K msgs/sec")
                .latency("~5ms")
                .persistence("BookKeeper")
                .useCase("Multi-tenant, Geo-replication")
                .build()
        );
    }
    
    public BrokerRecommendation recommend(WorkloadCharacteristics workload) {
        List<BrokerScore> scores = brokers.entrySet().stream()
            .map(entry -> new BrokerScore(
                entry.getKey(),
                calculateScore(entry.getValue(), workload)
            ))
            .sorted((a, b) -> Double.compare(b.getScore(), a.getScore()))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        return BrokerRecommendation.builder()
            .primary(scores.get(0).getBroker())
            .alternatives(scores.subList(1, Math.min(3, scores.size())))
            .rationale(generateRationale(scores))
            .build();
    }
    
    private double calculateScore(BrokerCapabilities capabilities, 
                                  WorkloadCharacteristics workload) {
        double score = 0.0;
        
        if (workload.isHighThroughput() && capabilities.getMaxThroughput() > 100000) {
            score += 30;
        }
        
        if (workload.requiresMessageOrdering() && capabilities.isMessageOrdering()) {
            score += 25;
        }
        
        if (workload.getLatencyRequirement() < 10 && 
            capabilities.getLatency() < 10) {
            score += 20;
        }
        
        if (workload.requiresStreamProcessing() && 
            capabilities.isStreamProcessing()) {
            score += 15;
        }
        
        return score;
    }
}

        durable: true
        arguments:
          x-max-priority: 10
      - name: notification.queue
        durable: true
        arguments:
          x-dead-letter-exchange: dlx
          x-message-ttl: 3600000
