        type: topic
        durable: true
      - name: user.exchange
        type: direct
        durable: true
3. Apache Kafka Deep Dive
3.1 Kafka Cluster Configuration
java
@Configuration
@EnableKafka
public class KafkaConfig {
    
    @Value("${kafka.bootstrap-servers}")
    private String bootstrapServers;
    
    // Producer Configuration
    @Bean
    public ProducerFactory<String, Object> producerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrapServers);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, 
            StringSerializer.class);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, 
            JsonSerializer.class);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.ACKS_CONFIG, "all");
        config.put(ProducerConfig.RETRIES_CONFIG, 3);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BATCH_SIZE_CONFIG, 16384);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.LINGER_MS_CONFIG, 5);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.COMPRESSION_TYPE_CONFIG, "snappy");
        config.put(ProducerConfig.MAX_IN_FLIGHT_REQUESTS_PER_CONNECTION, 5);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.ENABLE_IDEMPOTENCE_CONFIG, true);
        
        return new DefaultKafkaProducerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    // Consumer Configuration with Custom Error Handling
    @Bean
    public ConsumerFactory<String, Object> consumerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrapServers);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.GROUP_ID_CONFIG, "microservices-group");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, 
            StringDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, 
            JsonDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.AUTO_OFFSET_RESET_CONFIG, "earliest");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.ENABLE_AUTO_COMMIT_CONFIG, false);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.MAX_POLL_RECORDS_CONFIG, 500);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.MAX_POLL_INTERVAL_MS_CONFIG, 300000);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS_CONFIG, 30000);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS_CONFIG, 3000);
        config.put(JsonDeserializer.TRUSTED_PACKAGES, "*");
        
        return new DefaultKafkaConsumerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<String, Object> 
            kafkaListenerContainerFactory() {
        ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<String, Object> factory = 
            new ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<>();
        factory.setConsumerFactory(consumerFactory());
        factory.setConcurrency(3);
        factory.setBatchListener(true);
        factory.getContainerProperties().setAckMode(ContainerProperties.AckMode.MANUAL);
        factory.getContainerProperties().setPollTimeout(5000);
        factory.getContainerProperties().setSyncCommits(false);
        
        // Custom error handler
        factory.setCommonErrorHandler(new DefaultErrorHandler(
            new FixedBackOff(1000L, 3)
        ));
        
        return factory;
    }
    
    // Kafka Admin Configuration
    @Bean
    public KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(AdminClientConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrapServers);
        return new KafkaAdmin(config);
    }
    
    // Topic Creation
    @Bean
    public NewTopic userEventsTopic() {
        return TopicBuilder.name("user-events")
            .partitions(6)
            .replicas(3)
            .config(TopicConfig.RETENTION_MS_CONFIG, "604800000") // 7 days
            .config(TopicConfig.COMPRESSION_TYPE_CONFIG, "snappy")
            .config(TopicConfig.CLEANUP_POLICY_CONFIG, "delete")
            .config(TopicConfig.MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES_CONFIG, "10485760") // 10MB
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public NewTopic orderEventsTopic() {
        return TopicBuilder.name("order-events")
            .partitions(12)
            .replicas(3)
            .config(TopicConfig.RETENTION_MS_CONFIG, "2592000000") // 30 days
            .config(TopicConfig.CLEANUP_POLICY_CONFIG, "compact,delete")
            .config(TopicConfig.MIN_COMPACTION_LAG_MS_CONFIG, "86400000") // 1 day
            .build();
    }
}
3.2 Kafka Producer with Advanced Features
java
@Service
@Slf4j
public class KafkaEventProducer {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    private final KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin;
    
    @Autowired
    public KafkaEventProducer(KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate,
                              MeterRegistry meterRegistry,
                              KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin) {
        this.kafkaTemplate = kafkaTemplate;
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        this.kafkaAdmin = kafkaAdmin;
    }
    
    // Exactly-once semantics producer
    public Mono<SendResult<String, Object>> sendWithExactlyOnce(
            String topic, 
            String key, 
            Object message) {
        
        ProducerRecord<String, Object> record = new ProducerRecord<>(
            topic, 
            key, 
            message
        );
        
        // Add headers for tracing
        record.headers().add("X-Correlation-Id", 
            UUID.randomUUID().toString().getBytes());
        record.headers().add("X-Timestamp", 
            String.valueOf(System.currentTimeMillis()).getBytes());
        
        return Mono.fromFuture(kafkaTemplate.send(record))
            .doOnSuccess(result -> {
                meterRegistry.counter("kafka.producer.success",
                    "topic", topic).increment();
                
                log.debug("Message sent successfully: topic={}, partition={}, offset={}",
                    topic, result.getRecordMetadata().partition(),
                    result.getRecordMetadata().offset());
            })
            .doOnError(error -> {
                meterRegistry.counter("kafka.producer.error",
                    "topic", topic,
                    "error", error.getClass().getSimpleName()).increment();
                
                log.error("Failed to send message to topic: {}", topic, error);
            });
    }
    
    // Transactional producer
    @Transactional
    public Mono<Void> sendTransactionalBatch(String topic, 
                                             List<Message<?>> messages) {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            kafkaTemplate.executeInTransaction(operations -> {
                messages.forEach(message -> {
                    operations.send(topic, 
                        message.getCorrelationId(), 
                        message.getPayload());
                });
                return true;
            });
        });
    }
    
    // Async producer with callback
    public CompletableFuture<RecordMetadata> sendAsync(String topic, 
                                                        Object message) {
        CompletableFuture<RecordMetadata> future = new CompletableFuture<>();
        
        kafkaTemplate.send(topic, message)
            .whenComplete((result, ex) -> {
                if (ex != null) {
                    future.completeExceptionally(ex);
                } else {
                    future.complete(result.getRecordMetadata());
                }
            });
        
        return future;
    }
    
    // Batch producer with dynamic batching
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 1000)
    public void flushBatchMessages() {
        batchQueue.drainTo().forEach(batch -> {
            List<ProducerRecord<String, Object>> records = batch.getRecords();
            
            kafkaTemplate.executeInTransaction(operations -> {
                records.forEach(operations::send);
                return true;
            });
        });
    }
}
3.3 Kafka Consumer with Advanced Processing
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class KafkaEventConsumer {
    
    private final Map<String, MessageProcessor> processors;
    private final DeadLetterQueueService dlqService;
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = "user-events", 
                   groupId = "user-service-group",
                   containerFactory = "kafkaListenerContainerFactory")
    public void consumeUserEvents(List<ConsumerRecord<String, Object>> records,
                                   Acknowledgment acknowledgment) {
        
        records.forEach(record -> {
            try {
                String eventType = extractEventType(record);
                MessageProcessor processor = processors.get(eventType);
                
                if (processor != null) {
                    processor.process(record.value());
                } else {
                    log.warn("No processor found for event type: {}", eventType);
                }
                
                meterRegistry.counter("kafka.consumer.processed",
                    "topic", record.topic(),
                    "partition", String.valueOf(record.partition()))
                    .increment();
                    
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Error processing message: {}", record.value(), e);
                meterRegistry.counter("kafka.consumer.errors",
                    "topic", record.topic(),
                    "error", e.getClass().getSimpleName())
                    .increment();
                
                // Send to dead letter queue
                dlqService.sendToDLQ(record, e);
            }
        });
        
        // Manual acknowledgment after batch processing
        acknowledgment.acknowledge();
    }
    
    // Consumer with seek and replay capability
    @KafkaListener(topics = "order-events", 
                   groupId = "order-service-group")
    public void consumeWithReplay(ConsumerRecord<String, Object> record,
                                   Consumer<?, ?> consumer) {
        try {
            processOrderEvent(record.value());
        } catch (ReplayableException e) {
            // Seek to replay from last successful offset
            consumer.seek(new TopicPartition(record.topic(), record.partition()),
                record.offset() - 1);
        }
    }
    
    // Stateful consumer with Kafka Streams
    @Bean
    public KStream<String, OrderEvent> orderEventStream() {
        StreamsBuilder builder = new StreamsBuilder();
        
        KStream<String, OrderEvent> orderStream = builder
            .stream("order-events",
                Consumed.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(OrderEvent.class)))
            .peek((key, value) -> log.info("Processing order: {}", key))
            .filter((key, value) -> value.isValid())
            .mapValues(this::enrichOrderData)
            .groupByKey()
            .windowedBy(TimeWindows.of(Duration.ofMinutes(5)))
            .aggregate(
                () -> new OrderAggregation(),
                (key, value, aggregate) -> aggregate.add(value),
                Materialized.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(OrderAggregation.class))
            )
            .toStream()
            .map((windowedKey, value) -> KeyValue.pair(windowedKey.key(), value));
        
        // Branch stream based on order type
        orderStream.branch(
            (key, value) -> value.getAmount() > 10000,
            (key, value) -> value.isInternational(),
            (key, value) -> true
        );
        
        orderStream.to("processed-orders", 
            Produced.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(OrderAggregation.class)));
        
        return orderStream;
    }
}
4. RabbitMQ Advanced Patterns
4.1 RabbitMQ Configuration
java
@Configuration
public class RabbitMQConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public ConnectionFactory connectionFactory() {
        CachingConnectionFactory factory = new CachingConnectionFactory();
        factory.setAddresses("rabbitmq-1:5672,rabbitmq-2:5672,rabbitmq-3:5672");
        factory.setUsername(System.getenv("RABBITMQ_USER"));
        factory.setPassword(System.getenv("RABBITMQ_PASSWORD"));
        factory.setVirtualHost("/microservices");
        factory.setChannelCacheSize(25);
        factory.setConnectionTimeout(5000);
        factory.setRequestedHeartBeat(30);
        factory.setPublisherConfirmType(CachingConnectionFactory.ConfirmType.CORRELATED);
        factory.setPublisherReturns(true);
        
        return factory;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate(ConnectionFactory connectionFactory) {
        RabbitTemplate template = new RabbitTemplate(connectionFactory);
        template.setMessageConverter(new Jackson2JsonMessageConverter());
        template.setMandatory(true);
        template.setConfirmCallback((correlationData, ack, cause) -> {
            if (!ack) {
                log.error("Message not confirmed: {}", cause);
                meterRegistry.counter("rabbitmq.publish.confirm.failed").increment();
            } else {
                meterRegistry.counter("rabbitmq.publish.confirm.success").increment();
            }
        });
        template.setReturnsCallback(returned -> {
            log.error("Message returned: {}", returned);
            meterRegistry.counter("rabbitmq.publish.returned").increment();
        });
        
        return template;
    }
    
    // Advanced Queue Declarations
    @Bean
    public Queue mainQueue() {
        return QueueBuilder.durable("main.queue")
            .withArgument("x-max-priority", 10)
            .withArgument("x-message-ttl", 3600000) // 1 hour
            .withArgument("x-dead-letter-exchange", "dlx.exchange")
            .withArgument("x-dead-letter-routing-key", "dead-letter")
            .withArgument("x-max-length", 10000)
            .withArgument("x-overflow", "reject-publish")
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public Queue delayedQueue() {
        return QueueBuilder.durable("delayed.queue")
            .withArgument("x-dead-letter-exchange", "main.exchange")
            .withArgument("x-dead-letter-routing-key", "main")
            .withArgument("x-message-ttl", 5000) // 5 second delay
            .build();
    }
    
    // Topic Exchange for Routing
    @Bean
    public TopicExchange orderExchange() {
        return ExchangeBuilder.topicExchange("order.exchange")
            .durable(true)
            .alternate("order.alt.exchange")
            .build();
    }
    
    // Direct Exchange for RPC
    @Bean
    public DirectExchange rpcExchange() {
        return ExchangeBuilder.directExchange("rpc.exchange")
            .durable(true)
            .build();
    }
    
    // Fanout Exchange for Broadcasting
    @Bean
    public FanoutExchange notificationExchange() {
        return ExchangeBuilder.fanoutExchange("notification.exchange")
            .durable(true)
            .build();
    }
    
    // Bindings
    @Bean
    public Binding orderCreatedBinding() {
        return BindingBuilder.bind(mainQueue())
            .to(orderExchange())
            .with("order.created");
    }
    
    @Bean
    public Binding orderUpdatedBinding() {
        return BindingBuilder.bind(mainQueue())
            .to(orderExchange())
            .with("order.updated");
    }
    
    @Bean
    public Binding orderCancelledBinding() {
        return BindingBuilder.bind(mainQueue())
            .to(orderExchange())
            .with("order.cancelled");
    }
}
4.2 RabbitMQ Producer with Advanced Features
java
@Service
public class RabbitMQProducer {
    
    private final RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate;
    private final RetryTemplate retryTemplate;
    
    @Autowired
    public RabbitMQProducer(RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate) {
        this.rabbitTemplate = rabbitTemplate;
        
        this.retryTemplate = RetryTemplate.builder()
            .maxAttempts(3)
            .exponentialBackoff(1000, 2, 10000)
            .retryOn(AmqpException.class)
            .build();
    }
    
    // RPC Pattern with Correlation ID
    public Mono<Object> sendAndReceive(String exchange, 
                                        String routingKey, 
                                        Object message) {
        String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        
        MessageProperties props = new MessageProperties();
        props.setCorrelationId(correlationId.getBytes());
        props.setReplyTo("rpc.reply.queue");
        props.setExpiration("60000"); // 60 second timeout
        
        Message amqpMessage = MessageBuilder
            .withBody(JsonUtils.toJson(message).getBytes())
            .andProperties(props)
            .build();
        
        return Mono.fromFuture(
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> 
                rabbitTemplate.sendAndReceive(exchange, routingKey, amqpMessage)
            )
        ).map(response -> {
            if (response != null) {
                return JsonUtils.parse(response.getBody());
            }
            throw new RpcTimeoutException("RPC call timed out");
        });
    }
    
    // Delayed Message with TTL
    public void sendDelayedMessage(String exchange, 
                                    String routingKey, 
                                    Object message,
                                    long delayMillis) {
        
        MessageProperties props = new MessageProperties();
        props.setExpiration(String.valueOf(delayMillis));
        props.setHeader("x-delay", delayMillis);
        
        Message amqpMessage = MessageBuilder
            .withBody(JsonUtils.toJson(message).getBytes())
            .andProperties(props)
            .build();
        
        rabbitTemplate.convertAndSend(exchange, routingKey, amqpMessage);
    }
    
    // Transactional Message Publishing
    @Transactional
    public void sendTransactionalMessage(String exchange, 
                                          String routingKey, 
                                          Object message) {
        rabbitTemplate.convertAndSend(exchange, routingKey, message);
        
        // Additional database operations
        // The message will only be sent when the transaction commits
    }
    
    // Batch Publishing
    public void sendBatch(String exchange, 
                          String routingKey, 
                          List<Object> messages) {
        rabbitTemplate.invoke(operations -> {
            messages.forEach(message -> 
                operations.convertAndSend(exchange, routingKey, message)
            );
            return null;
        });
    }
}
4.3 RabbitMQ Consumer with Advanced Error Handling
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class RabbitMQConsumer {
    
    private final Map<String, MessageHandler> handlers;
    private final DeadLetterHandler deadLetterHandler;
    
    @RabbitListener(queues = "main.queue", 
                    concurrency = "5-10",
                    containerFactory = "rabbitListenerContainerFactory")
    public void handleMessage(Message message, 
                              Channel channel,
                              @Header(AmqpHeaders.DELIVERY_TAG) long tag) {
        
        try {
            String messageType = (String) message.getMessageProperties()
                .getHeaders().get("type");
            
            MessageHandler handler = handlers.get(messageType);
            if (handler != null) {
                handler.handle(message.getBody());
                channel.basicAck(tag, false);
                
                meterRegistry.counter("rabbitmq.consumer.processed",
                    "type", messageType).increment();
            } else {
                log.warn("No handler for message type: {}", messageType);
                channel.basicNack(tag, false, false); // Reject, don't requeue
            }
            
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Error processing message", e);
            
            // Check if we should retry
            if (shouldRetry(message)) {
                try {
                    channel.basicNack(tag, false, true); // Requeue
                    meterRegistry.counter("rabbitmq.consumer.retry").increment();
                } catch (IOException ioException) {
                    log.error("Failed to requeue message", ioException);
                }
            } else {
                // Send to dead letter queue
                deadLetterHandler.sendToDLQ(message, e);
                try {
                    channel.basicAck(tag, false); // Ack original message
                } catch (IOException ioException) {
                    log.error("Failed to ack message after DLQ", ioException);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Custom Message Converter
    @Bean
    public MessageConverter messageConverter() {
        Jackson2JsonMessageConverter converter = new Jackson2JsonMessageConverter();
        converter.setTypePrecedence(Jackson2JsonMessageConverter.TypePrecedence.TYPE_ID);
        converter.setClassMapper(classMapper());
        return converter;
    }
    
    private ClassMapper classMapper() {
        DefaultClassMapper mapper = new DefaultClassMapper();
        Map<String, Class<?>> idMapping = Map.of(
            "user.created", UserCreatedEvent.class,
            "order.created", OrderCreatedEvent.class,
            "product.updated", ProductUpdatedEvent.class
        );
        mapper.setIdClassMapping(idMapping);
        return mapper;
    }
    
    // Dead Letter Queue Processor
    @RabbitListener(queues = "dlq.queue")
    public void processDeadLetter(Message message) {
        // Inspect and potentially recover dead letter messages
        Map<String, Object> headers = message.getMessageProperties().getHeaders();
        String reason = (String) headers.get("x-exception-stacktrace");
        
        if (isRecoverable(message)) {
            // Re-publish to main queue
            rabbitTemplate.send("main.exchange", "main", message);
        } else {
            // Store for manual intervention
            storeInDeadLetterStorage(message);
        }
    }
}
5. Hybrid Broker Architectures
5.1 Kafka + RabbitMQ Hybrid Pattern
java
@Component
public class HybridBrokerOrchestrator {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    private final RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate;
    
    /**
     * Use Kafka for event sourcing and stream processing
     * Use RabbitMQ for RPC and immediate processing
     */
    public Mono<OrderProcessingResult> processOrder(Order order) {
        // 1. Send order event to Kafka for event sourcing
        kafkaTemplate.send("order-events", "order.created", order)
            .whenComplete((result, ex) -> {
                if (ex != null) {
                    log.error("Failed to send to Kafka", ex);
                }
            });
        
        // 2. Send to RabbitMQ for immediate processing
        return Mono.fromFuture(
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> 
                rabbitTemplate.convertSendAndReceive("order.exchange", 
                    "order.process", order)
            )
        ).map(response -> (OrderProcessingResult) response);
    }
    
    // Bridge between Kafka and RabbitMQ
    @KafkaListener(topics = "order-events")
    public void bridgeToRabbitMQ(ConsumerRecord<String, Order> record) {
        Order order = record.value();
        
        // Convert Kafka event to RabbitMQ message
        rabbitTemplate.convertAndSend("order.bridge.exchange", 
            "order.processed", order);
        
        meterRegistry.counter("bridge.kafka-to-rabbitmq",
            "status", "success").increment();
    }
    
    @RabbitListener(queues = "order.bridge.queue")
    public void bridgeToKafka(Order order) {
        // Send RabbitMQ message to Kafka
        kafkaTemplate.send("external-events", 
            "order.received", order);
        
        meterRegistry.counter("bridge.rabbitmq-to-kafka",
            "status", "success").increment();
    }
}
5.2 Multi-Region Broker Architecture
java
@Component
public class MultiRegionBrokerManager {
    
    private final Map<String, KafkaTemplate<String, Object>> regionProducers;
    private final Map<String, RabbitTemplate> regionRabbitTemplates;
    private final GeoReplicationService replicationService;
    
    @Autowired
    public MultiRegionBrokerManager(KafkaProperties kafkaProperties,
                                     RabbitProperties rabbitProperties) {
        this.regionProducers = initializeKafkaProducers(kafkaProperties);
        this.regionRabbitTemplates = initializeRabbitTemplates(rabbitProperties);
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> publishToRegion(String region, 
                                       String topic, 
                                       Object message) {
        KafkaTemplate<String, Object> producer = regionProducers.get(region);
        if (producer == null) {
            return Mono.error(new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid region: " + region));
        }
        
        return Mono.fromFuture(producer.send(topic, message))
            .then()
            .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                // Replicate to other regions asynchronously
                replicateToOtherRegions(region, topic, message);
            });
    }
    
    private void replicateToOtherRegions(String sourceRegion, 
                                         String topic, 
                                         Object message) {
        regionProducers.entrySet().stream()
            .filter(entry -> !entry.getKey().equals(sourceRegion))
            .forEach(entry -> {
                entry.getValue().send(topic, message)
                    .whenComplete((result, ex) -> {
                        if (ex != null) {
                            log.error("Failed to replicate to region: {}", 
                                entry.getKey(), ex);
                            meterRegistry.counter("replication.failed",
                                "source", sourceRegion,
                                "target", entry.getKey()).increment();
                        } else {
                            meterRegistry.counter("replication.success",
                                "source", sourceRegion,
                                "target", entry.getKey()).increment();
                        }
                    });
            });
    }
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 30000)
    public void syncRegionMetadata() {
        // Sync consumer group offsets and topic metadata across regions
        regionProducers.forEach((region, producer) -> {
            replicationService.syncMetadata(region, producer);
        });
    }
}
6. Message Patterns & Protocols
6.1 Request-Reply Pattern
java
@Component
public class RequestReplyPattern {
    
    private final RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate;
    private final Map<String, CompletableFuture<Object>> pendingRequests;
    
    @Autowired
    public RequestReplyPattern(RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate) {
        this.rabbitTemplate = rabbitTemplate;
        this.pendingRequests = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        
        // Setup reply listener
        rabbitTemplate.setReplyConsumer((message, correlationData) -> {
            String correlationId = message.getMessageProperties().getCorrelationId();
            CompletableFuture<Object> future = pendingRequests.remove(correlationId);
            if (future != null) {
                future.complete(message.getBody());
            }
        });
    }
    
    public CompletableFuture<Object> sendRequest(String exchange, 
                                                  String routingKey, 
                                                  Object request) {
        String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        CompletableFuture<Object> future = new CompletableFuture<>();
        pendingRequests.put(correlationId, future);
        
        MessageProperties props = new MessageProperties();
        props.setCorrelationId(correlationId);
        props.setReplyTo("reply.queue");
        
        Message message = MessageBuilder
            .withBody(JsonUtils.toJson(request).getBytes())
            .andProperties(props)
            .build();
        
        rabbitTemplate.send(exchange, routingKey, message);
        
        return future.orTimeout(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .exceptionally(throwable -> {
                pendingRequests.remove(correlationId);
                throw new RuntimeException("Request timeout", throwable);
            });
    }
    
    @RabbitListener(queues = "reply.queue")
    public void handleReply(Message reply) {
        String correlationId = reply.getMessageProperties().getCorrelationId();
        CompletableFuture<Object> future = pendingRequests.remove(correlationId);
        if (future != null) {
            future.complete(reply.getBody());
        }
    }
}
6.2 Publish-Subscribe Pattern
java
@Component
public class PublishSubscribePattern {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    private final Map<String, Set<Subscriber>> subscribers;
    
    @Autowired
    public PublishSubscribePattern(KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate) {
        this.kafkaTemplate = kafkaTemplate;
        this.subscribers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    }
    
    // Kafka-based pub-sub
    public Mono<Void> publishEvent(String topic, Object event) {
        return Mono.fromFuture(kafkaTemplate.send(topic, event))
            .then()
            .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                meterRegistry.counter("pubsub.published",
                    "topic", topic).increment();
            });
    }
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = "#{__listener.topics}")
    public void consumeEvent(ConsumerRecord<String, Object> record) {
        String topic = record.topic();
        Set<Subscriber> topicSubscribers = subscribers.get(topic);
        
        if (topicSubscribers != null) {
            topicSubscribers.forEach(subscriber -> {
                subscriber.onEvent(record.value());
            });
        }
        
        meterRegistry.counter("pubsub.consumed",
            "topic", topic).increment();
    }
    
    // WebSocket-based real-time pub-sub
    @MessageMapping("/topic/{topic}")
    public void handleSubscription(@DestinationVariable String topic,
                                    Principal principal) {
        // Handle WebSocket subscription
    }
    
    public void subscribe(String topic, Subscriber subscriber) {
        subscribers.computeIfAbsent(topic, k -> ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet())
            .add(subscriber);
    }
    
    public void unsubscribe(String topic, Subscriber subscriber) {
        Set<Subscriber> topicSubscribers = subscribers.get(topic);
        if (topicSubscribers != null) {
            topicSubscribers.remove(subscriber);
            if (topicSubscribers.isEmpty()) {
                subscribers.remove(topic);
            }
        }
    }
}
6.3 Event Sourcing Pattern
java
@Component
public class EventSourcingPattern {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Event> eventStore;
    private final Map<String, List<EventHandler>> eventHandlers;
    private final MongoTemplate eventRepository;
    
    @Autowired
    public EventSourcingPattern(KafkaTemplate<String, Event> eventStore,
                                MongoTemplate eventRepository) {
        this.eventStore = eventStore;
        this.eventRepository = eventRepository;
        this.eventHandlers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    }
    
    // Store event in event store
    public Mono<Void> storeEvent(String aggregateId, Event event) {
        event.setAggregateId(aggregateId);
        event.setSequence(getNextSequence(aggregateId));
        event.setTimestamp(Instant.now());
        
        // Store in Kafka for event sourcing
        return Mono.fromFuture(eventStore.send("event-store", aggregateId, event))
            .then()
            .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                // Also store in MongoDB for querying
                eventRepository.save(event);
                
                // Notify event handlers
                notifyHandlers(aggregateId, event);
                
                meterRegistry.counter("eventsourcing.stored",
                    "aggregate", aggregateId).increment();
            });
    }
    
    // Rebuild aggregate from events
    public Mono<Aggregate> rebuildAggregate(String aggregateId, 
                                            Class<? extends Aggregate> aggregateClass) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(eventRepository.find(
            Query.query(Criteria.where("aggregateId").is(aggregateId)),
            Event.class
        ))
        .sort(Comparator.comparing(Event::getSequence))
        .reduce(createNewAggregate(aggregateClass), (aggregate, event) -> {
            aggregate.applyEvent(event);
            return aggregate;
        });
    }
    
    // Snapshot for performance
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 */6 * * *") // Every 6 hours
    public void createSnapshots() {
        // Find aggregates with many events
        List<String> largeAggregates = getLargeAggregates();
        
        largeAggregates.forEach(aggregateId -> {
            rebuildAggregate(aggregateId, Aggregate.class)
                .subscribe(aggregate -> {
                    // Store snapshot
                    Snapshot snapshot = Snapshot.builder()
                        .aggregateId(aggregateId)
                        .state(aggregate)
                        .eventSequence(aggregate.getVersion())
                        .timestamp(Instant.now())
                        .build();
                    
                    eventRepository.save(snapshot);
                    
                    // Delete old events
                    deleteOldEvents(aggregateId, aggregate.getVersion());
                });
        });
    }
    
    // Event replay for debugging/testing
    public Flux<Event> replayEvents(String aggregateId, 
                                    long fromSequence, 
                                    long toSequence) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(eventRepository.find(
            Query.query(Criteria.where("aggregateId").is(aggregateId)
                .and("sequence").gte(fromSequence).lte(toSequence)),
            Event.class
        ));
    }
}
7. Reliability & Delivery Guarantees
7.1 Exactly-Once Processing
java
@Component
public class ExactlyOnceProcessor {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;
    
    // Exactly-once with idempotent consumer
    @KafkaListener(topics = "order-events", 
                   groupId = "order-processor",
                   containerFactory = "exactlyOnceContainerFactory")
    public void processExactlyOnce(ConsumerRecord<String, Order> record,
                                    Acknowledgment acknowledgment) {
        
        String idempotencyKey = record.value().getId();
        String processedKey = "processed:" + idempotencyKey;
        
        // Check if already processed
        Boolean processed = redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .setIfAbsent(processedKey, "processing", Duration.ofMinutes(5));
        
        if (Boolean.FALSE.equals(processed)) {
            log.info("Message already processing: {}", idempotencyKey);
            return;
        }
        
        try {
            // Process message with database transaction
            transactionTemplate.execute(status -> {
                try {
                    // Business logic
                    processOrder(record.value());
                    
                    // Store processing record
                    storeProcessedRecord(record);
                    
                    return true;
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    status.setRollbackOnly();
                    throw e;
                }
            });
            
            // Mark as processed
            redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                .set(processedKey, "completed", Duration.ofDays(7));
            
            acknowledgment.acknowledge();
            
            meterRegistry.counter("exactly-once.processed",
                "status", "success").increment();
                
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Processing failed", e);
            redisTemplate.delete(processedKey);
            meterRegistry.counter("exactly-once.processed",
                "status", "failed").increment();
            throw e;
        }
    }
    
    // Idempotent producer
    public Mono<SendResult<String, Object>> sendIdempotent(String topic, 
                                                           String key, 
                                                           Object message) {
        String idempotencyKey = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        
        ProducerRecord<String, Object> record = new ProducerRecord<>(
            topic, key, message
        );
        
        record.headers().add("idempotency-key", idempotencyKey.getBytes());
        
        return Mono.fromFuture(kafkaTemplate.send(record))
            .doOnSuccess(result -> {
                // Store idempotency record
                redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                    .set("idempotent:" + idempotencyKey, 
                         "sent", 
                         Duration.ofDays(7));
            });
    }
    
    // Transactional outbox pattern
    @Transactional
    public void createOrderWithEvent(Order order) {
        // Save order to database
        orderRepository.save(order);
        
        // Store event in outbox table
        OutboxEvent outbox = OutboxEvent.builder()
            .aggregateId(order.getId())
            .eventType("ORDER_CREATED")
            .payload(JsonUtils.toJson(order))
            .status("PENDING")
            .build();
        
        outboxRepository.save(outbox);
    }
    
    // Outbox publisher
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 1000)
    public void publishOutboxEvents() {
        List<OutboxEvent> pendingEvents = outboxRepository.findByStatus("PENDING");
        
        pendingEvents.forEach(event -> {
            try {
                // Publish to message broker
                kafkaTemplate.send("order-events", event.getPayload())
                    .whenComplete((result, ex) -> {
                        if (ex != null) {
                            log.error("Failed to publish event", ex);
                        } else {
                            event.setStatus("PUBLISHED");
                            outboxRepository.save(event);
                        }
                    });
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Error publishing outbox event", e);
            }
        });
    }
}
7.2 Dead Letter Queue Management
java
@Component
public class DeadLetterManager {
    
    private final RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate;
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    private final MongoTemplate dlqStorage;
    
    @RabbitListener(queues = "dlq.main")
    public void processDeadLetter(Message message) {
        Map<String, Object> headers = message.getMessageProperties().getHeaders();
        String originalQueue = (String) headers.get("x-original-queue");
        String reason = (String) headers.get("x-exception-stacktrace");
        int retryCount = (Integer) headers.getOrDefault("x-retry-count", 0);
        
        if (retryCount < 3) {
            // Retry with exponential backoff
            long delay = calculateBackoff(retryCount);
            scheduleRetry(message, originalQueue, delay);
        } else {
            // Store in dead letter storage
            DeadLetterRecord dlqRecord = DeadLetterRecord.builder()
                .message(message)
                .originalQueue(originalQueue)
                .reason(reason)
                .retryCount(retryCount)
                .timestamp(Instant.now())
                .build();
            
            dlqStorage.save(dlqRecord);
            
            // Send alert
            sendDeadLetterAlert(dlqRecord);
        }
    }
    
    // Dead letter replay
    @PostMapping("/admin/dlq/replay")
    public Mono<Void> replayDeadLetters(@RequestBody ReplayRequest request) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(dlqStorage.find(
            Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").in(request.getMessageIds())),
            DeadLetterRecord.class
        ))
        .flatMap(record -> {
            Message message = record.getMessage();
            String originalQueue = record.getOriginalQueue();
            
            return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> 
                rabbitTemplate.send(originalQueue, message)
            );
        })
        .then();
    }
    
    // Dead letter analytics
    @GetMapping("/admin/dlq/analytics")
    public Mono<DeadLetterAnalytics> getDeadLetterAnalytics() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            List<DeadLetterRecord> records = dlqStorage.findAll(DeadLetterRecord.class);
            
            return DeadLetterAnalytics.builder()
                .totalCount(records.size())
                .byQueue(records.stream()
                    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                        DeadLetterRecord::getOriginalQueue, 
                        Collectors.counting()
                    )))
                .byError(records.stream()
                    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                        DeadLetterRecord::getErrorType,
                        Collectors.counting()
                    )))
                .trends(calculateTrends(records))
                .build();
        });
    }
}
8. Performance Optimization
8.1 Producer Optimization
java
@Component
public class OptimizedProducer {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    private final List<ProducerInterceptor> interceptors;
    
    @Bean
    public ProducerFactory<String, Object> optimizedProducerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        
        // Batch optimization
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BATCH_SIZE_CONFIG, 32768); // 32KB
        config.put(ProducerConfig.LINGER_MS_CONFIG, 10);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BUFFER_MEMORY_CONFIG, 67108864); // 64MB
        
        // Compression
        config.put(ProducerConfig.COMPRESSION_TYPE_CONFIG, "zstd");
        config.put(ProducerConfig.COMPRESSION_LEVEL_CONFIG, 3);
        
        // Throughput optimization
        config.put(ProducerConfig.MAX_REQUEST_SIZE_CONFIG, 10485760); // 10MB
        config.put(ProducerConfig.SEND_BUFFER_CONFIG, 131072); // 128KB
        config.put(ProducerConfig.RECEIVE_BUFFER_CONFIG, 131072);
        
        // Idempotent producer
        config.put(ProducerConfig.ENABLE_IDEMPOTENCE_CONFIG, true);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.MAX_IN_FLIGHT_REQUESTS_PER_CONNECTION, 5);
        
        // Retry configuration
        config.put(ProducerConfig.RETRIES_CONFIG, Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.DELIVERY_TIMEOUT_MS_CONFIG, 120000);
        
        return new DefaultKafkaProducerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    // Async producer with backpressure
    public Flux<SendResult<String, Object>> sendAsyncWithBackpressure(
            Flux<ProducerRecord<String, Object>> records) {
        
        return records
            .window(Duration.ofMillis(100))
            .flatMap(window -> window
                .buffer(1000)
                .flatMap(batch -> {
                    List<CompletableFuture<SendResult<String, Object>>> futures = 
                        batch.stream()
                            .map(record -> kafkaTemplate.send(record).toFuture())
                            .collect(Collectors.toList());
                    
                    return Flux.fromIterable(futures)
                        .flatMap(future -> Mono.fromFuture(future));
                })
                .subscribeOn(Schedulers.parallel())
            );
    }
    
    // Custom partitioner for better distribution
    @Bean
    public Partitioner customPartitioner() {
        return new Partitioner() {
            @Override
            public int partition(String topic, Object key, byte[] keyBytes,
                                 Object value, byte[] valueBytes, Cluster cluster) {
                List<PartitionInfo> partitions = cluster.partitionsForTopic(topic);
                int numPartitions = partitions.size();
                
                // Custom logic based on message type
                if (value instanceof Order) {
                    Order order = (Order) value;
                    return Math.abs(order.getUserId().hashCode()) % numPartitions;
                } else {
                    return Math.abs(key.hashCode()) % numPartitions;
                }
            }
            
            @Override
            public void close() {}
            
            @Override
            public void configure(Map<String, ?> configs) {}
        };
    }
}

@Component

    public ConsumerFactory<String, Object> optimizedConsumerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();

        config.put(ConsumerConfig.FETCH_MIN_BYTES_CONFIG, 1024);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.FETCH_MAX_BYTES_CONFIG, 52428800); // 50MB
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.FETCH_MAX_WAIT_MS_CONFIG, 500);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.MAX_PARTITION_FETCH_BYTES_CONFIG, 1048576); // 1MB
        
        // Consumer performance
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.MAX_POLL_RECORDS_CONFIG, 1000);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.MAX_POLL_INTERVAL_MS_CONFIG, 300000);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS_CONFIG, 45000);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS_CONFIG, 3000);
        
        // Auto offset reset
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.AUTO_OFFSET_RESET_CONFIG, "earliest");
        
        // Enable auto commit with low interval
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.ENABLE_AUTO_COMMIT_CONFIG, false);
        
        return new DefaultKafkaConsumerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    // Parallel consumer with partitioning
    @KafkaListener(topics = "high-throughput-topic", 
                   concurrency = "6",
                   containerFactory = "optimizedContainerFactory")
    public void parallelConsume(List<ConsumerRecord<String, Object>> records,
                                 Acknowledgment acknowledgment) {
        
        Flux.fromIterable(records)
            .parallel()
            .runOn(Schedulers.parallel())
            .subscribe(record -> {
                processRecord(record);
            });
        
        acknowledgment.acknowledge();
    }
    
    // Consumer with dynamic partition assignment
    @KafkaListener(topicPartitions = @TopicPartition(topic = "dynamic-topic",
                       partitionOffsets = @PartitionOffset(partition = "*", 
                       initialOffset = "0")))
    public void dynamicPartitionConsumer(ConsumerRecord<String, Object> record) {
        // Handle record
    }
    
    // Rate-limited consumer
    @KafkaListener(topics = "rate-limited-topic")
    public void rateLimitedConsume(ConsumerRecord<String, Object> record) {
        rateLimiter.acquire(); // Throttle processing
        
        processRecord(record);
    }
}
9. Security & Compliance
9.1 Message Encryption
java
@Component
public class MessageEncryption {
    
    private final KeyStore keyStore;
    private final Cipher encryptCipher;
    private final Cipher decryptCipher;
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() throws Exception {
        // Load encryption keys
        Key key = loadKeyFromKeyStore();
        
        encryptCipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding");
        encryptCipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key);
        
        decryptCipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding");
        decryptCipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, key);
    }
    
    public byte[] encryptMessage(byte[] plaintext) {
        try {
            byte[] iv = encryptCipher.getIV();
            byte[] ciphertext = encryptCipher.doFinal(plaintext);
            
            // Combine IV and ciphertext
            ByteBuffer byteBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(iv.length + ciphertext.length);
            byteBuffer.put(iv);
            byteBuffer.put(ciphertext);
            
            return byteBuffer.array();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new EncryptionException("Failed to encrypt message", e);
        }
    }
    
    public byte[] decryptMessage(byte[] encrypted) {
        try {
            // Extract IV and ciphertext
            ByteBuffer byteBuffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(encrypted);
            byte[] iv = new byte[12];
            byteBuffer.get(iv);
            byte[] ciphertext = new byte[byteBuffer.remaining()];
            byteBuffer.get(ciphertext);
            
            GCMParameterSpec spec = new GCMParameterSpec(128, iv);
            decryptCipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, decryptCipher.getKey(), spec);
            
            return decryptCipher.doFinal(ciphertext);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new DecryptionException("Failed to decrypt message", e);
        }
    }
    
    // Kafka producer interceptor for encryption
    public static class EncryptionInterceptor 
            implements ProducerInterceptor<String, Object> {
        
        private final MessageEncryption encryption;
        
        @Override
        public ProducerRecord<String, Object> onSend(ProducerRecord<String, Object> record) {
            if (record.value() != null && shouldEncrypt(record.topic())) {
                byte[] encrypted = encryption.encryptMessage(
                    JsonUtils.toJson(record.value()).getBytes()
                );
                return new ProducerRecord<>(
                    record.topic(),
                    record.key(),
                    encrypted
                );
            }
            return record;
        }
        
        @Override
        public void onAcknowledgement(RecordMetadata metadata, Exception exception) {}
        
        @Override
        public void close() {}
        
        @Override
        public void configure(Map<String, ?> configs) {}
    }
}
9.2 Authentication & Authorization
java
@Configuration
public class MessageSecurityConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public KafkaSecurityManager kafkaSecurityManager() {
        return new KafkaSecurityManager() {
            @Override
            public void configure(KafkaConsumer<String, Object> consumer) {
                // Configure SSL
                consumer.setProperty("security.protocol", "SSL");
                consumer.setProperty("ssl.truststore.location", "/path/to/truststore.jks");
                consumer.setProperty("ssl.truststore.password", System.getenv("TRUSTSTORE_PWD"));
                consumer.setProperty("ssl.keystore.location", "/path/to/keystore.jks");
                consumer.setProperty("ssl.keystore.password", System.getenv("KEYSTORE_PWD"));
                consumer.setProperty("ssl.key.password", System.getenv("KEY_PWD"));
                
                // SASL authentication
                consumer.setProperty("sasl.mechanism", "PLAIN");
                consumer.setProperty("sasl.jaas.config", 
                    "org.apache.kafka.common.security.plain.PlainLoginModule required " +
                    "username=\"" + System.getenv("KAFKA_USER") + "\" " +
                    "password=\"" + System.getenv("KAFKA_PASSWORD") + "\";");
            }
        };
    }
    
    @Bean
    public AccessControlList aclManager() {
        return new AccessControlList() {
            @Override
            public boolean canProduce(String topic, Principal principal) {
                // Check if user can produce to topic
                return checkPermission(principal, "produce", topic);
            }
            
            @Override
            public boolean canConsume(String topic, String groupId, Principal principal) {
                // Check if user can consume from topic
                return checkPermission(principal, "consume", topic);
            }
            
            private boolean checkPermission(Principal principal, String action, String resource) {
                // Implement permission checking logic
                return true;
            }
        };
    }
}
10. Monitoring & Operations
10.1 Comprehensive Monitoring
java
@Component
public class BrokerMonitoring {
    
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    private final KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin;
    private final RabbitAdmin rabbitAdmin;
    
    @Autowired
    public BrokerMonitoring(MeterRegistry meterRegistry,
                            KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin,
                            RabbitAdmin rabbitAdmin) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        this.kafkaAdmin = kafkaAdmin;
        this.rabbitAdmin = rabbitAdmin;
        
        initializeMetrics();
        startMonitoringScheduler();
    }
    
    private void initializeMetrics() {
        // Kafka metrics
        meterRegistry.gauge("kafka.brokers.count", 
            kafkaAdmin.getBrokerCount());
        
        meterRegistry.gauge("kafka.topics.count",
            kafkaAdmin.getTopicCount());
        
        // RabbitMQ metrics
        meterRegistry.gauge("rabbitmq.queues.count",
            rabbitAdmin.getQueueCount());
        
        meterRegistry.gauge("rabbitmq.connections.count",
            rabbitAdmin.getConnectionCount());
    }
    
    private void startMonitoringScheduler() {
        ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
        
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
            // Monitor Kafka consumer lag
            monitorConsumerLag();
            
            // Monitor RabbitMQ queue depth
            monitorQueueDepth();
            
            // Monitor message throughput
            monitorThroughput();
            
        }, 0, 30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    
    private void monitorConsumerLag() {
        Map<String, Map<Integer, Long>> consumerLag = getConsumerLag();
        
        consumerLag.forEach((groupId, partitions) -> {
            partitions.forEach((partition, lag) -> {
                meterRegistry.gauge("kafka.consumer.lag",
                    lag,
                    "group", groupId,
                    "partition", String.valueOf(partition));
                
                if (lag > 10000) {
                    alertHighLag(groupId, partition, lag);
                }
            });
        });
    }
    
    private void monitorQueueDepth() {
        rabbitAdmin.getQueueNames().forEach(queue -> {
            long depth = rabbitAdmin.getQueueDepth(queue);
            
            meterRegistry.gauge("rabbitmq.queue.depth",
                depth,
                "queue", queue);
            
            if (depth > 5000) {
                alertHighQueueDepth(queue, depth);
            }
        });
    }
    
    private void monitorThroughput() {
        // Record message rates
        meterRegistry.meter("broker.message.rate",
            "broker", "kafka",
            "type", "producer");
        
        meterRegistry.meter("broker.message.rate",
            "broker", "kafka",
            "type", "consumer");
        
        meterRegistry.meter("broker.message.rate",
            "broker", "rabbitmq",
            "type", "publish");
        
        meterRegistry.meter("broker.message.rate",
            "broker", "rabbitmq",
            "type", "consume");
    }
    
    private void alertHighLag(String groupId, int partition, long lag) {
        // Send alert to monitoring system
        alertService.sendAlert(Alert.builder()
            .severity(Severity.WARNING)
            .title("High consumer lag detected")
            .description(String.format("Group: %s, Partition: %d, Lag: %d", 
                groupId, partition, lag))
            .build());
    }
}
10.2 Health Checks
java
@Component
public class BrokerHealthIndicator implements ReactiveHealthIndicator {
    
    private final KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin;
    private final RabbitAdmin rabbitAdmin;
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Health> health() {
        return checkKafkaHealth()
            .zipWith(checkRabbitMQHealth())
            .map(tuple -> Health.builder()
                .withDetail("kafka", tuple.getT1())
                .withDetail("rabbitmq", tuple.getT2())
                .status(tuple.getT1().isUp() && tuple.getT2().isUp() ? 
                    Status.UP : Status.DOWN)
                .build());
    }
    
    private Mono<Health> checkKafkaHealth() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            try {
                // Check if we can list topics
                ListTopicsResult topics = kafkaAdmin.listTopics();
                topics.listings().get();
                
                return Health.up()
                    .withDetail("brokers", kafkaAdmin.getBrokerCount())
                    .withDetail("topics", kafkaAdmin.getTopicCount())
                    .build();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return Health.down()
                    .withException(e)
                    .build();
            }
        }).timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
          .onErrorResume(e -> Mono.just(Health.down().withException(e).build()));
    }
    
    private Mono<Health> checkRabbitMQHealth() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            try {
                // Check if we can connect to RabbitMQ
                rabbitAdmin.getQueueNames();
                
                return Health.up()
                    .withDetail("queues", rabbitAdmin.getQueueCount())
                    .withDetail("connections", rabbitAdmin.getConnectionCount())
                    .build();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return Health.down()
                    .withException(e)
                    .build();
            }
        }).timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
          .onErrorResume(e -> Mono.just(Health.down().withException(e).build()));
    }
}
10.3 Operational Dashboard
java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin/broker")
public class BrokerOperationsController {
    
    private final KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin;
    private final RabbitAdmin rabbitAdmin;
    private final BrokerMonitoring monitoring;
    
    @GetMapping("/topics")
    public Mono<Map<String, TopicInfo>> getTopics() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            Map<String, TopicInfo> topics = new HashMap<>();
            
            kafkaAdmin.getTopics().forEach(topic -> {
                topics.put(topic, TopicInfo.builder()
                    .name(topic)
                    .partitions(kafkaAdmin.getPartitionCount(topic))
                    .replicationFactor(kafkaAdmin.getReplicationFactor(topic))
                    .messageCount(kafkaAdmin.getMessageCount(topic))
                    .build());
            });
            
            return topics;
        });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/queues")
    public Mono<List<QueueInfo>> getQueues() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            List<QueueInfo> queues = new ArrayList<>();
            
            rabbitAdmin.getQueueNames().forEach(queue -> {
                queues.add(QueueInfo.builder()
                    .name(queue)
                    .depth(rabbitAdmin.getQueueDepth(queue))
                    .consumers(rabbitAdmin.getConsumerCount(queue))
                    .unacknowledged(rabbitAdmin.getUnackedCount(queue))
                    .build());
            });
            
            return queues;
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/topics/{topic}/rebalance")
    public Mono<Void> rebalanceTopic(@PathVariable String topic) {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            // Trigger partition rebalance
            kafkaAdmin.rebalance(topic);
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/queues/{queue}/purge")
    public Mono<Void> purgeQueue(@PathVariable String queue) {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            rabbitAdmin.purgeQueue(queue);
        });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/metrics")
    public Mono<BrokerMetrics> getMetrics() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> BrokerMetrics.builder()
            .kafkaMetrics(monitoring.getKafkaMetrics())
            .rabbitMQMetrics(monitoring.getRabbitMQMetrics())
            .throughput(monitoring.getThroughputMetrics())
            .errors(monitoring.getErrorMetrics())
            .build());
    }
}
Best Practices Summary
Architecture Decisions
Choose the right broker: Kafka for event streaming, RabbitMQ for RPC/task queues

Implement hybrid approach: Use multiple brokers for different use cases

Design for failure: Implement dead letter queues, retries, and circuit breakers

Ensure exactly-once processing: Use idempotent consumers and transactional producers

Performance Optimization
Batching: Configure appropriate batch sizes and linger times

Compression: Use compression (snappy, zstd) for large messages

Parallelism: Increase consumer concurrency for high throughput

Connection pooling: Reuse connections and channels

Reliability Patterns
Retry with backoff: Implement exponential backoff for failures

Dead letter queues: Handle failed messages gracefully

Idempotent processing: Ensure messages can be processed multiple times

Transaction boundaries: Maintain consistency across databases and messages

Monitoring & Operations
Track consumer lag: Monitor and alert on increasing lag

Queue depth monitoring: Prevent queue buildup

End-to-end tracing: Trace messages across producers and consumers

Capacity planning: Monitor throughput and plan for scaling

Security Considerations
Encryption at rest: Encrypt messages stored in brokers

Encryption in transit: Use SSL/TLS for all communication

Authentication: Implement SASL/PLAIN or certificate-based auth

Authorization: Use ACLs to control topic/queue access

This comprehensive guide provides production-ready message broker implementations for microservices architectures, covering all aspects from basic configuration to advanced patterns, security, and operations.

1.2 Cassandra Configuration
java
@Configuration
public class CassandraConfig {
    
    @Value("${cassandra.contact-points}")
    private String contactPoints;
    
    @Value("${cassandra.port}")
    private int port;
    
    @Value("${cassandra.keyspace}")
    private String keyspace;
    
    @Bean
    public CqlSession session() {
        return CqlSession.builder()
            .addContactPoints(parseContactPoints(contactPoints))
            .withPort(port)
            .withLocalDatacenter("datacenter1")
            .withKeyspace(keyspace)
            .withAuthCredentials(
                System.getenv("CASSANDRA_USER"),
                System.getenv("CASSANDRA_PASSWORD")
            )
            .withSslContext(createSSLContext())
            .withQueryLogger(QueryLogger.builder()
                .build())
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate(CqlSession session) {
        return new CassandraTemplate(session);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public CassandraConverter cassandraConverter() {
        MappingCassandraConverter converter = new MappingCassandraConverter();
        converter.setCodecRegistry(DefaultCodecRegistry.INSTANCE);
        return converter;
    }
    
    private List<InetSocketAddress> parseContactPoints(String contactPoints) {
        return Arrays.stream(contactPoints.split(","))
            .map(host -> new InetSocketAddress(host, port))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }
    
    private SSLContext createSSLContext() {
        try {
            TrustManagerFactory tmf = TrustManagerFactory.getInstance(
                TrustManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm());
            KeyStore trustStore = KeyStore.getInstance("JKS");
            trustStore.load(new FileInputStream("/path/to/truststore.jks"),
                System.getenv("TRUSTSTORE_PASSWORD").toCharArray());
            tmf.init(trustStore);
            
            SSLContext sslContext = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS");
            sslContext.init(null, tmf.getTrustManagers(), null);
            return sslContext;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Failed to create SSL context", e);
        }
    }
}
1.3 Keyspace & Table Design
sql
-- Keyspace creation with network topology strategy
CREATE KEYSPACE IF NOT EXISTS microservices 
WITH replication = {
    'class': 'NetworkTopologyStrategy',
    'datacenter1': 3,
    'datacenter2': 3
} AND durable_writes = true;

-- User table with composite partition key
CREATE TABLE users (
    user_id UUID,
    email TEXT,
    username TEXT,
    full_name TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP,
    is_active BOOLEAN,
    metadata MAP<TEXT, TEXT>,
    PRIMARY KEY ((user_id), created_at)
) WITH CLUSTERING ORDER BY (created_at DESC)
    AND bloom_filter_fp_chance = 0.01
    AND caching = {'keys': 'ALL', 'rows_per_partition': '100'}
    AND comment = 'Users table with time-based clustering'
    AND compaction = {'class': 'SizeTieredCompactionStrategy', 
                      'bucket_high': '1.5', 
                      'bucket_low': '0.5'};

-- Orders table with composite partition key for sharding
CREATE TABLE orders (
    user_id UUID,
    order_id UUID,
    order_date DATE,
    total_amount DECIMAL,
    status TEXT,
    items LIST<FROZEN<OrderItem>>,
    shipping_address FROZEN<Address>,
    PRIMARY KEY ((user_id, order_date), order_id)
) WITH CLUSTERING ORDER BY (order_id DESC)
    AND default_time_to_live = 31536000; -- 1 year TTL

-- Product inventory with materialized view
CREATE TABLE products (
    product_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    name TEXT,
    category TEXT,
    price DECIMAL,
    inventory_count INT,
    last_updated TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW products_by_category AS
    SELECT product_id, name, category, price, inventory_count
    FROM products
    WHERE category IS NOT NULL AND product_id IS NOT NULL
    PRIMARY KEY ((category), product_id)
    WITH CLUSTERING ORDER BY (product_id DESC);
2. Sharding Strategies
2.1 Partition Key Design Patterns
java
@Entity
@Table(name = "users")
public class UserEntity {
    
    @PrimaryKeyColumn(name = "user_id", 
                       ordinal = 0, 
                       type = PrimaryKeyType.PARTITIONED)
    private UUID userId;
    
    @PrimaryKeyColumn(name = "created_at", 
                       ordinal = 1, 
                       type = PrimaryKeyType.CLUSTERED,
                       ordering = Ordering.DESCENDING)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    
    @Column
    private String email;
    
    @Column
    private String username;
    
    // Custom partition key generator
    public static class PartitionKeyGenerator {
        
        // Time-based partitioning for time-series data
        public static String generateTimeBasedPartition(LocalDateTime timestamp) {
            return timestamp.format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd"));
        }
        
        // Bucket-based partitioning for load balancing
        public static int generateBucketPartition(String userId, int numBuckets) {
            return Math.abs(userId.hashCode()) % numBuckets;
        }
        
        // Consistent hashing with virtual nodes
        public static long generateConsistentHash(String key, int numVirtualNodes) {
            long hash = MurmurHash3.hash64(key);
            return hash % numVirtualNodes;
        }
        
        // Composite partition key for complex queries
        public static CompositeKey generateCompositeKey(String tenantId, 
                                                        String entityId) {
            return new CompositeKey(tenantId, entityId);
        }
    }
}

// Composite partition key implementation
public class CompositeKey {
    private String tenantId;
    private String entityId;
    
    public CompositeKey(String tenantId, String entityId) {
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
        this.entityId = entityId;
    }
    
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return tenantId + ":" + entityId;
    }
}
2.2 Dynamic Sharding Manager
java
@Component
public class DynamicShardingManager {
    
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    private final ShardingConfiguration shardingConfig;
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<String, ShardInfo> shardCache;
    
    @Autowired
    public DynamicShardingManager(CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate,
                                   ShardingConfiguration shardingConfig) {
        this.cassandraTemplate = cassandraTemplate;
        this.shardingConfig = shardingConfig;
        this.shardCache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        
        initializeShards();
        startShardRebalancing();
    }
    
    public ShardInfo getShardForKey(String partitionKey) {
        return shardCache.computeIfAbsent(partitionKey, key -> {
            int shardId = calculateShardId(key);
            return new ShardInfo(shardId, getShardNodes(shardId));
        });
    }
    
    private int calculateShardId(String key) {
        // Consistent hashing with virtual nodes
        long hash = MurmurHash3.hash64(key);
        int shardId = (int) (hash % shardingConfig.getTotalShards());
        
        // Check for shard splitting
        if (shardingConfig.isShardSplittingEnabled()) {
            ShardLoad load = getShardLoad(shardId);
            if (load.isOverloaded()) {
                shardId = splitShard(shardId, key);
            }
        }
        
        return shardId;
    }
    
    private int splitShard(int originalShardId, String key) {
        // Create new shard
        int newShardId = shardingConfig.getNextShardId();
        
        // Split range
        Range originalRange = shardingConfig.getShardRange(originalShardId);
        Range newRange = originalRange.split();
        
        // Update shard configurations
        shardingConfig.addShard(newShardId, newRange);
        shardingConfig.updateShardRange(originalShardId, originalRange);
        
        // Migrate data to new shard
        migrateShardData(originalShardId, newShardId, newRange);
        
        return newShardId;
    }
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 60000)
    public void rebalanceShards() {
        // Calculate shard load distribution
        Map<Integer, ShardLoad> shardLoads = getAllShardLoads();
        
        double avgLoad = shardLoads.values().stream()
            .mapToDouble(ShardLoad::getLoadFactor)
            .average()
            .orElse(0.0);
        
        // Identify hot shards
        List<Integer> hotShards = shardLoads.entrySet().stream()
            .filter(entry -> entry.getValue().getLoadFactor() > avgLoad * 1.5)
            .map(Map.Entry::getKey)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        // Rebalance hot shards
        hotShards.forEach(shardId -> {
            List<String> keysToMove = selectKeysForRebalancing(shardId);
            keysToMove.forEach(key -> {
                int targetShard = findLeastLoadedShard();
                migrateKey(key, shardId, targetShard);
            });
        });
    }
    
    private void migrateKey(String key, int sourceShard, int targetShard) {
        // Implement key migration logic
        CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
            try {
                // Read data from source
                Object data = readDataFromShard(key, sourceShard);
                
                // Write to target
                writeDataToShard(key, data, targetShard);
                
                // Update routing table
                updateRoutingTable(key, targetShard);
                
                // Delete from source (after verification)
                deleteFromShard(key, sourceShard);
                
                // Log migration
                logMigration(key, sourceShard, targetShard);
                
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Failed to migrate key: {}", key, e);
                handleMigrationFailure(key, sourceShard, targetShard);
            }
        });
    }
}
