3.2 Time-Series Data Modeling
java
@Entity
@Table(name = "user_activity")
public class UserActivity {
    
    @PrimaryKey
    private UserActivityKey key;
    
    @Column
    private String activityType;
    
    @Column
    private Map<String, String> metadata;
    
    @Column
    private int duration;
    
    public static class UserActivityKey {
        
        @PrimaryKeyColumn(name = "user_id", 
                           type = PrimaryKeyType.PARTITIONED)
        private UUID userId;
        
        @PrimaryKeyColumn(name = "date", 
                           type = PrimaryKeyType.CLUSTERED,
                           ordering = Ordering.DESCENDING)
        private LocalDate date;
        
        @PrimaryKeyColumn(name = "timestamp", 
                           type = PrimaryKeyType.CLUSTERED,
                           ordering = Ordering.DESCENDING)
        private Instant timestamp;
        
        @PrimaryKeyColumn(name = "activity_id", 
                           type = PrimaryKeyType.CLUSTERED)
        private UUID activityId;
        
        // Getters, setters, constructors
    }
}

// Time-series data service
@Service
public class TimeSeriesService {
    
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    
    // Bucket data by time intervals for efficient querying
    public Flux<UserActivity> getUserActivityByTimeBucket(UUID userId, 
                                                           Instant start, 
                                                           Instant end) {
        LocalDate startDate = start.atZone(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate();
        LocalDate endDate = end.atZone(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate();
        
        // Generate date range for partitioning
        List<LocalDate> dateRange = generateDateRange(startDate, endDate);
        
        return Flux.fromIterable(dateRange)
            .flatMap(date -> cassandraTemplate
                .query(UserActivity.class, 
                    QueryBuilder.selectFrom("user_activity")
                        .whereColumn("user_id").isEqualTo(userId)
                        .whereColumn("date").isEqualTo(date)
                        .whereColumn("timestamp").isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(start)
                        .whereColumn("timestamp").isLessThanOrEqualTo(end)
                        .build()
                ))
            .sort(Comparator.comparing(activity -> 
                activity.getKey().getTimestamp()).reversed());
    }
    
    // Rollup data for aggregations
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 1 * * ?") // Daily at 1 AM
    public void rollupActivityData() {
        LocalDate yesterday = LocalDate.now().minusDays(1);
        
        // Aggregate daily activity
        String query = String.format(
            "SELECT user_id, activity_type, COUNT(*) as count " +
            "FROM user_activity " +
            "WHERE date = '%s' " +
            "GROUP BY user_id, activity_type", yesterday);
        
        cassandraTemplate.execute(query)
            .flatMap(result -> {
                // Store aggregated data
                DailyActivityRollup rollup = new DailyActivityRollup();
                rollup.setDate(yesterday);
                rollup.setUserId(result.getUUID("user_id"));
                rollup.setActivityType(result.getString("activity_type"));
                rollup.setCount(result.getLong("count"));
                
                return cassandraTemplate.insert(rollup);
            })
            .subscribe();
    }
}
4. Advanced Sharding Patterns
4.1 Multi-Tenant Sharding
java
@Component
public class MultiTenantShardingManager {
    
    private final Map<String, CassandraTemplate> tenantSessions;
    private final TenantResolver tenantResolver;
    
    @Autowired
    public MultiTenantShardingManager(TenantResolver tenantResolver) {
        this.tenantResolver = tenantResolver;
        this.tenantSessions = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    }
    
    public CassandraTemplate getSessionForTenant(String tenantId) {
        return tenantSessions.computeIfAbsent(tenantId, this::createTenantSession);
    }
    
    private CassandraTemplate createTenantSession(String tenantId) {
        // Determine shard for tenant
        ShardInfo shard = getTenantShard(tenantId);
        
        CqlSession session = CqlSession.builder()
            .addContactPoints(shard.getNodes())
            .withKeyspace("tenant_" + tenantId)
            .withLocalDatacenter("datacenter1")
            .build();
        
        return new CassandraTemplate(session);
    }
    
    // Dynamic tenant placement based on size/load
    public void reassignTenant(String tenantId, ShardInfo targetShard) {
        CassandraTemplate sourceSession = tenantSessions.get(tenantId);
        CassandraTemplate targetSession = createTenantSession(targetShard);
        
        // Migrate all tenant data
        migrateTenantData(tenantId, sourceSession, targetSession)
            .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                // Update routing
                updateTenantRouting(tenantId, targetShard);
                
                // Close source session
                sourceSession.getCqlSession().close();
                
                // Replace session
                tenantSessions.put(tenantId, targetSession);
            })
            .subscribe();
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> migrateTenantData(String tenantId,
                                          CassandraTemplate source,
                                          CassandraTemplate target) {
        // Query all tables for tenant
        return Flux.fromIterable(getTenantTables(tenantId))
            .flatMap(table -> source.query(table, QueryBuilder.selectFrom(table).all()))
            .buffer(1000)
            .flatMap(batch -> target.insert(batch))
            .then();
    }
}
4.2 Hybrid Sharding with Redis Cache
java
@Component
public class HybridShardingService {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    private final ShardRouter shardRouter;
    
    // Read-through cache with sharding
    public Mono<Order> getOrder(String orderId) {
        String cacheKey = "order:" + orderId;
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(cacheKey)
            .cast(Order.class)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> {
                // Determine shard from order ID
                String shardKey = shardRouter.getShardKey(orderId);
                
                return cassandraTemplate.queryOne(Order.class,
                    QueryBuilder.selectFrom("orders")
                        .whereColumn("order_id").isEqualTo(UUID.fromString(orderId))
                        .build()
                )
                .doOnNext(order -> {
                    // Cache with TTL based on order status
                    Duration ttl = calculateTTL(order);
                    redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                        .set(cacheKey, order, ttl)
                        .subscribe();
                });
            }));
    }
    
    // Write-behind cache with eventual consistency
    public Mono<Void> createOrder(Order order) {
        String cacheKey = "order:" + order.getId();
        
        // Write to cache immediately
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .set(cacheKey, order, Duration.ofHours(1))
            .then(Mono.defer(() -> {
                // Write to Cassandra asynchronously
                return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
                    String shardKey = shardRouter.getShardKey(order.getId());
                    
                    cassandraTemplate.insert(order)
                        .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                            // Update cache with final status
                            redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                                .set(cacheKey, order, Duration.ofDays(7))
                                .subscribe();
                        })
                        .doOnError(e -> {
                            // Mark cache as stale
                            redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                                .set(cacheKey + ":stale", true, Duration.ofHours(1))
                                .subscribe();
                        })
                        .subscribe();
                });
            }));
    }
    
    private Duration calculateTTL(Order order) {
        if (order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.COMPLETED) {
            return Duration.ofDays(7);
        } else if (order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.CANCELLED) {
            return Duration.ofHours(24);
        } else {
            return Duration.ofMinutes(30);
        }
    }
}
5. Multi-Region Replication
5.1 Global Data Distribution
java
@Component
public class GlobalReplicationManager {
    
    private final Map<String, CassandraTemplate> regionalSessions;
    private final ReplicationConfig replicationConfig;
    
    @Autowired
    public GlobalReplicationManager(ReplicationConfig replicationConfig) {
        this.replicationConfig = replicationConfig;
        this.regionalSessions = new HashMap<>();
        
        initializeRegionalSessions();
        startReplicationMonitor();
    }
    
    public Mono<WriteResult> writeWithGlobalConsistency(Object entity, 
                                                          ConsistencyLevel level) {
        String entityKey = extractPartitionKey(entity);
        String primaryRegion = getPrimaryRegion(entityKey);
        
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            WriteResult result = new WriteResult();
            
            // Write to primary region
            regionalSessions.get(primaryRegion).insert(entity);
            result.addRegion(primaryRegion, true);
            
            // Replicate to other regions based on consistency level
            switch (level) {
                case QUORUM:
                    replicateToQuorum(entity, primaryRegion);
                    break;
                case ALL:
                    replicateToAllRegions(entity, primaryRegion);
                    break;
                case LOCAL_QUORUM:
                    replicateToLocalDatacenters(entity, primaryRegion);
                    break;
                case EACH_QUORUM:
                    replicateToEachRegion(entity, primaryRegion);
                    break;
            }
            
            return result;
        });
    }
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 5000)
    public void replicatePendingWrites() {
        // Process replication queue
        List<ReplicationTask> pendingTasks = getPendingReplicationTasks();
        
        pendingTasks.forEach(task -> {
            regionalSessions.get(task.getTargetRegion())
                .insert(task.getEntity())
                .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                    task.setStatus(ReplicationStatus.COMPLETED);
                    updateReplicationLog(task);
                })
                .doOnError(e -> {
                    task.incrementRetryCount();
                    if (task.getRetryCount() > 3) {
                        task.setStatus(ReplicationStatus.FAILED);
                        alertReplicationFailure(task);
                    }
                    updateReplicationLog(task);
                })
                .subscribe();
        });
    }
    
    // Conflict resolution for multi-region writes
    public Mono<Object> resolveConflict(ConflictRecord conflict) {
        // Last Write Wins with timestamp
        if (conflict.getTimestamp1().isAfter(conflict.getTimestamp2())) {
            return applyResolution(conflict.getEntity1(), conflict);
        } else {
            return applyResolution(conflict.getEntity2(), conflict);
        }
    }
    
    private void replicateToAllRegions(Object entity, String sourceRegion) {
        regionalSessions.keySet().stream()
            .filter(region -> !region.equals(sourceRegion))
            .forEach(region -> {
                regionalSessions.get(region).insert(entity)
                    .subscribe(
                        v -> log.info("Replicated to region: {}", region),
                        e -> log.error("Failed to replicate to region: {}", region, e)
                    );
            });
    }
}
5.2 Cross-Region Sync Manager
java
@Component
public class CrossRegionSyncManager {
    
    private final CassandraTemplate primarySession;
    private final CassandraTemplate secondarySession;
    private final MetricsCollector metricsCollector;
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 30000)
    public void syncRegions() {
        // Get last sync timestamp
        Instant lastSync = getLastSyncTimestamp();
        Instant currentSync = Instant.now();
        
        // Find records modified since last sync
        List<String> tables = getTablesToSync();
        
        tables.forEach(table -> {
            // Query changes in primary region
            String query = String.format(
                "SELECT * FROM %s WHERE last_updated >= '%s' ALLOW FILTERING",
                table, lastSync);
            
            primarySession.execute(query)
                .map(row -> convertToEntity(row, table))
                .buffer(100)
                .flatMap(batch -> {
                    // Apply changes to secondary region
                    return secondarySession.insert(batch)
                        .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                            metricsCollector.recordSync(table, batch.size());
                        })
                        .doOnError(e -> {
                            metricsCollector.recordSyncError(table, e);
                            handleSyncError(batch, e);
                        });
                })
                .subscribe();
        });
        
        // Update sync timestamp
        updateSyncTimestamp(currentSync);
    }
    
    // Bidirectional sync with conflict resolution
    public Mono<Void> bidirectionalSync() {
        return Mono.zip(
            syncFromPrimaryToSecondary(),
            syncFromSecondaryToPrimary()
        ).then();
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> syncFromPrimaryToSecondary() {
        // Implementation for primary to secondary sync
        return Flux.fromIterable(getTablesToSync())
            .flatMap(this::syncTableToSecondary)
            .then();
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> syncTableToSecondary(String table) {
        String lastSyncKey = "sync:" + table + ":timestamp";
        Instant lastSync = getSyncTimestamp(lastSyncKey);
        
        return primarySession.query(table, 
            QueryBuilder.selectFrom(table)
                .whereColumn("last_updated").isGreaterThan(lastSync)
                .build())
            .collectList()
            .flatMap(entities -> {
                if (entities.isEmpty()) {
                    return Mono.empty();
                }
                
                return secondarySession.insert(entities)
                    .doOnSuccess(v -> updateSyncTimestamp(lastSyncKey));
            });
    }
}
6. Performance Optimization
6.1 Query Optimization
java
@Component
public class QueryOptimizer {
    
    private final PreparedStatementCache preparedStatementCache;
    private final QueryMetrics metrics;
    
    @Autowired
    public QueryOptimizer(CqlSession session) {
        this.preparedStatementCache = new PreparedStatementCache(session);
        this.metrics = new QueryMetrics();
    }
    
    // Use prepared statements for repeated queries
    public Mono<ResultSet> executeOptimizedQuery(String query, Object... params) {
        long startTime = System.nanoTime();
        
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            PreparedStatement statement = preparedStatementCache.get(query);
            BoundStatement bound = statement.bind(params);
            
            // Set consistency level based on query type
            bound.setConsistencyLevel(determineConsistencyLevel(query));
            
            // Set query timeout
            bound.setTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10));
            
            return bound;
        })
        .flatMap(bound -> Mono.fromCompletionStage(
            preparedStatementCache.getSession().executeAsync(bound)))
        .doOnSuccess(result -> {
            long duration = System.nanoTime() - startTime;
            metrics.recordQuery(query, duration, true);
        })
        .doOnError(error -> {
            long duration = System.nanoTime() - startTime;
            metrics.recordQuery(query, duration, false);
            log.error("Query failed: {}", query, error);
        });
    }
    
    // Batch operations for better throughput
    public Mono<Void> executeBatch(List<Object> entities, 
                                    BatchType batchType) {
        BatchStatement batch = BatchStatement.builder()
            .setBatchType(batchType)
            .build();
        
        entities.forEach(entity -> {
            PreparedStatement statement = preparedStatementCache
                .get(getInsertQuery(entity));
            BoundStatement bound = statement.bind(entity);
            batch.add(bound);
        });
        
        return Mono.fromCompletionStage(
            preparedStatementCache.getSession().executeAsync(batch))
        .then();
    }
    
    // Paging for large result sets
    public Flux<Object> queryWithPaging(String query, int pageSize) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> 
            preparedStatementCache.getSession().execute(query))
        .flatMapMany(resultSet -> {
            return Flux.generate(() -> resultSet, (rs, sink) -> {
                if (rs.getAvailableWithoutFetching() > 0) {
                    Row row = rs.one();
                    if (row != null) {
                        sink.next(convertRowToObject(row));
                    } else {
                        sink.complete();
                    }
                } else if (!rs.isFullyFetched()) {
                    rs.fetchMoreResults().get();
                    sink.next(null); // Continue
                } else {
                    sink.complete();
                }
                return rs;
            });
        });
    }
    
    private ConsistencyLevel determineConsistencyLevel(String query) {
        if (query.toLowerCase().contains("select")) {
            return ConsistencyLevel.LOCAL_QUORUM;
        } else {
            return ConsistencyLevel.QUORUM;
        }
    }
}

// Prepared statement cache with eviction
class PreparedStatementCache {
    private final CqlSession session;
    private final Map<String, PreparedStatement> cache;
    private final CacheStats stats;
    
    public PreparedStatementCache(CqlSession session) {
        this.session = session;
        this.cache = Caffeine.newBuilder()
            .maximumSize(1000)
            .expireAfterAccess(Duration.ofHours(1))
            .recordStats()
            .build();
        this.stats = new CacheStats();
    }
    
    public PreparedStatement get(String query) {
        return cache.computeIfAbsent(query, q -> {
            stats.recordMiss();
            return session.prepare(q);
        });
    }
    
    public CqlSession getSession() {
        return session;
    }
}
6.2 Connection Pool Optimization
java
@Configuration
public class CassandraConnectionPool {
    
    @Bean
    public DriverConfigLoader driverConfigLoader() {
        return DriverConfigLoader.fromMap(Map.of(
            "datastax-java-driver.advanced.connection.pool.local.size", 10,
            "datastax-java-driver.advanced.connection.pool.remote.size", 10,
            "datastax-java-driver.advanced.connection.max-requests-per-connection", 32768,
            "datastax-java-driver.advanced.connection.connect-timeout", "10 seconds",
            "datastax-java-driver.advanced.connection.init-query-timeout", "5 seconds",
            "datastax-java-driver.advanced.heartbeat.interval", "30 seconds",
            "datastax-java-driver.advanced.heartbeat.timeout", "5 seconds"
        ));
    }
    
    @Bean
    public CqlSession optimizedSession(DriverConfigLoader configLoader) {
        return CqlSession.builder()
            .withConfigLoader(configLoader)
            .addContactPoints(getContactPoints())
            .withLocalDatacenter("datacenter1")
            .withKeyspace("microservices")
            .withMetrics(new MicrometerMetricsFactory())
            .build();
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ConnectionPoolMetrics connectionPoolMetrics(CqlSession session) {
        return new ConnectionPoolMetrics(session);
    }
}

@Component
class ConnectionPoolMetrics {
    
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    private final CqlSession session;
    
    @Autowired
    public ConnectionPoolMetrics(MeterRegistry meterRegistry, 
                                  CqlSession session) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        this.session = session;
        
        initializeMetrics();
    }
    
    private void initializeMetrics() {
        ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
        
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
            session.getMetricRegistry().ifPresent(registry -> {
                registry.getTimer("cql.requests").ifPresent(timer -> {
                    meterRegistry.timer("cassandra.requests.duration",
                        "type", "cql")
                        .record(timer.getSnapshot().getMean(), 
                            TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
                });
                
                registry.getMeter("connection-pool.open-connections").ifPresent(meter -> {
                    meterRegistry.gauge("cassandra.connections.open", 
                        meter.getValue());
                });
            });
        }, 0, 30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
}
7. Resilience & High Availability
7.1 Retry & Fallback Mechanisms
java
@Component
public class ResilientCassandraClient {
    
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    private final CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker;
    private final RetryTemplate retryTemplate;
    
    @Autowired
    public ResilientCassandraClient(CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate) {
        this.cassandraTemplate = cassandraTemplate;
        
        // Configure circuit breaker
        this.circuitBreaker = CircuitBreaker.of("cassandra",
            CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
                .failureRateThreshold(50)
                .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(60))
                .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(10)
                .slidingWindowSize(100)
                .recordException(this::isRecordableException)
                .build());
        
        // Configure retry template
        this.retryTemplate = RetryTemplate.builder()
            .maxAttempts(3)
            .exponentialBackoff(1000, 2, 10000)
            .retryOn(WriteTimeoutException.class)
            .retryOn(ReadTimeoutException.class)
            .retryOn(UnavailableException.class)
            .build();
    }
    
    public Mono<Object> executeWithResilience(String query, Object... params) {
        return Mono.defer(() -> 
            Mono.fromCallable(() -> 
                retryTemplate.execute(context -> {
                    if (circuitBreaker.tryAcquirePermission()) {
                        try {
                            return cassandraTemplate.execute(query, params);
                        } finally {
                            circuitBreaker.releasePermission();
                        }
                    } else {
                        throw new CircuitBreakerOpenException("Cassandra circuit breaker is open");
                    }
                }))
        )
        .onErrorResume(CircuitBreakerOpenException.class, e -> getFallbackResponse(query))
        .onErrorResume(WriteTimeoutException.class, e -> handleWriteTimeout(e, query, params))
        .onErrorResume(ReadTimeoutException.class, e -> handleReadTimeout(e, query))
        .doOnError(e -> recordFailure(e));
    }
    
    private Mono<Object> getFallbackResponse(String query) {
        // Check cache for stale data
        return getFromCache(query)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> 
                // Return default/empty response
                Mono.just(getDefaultResponse(query))
            ));
    }
    
    private Mono<Object> handleWriteTimeout(WriteTimeoutException e, 
                                             String query, 
                                             Object[] params) {
        // Log and record metrics
        log.error("Write timeout for query: {}", query, e);
        meterRegistry.counter("cassandra.write.timeout").increment();
        
        // Attempt to write to dead letter queue
        return writeToDeadLetterQueue(query, params)
            .thenReturn(new WriteResult(false, "Queued for retry"));
    }
    
    private Mono<Object> getFromCache(String query) {
        // Implement cache lookup logic
        String cacheKey = "query:" + DigestUtils.md5DigestAsHex(query.getBytes());
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(cacheKey);
    }
    
    private boolean isRecordableException(Throwable throwable) {
        return throwable instanceof WriteTimeoutException ||
               throwable instanceof ReadTimeoutException ||
               throwable instanceof UnavailableException;
    }
}
7.2 Failover & Disaster Recovery
java
@Component
public class DisasterRecoveryManager {
    
    private final Map<String, CassandraTemplate> regionalClusters;
    private final FailoverCoordinator failoverCoordinator;
    
    @Autowired
    public DisasterRecoveryManager(FailoverCoordinator failoverCoordinator) {
        this.failoverCoordinator = failoverCoordinator;
        this.regionalClusters = initializeRegionalClusters();
        
        startHealthCheck();
        setupCrossRegionReplication();
    }
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 10000)
    public void monitorClusterHealth() {
        regionalClusters.forEach((region, session) -> {
            checkClusterHealth(region, session)
                .subscribe(health -> {
                    if (!health.isHealthy()) {
                        handleClusterFailure(region, health);
                    }
                });
        });
    }
    
    private Mono<ClusterHealth> checkClusterHealth(String region, 
                                                   CassandraTemplate session) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            // Execute a simple query to check health
            ResultSet result = session.execute("SELECT release_version FROM system.local");
            Row row = result.one();
            
            ClusterHealth health = new ClusterHealth();
            health.setRegion(region);
            health.setHealthy(true);
            health.setVersion(row.getString("release_version"));
            health.setLatency(calculateLatency(session));
            health.setTimestamp(Instant.now());
            
            return health;
        })
        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
        .onErrorResume(e -> Mono.just(ClusterHealth.unhealthy(region, e.getMessage())));
    }
    
    private void handleClusterFailure(String failedRegion, ClusterHealth health) {
        log.error("Cluster failure detected in region: {}", failedRegion);
        
        // Trigger failover to backup region
        String backupRegion = getBackupRegion(failedRegion);
        CassandraTemplate backupCluster = regionalClusters.get(backupRegion);
        
        // Redirect traffic to backup
        failoverCoordinator.failoverTo(failedRegion, backupCluster);
        
        // Start recovery process
        startRecoveryProcess(failedRegion, health);
        
        // Send alerts
        sendDisasterAlert(failedRegion, health);
    }
    
    private void startRecoveryProcess(String region, ClusterHealth health) {
        CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
            try {
                // Wait for cluster to stabilize
                Thread.sleep(30000);
                
                // Attempt to reconnect
                boolean recovered = attemptRecovery(region);
                
                if (recovered) {
                    // Sync data from backup
                    syncFromBackup(region);
                    
                    // Fail back to original region
                    failoverCoordinator.failbackTo(region);
                    
                    log.info("Recovery successful for region: {}", region);
                } else {
                    log.error("Recovery failed for region: {}", region);
                    escalateFailure(region);
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Recovery process failed", e);
            }
        });
    }
    
    private boolean attemptRecovery(String region) {
        CassandraTemplate session = regionalClusters.get(region);
        
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            try {
                session.execute("SELECT * FROM system.local");
                return true;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(5000);
                } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        
        return false;
    }
    
    private void syncFromBackup(String region) {
        String backupRegion = getBackupRegion(region);
        CassandraTemplate backup = regionalClusters.get(backupRegion);
        CassandraTemplate primary = regionalClusters.get(region);
        
        // Sync data from backup to primary
        List<String> tables = getTablesToSync();
        
        tables.forEach(table -> {
            backup.execute("SELECT * FROM " + table)
                .forEach(row -> {
                    primary.execute("INSERT INTO " + table + " VALUES (?, ?, ?)", 
                        row.getObject(0), row.getObject(1), row.getObject(2));
                });
        });
    }
}
8. Migration & Operations
8.1 Schema Migration Management
java
@Component
public class SchemaMigrationManager {
    
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    private final MigrationLock migrationLock;
    private final SchemaVersionRepository versionRepository;
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void migrate() {
        // Acquire distributed lock
        migrationLock.acquireLock("schema-migration", Duration.ofMinutes(5))
            .flatMap(locked -> {
                if (!locked) {
                    log.info("Migration already in progress, waiting...");
                    return waitForMigration();
                }
                
                return performMigrations();
            })
            .subscribe();
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> performMigrations() {
        return versionRepository.getCurrentVersion()
            .flatMap(currentVersion -> {
                List<Migration> migrations = getPendingMigrations(currentVersion);
                
                return Flux.fromIterable(migrations)
                    .flatMap(this::applyMigration)
                    .then(versionRepository.updateVersion(getLatestVersion()))
                    .doFinally(signal -> migrationLock.releaseLock("schema-migration"));
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> applyMigration(Migration migration) {
        log.info("Applying migration: {}", migration.getVersion());
        
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            // Start transaction (if supported)
            cassandraTemplate.execute("BEGIN BATCH");
            
            try {
                // Execute migration script
                migration.getScripts().forEach(script -> {
                    cassandraTemplate.execute(script);
                });
                
                // Record migration
                cassandraTemplate.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO schema_version (version, applied_at, script) " +
                    "VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                    migration.getVersion(), Instant.now(), migration.getName());
                
                // Commit transaction
                cassandraTemplate.execute("APPLY BATCH");
                
                log.info("Migration {} applied successfully", migration.getVersion());
                
            } catch (Exception e) {
                cassandraTemplate.execute("ROLLBACK");
                log.error("Migration {} failed", migration.getVersion(), e);
                throw new MigrationException("Migration failed", e);
            }
        });
    }
    
    // Rollback support
    public Mono<Void> rollback(String targetVersion) {
        return versionRepository.getCurrentVersion()
            .flatMap(currentVersion -> {
                List<Migration> migrations = getMigrationsBetween(targetVersion, currentVersion);
                
                return Flux.fromIterable(migrations)
                    .sort(Comparator.reverseOrder())
                    .flatMap(this::rollbackMigration)
                    .then(versionRepository.updateVersion(targetVersion));
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> rollbackMigration(Migration migration) {
        log.info("Rolling back migration: {}", migration.getVersion());
        
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            cassandraTemplate.execute("BEGIN BATCH");
            
            try {
                migration.getRollbackScripts().forEach(script -> {
                    cassandraTemplate.execute(script);
                });
                
                cassandraTemplate.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM schema_version WHERE version = ?",
                    migration.getVersion());
                
                cassandraTemplate.execute("APPLY BATCH");
                
            } catch (Exception e) {
                cassandraTemplate.execute("ROLLBACK");
                throw new MigrationException("Rollback failed", e);
            }
        });
    }
}
8.2 Data Archival & Cleanup
java
@Component
public class DataArchivalManager {
    
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    private final S3Client s3Client;
    
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 2 * * ?") // Daily at 2 AM
    public void archiveOldData() {
        List<ArchivePolicy> policies = getArchivePolicies();
        
        policies.forEach(policy -> {
            Instant archiveBefore = Instant.now().minus(policy.getRetentionPeriod());
            
            String query = String.format(
                "SELECT * FROM %s WHERE created_at < '%s'",
                policy.getTableName(), archiveBefore);
            
            cassandraTemplate.execute(query)
                .buffer(1000)
                .flatMap(batch -> {
                    // Archive to S3
                    String archiveKey = generateArchiveKey(policy, batch);
                    byte[] archiveData = serializeBatch(batch);
                    
                    return Mono.fromFuture(
                        s3Client.putObject(archiveKey, archiveData)
                    ).doOnSuccess(v -> {
                        // Delete from Cassandra
                        deleteArchivedData(batch);
                        
                        // Record archive metadata
                        recordArchiveMetadata(archiveKey, policy, batch.size());
                    });
                })
                .subscribe();
        });
    }
    
    // TTL-based data expiration
    public void configureTableTTL(String tableName, int ttlDays) {
        String alterQuery = String.format(
            "ALTER TABLE %s WITH default_time_to_live = %d",
            tableName, ttlDays * 86400);
        
        cassandraTemplate.execute(alterQuery);
    }
    
    // Manual data purge
    public Mono<Long> purgeData(String tableName, PurgeCriteria criteria) {
        String deleteQuery = buildDeleteQuery(tableName, criteria);
        
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            ResultSet result = cassandraTemplate.execute(deleteQuery);
            return result.wasApplied() ? result.getRows().size() : 0;
        });
    }
    
    // Data restoration from archive
    public Mono<Void> restoreArchive(String archiveKey) {
        return Mono.fromFuture(s3Client.getObject(archiveKey))
            .flatMap(archiveData -> {
                List<Object> batch = deserializeBatch(archiveData);
                
                return cassandraTemplate.insert(batch)
                    .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                        log.info("Restored {} records from archive: {}", 
                            batch.size(), archiveKey);
                    });
            });
    }
}
9. Monitoring & Observability
9.1 Comprehensive Monitoring
java
@Component
public class CassandraMonitoring {
    
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    private final MetricsCollector metricsCollector;
    
    @Autowired
    public CassandraMonitoring(MeterRegistry meterRegistry,
                                CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        this.cassandraTemplate = cassandraTemplate;
        this.metricsCollector = new MetricsCollector();
        
        startMonitoring();
    }
    
    private void startMonitoring() {
        ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
        
        // Monitor table sizes
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::monitorTableSizes, 0, 5, TimeUnit.MINUTES);
        
        // Monitor query performance
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::monitorQueryPerformance, 0, 1, TimeUnit.MINUTES);
        
        // Monitor node health
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::monitorNodeHealth, 0, 30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        
        // Monitor replication lag
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::monitorReplicationLag, 0, 1, TimeUnit.MINUTES);
    }
    
    private void monitorTableSizes() {
        try {
            ResultSet result = cassandraTemplate.execute(
                "SELECT table_name, partition_count, mean_partition_size, " +
                "estimated_partition_count FROM system.size_estimates");
            
            result.forEach(row -> {
                String table = row.getString("table_name");
                long partitionCount = row.getLong("estimated_partition_count");
                long avgSize = row.getLong("mean_partition_size");
                long totalSize = partitionCount * avgSize;
                
                meterRegistry.gauge("cassandra.table.partitions", 
                    partitionCount, "table", table);
                meterRegistry.gauge("cassandra.table.size", 
                    totalSize, "table", table);
                meterRegistry.gauge("cassandra.table.avg_partition_size", 
                    avgSize, "table", table);
            });
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Failed to monitor table sizes", e);
        }
    }
    
    private void monitorQueryPerformance() {
        // Track slow queries
        List<SlowQuery> slowQueries = metricsCollector.getSlowQueries(Duration.ofSeconds(1));
        
        slowQueries.forEach(query -> {
            meterRegistry.counter("cassandra.slow_queries",
                "query", query.getQuery(),
                "duration", String.valueOf(query.getDuration().toMillis()))
                .increment();
            
            // Alert for extremely slow queries
            if (query.getDuration().toSeconds() > 5) {
                alertSlowQuery(query);
            }
        });
    }
    
    private void monitorNodeHealth() {
        try {
            ResultSet result = cassandraTemplate.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM system.local");
            
            Row row = result.one();
            String releaseVersion = row.getString("release_version");
            String dataCenter = row.getString("data_center");
            String rack = row.getString("rack");
            boolean isUp = row.getBoolean("status");
            
            meterRegistry.gauge("cassandra.node.status", 
                isUp ? 1 : 0, "dc", dataCenter, "rack", rack);
            
            meterRegistry.gauge("cassandra.version", 
                releaseVersion, "version", releaseVersion);
            
        } catch (Exception e) {
            meterRegistry.counter("cassandra.health_check.failed").increment();
            log.error("Node health check failed", e);
        }
    }
    
    private void monitorReplicationLag() {
        try {
            ResultSet result = cassandraTemplate.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM system.peers");
            
            result.forEach(row -> {
                String peer = row.getInetAddress("peer").getHostAddress();
                UUID schemaVersion = row.getUUID("schema_version");
                long timeDifference = Math.abs(schemaVersion.timestamp() - 
                    System.currentTimeMillis());
                
                meterRegistry.gauge("cassandra.replication.lag",
                    timeDifference, "peer", peer);
            });
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Failed to monitor replication lag", e);
        }
    }
    
    private void alertSlowQuery(SlowQuery query) {
        // Send alert to monitoring system
        Alert alert = Alert.builder()
            .severity(Severity.WARNING)
            .title("Slow Cassandra Query Detected")
            .description(String.format("Query: %s\nDuration: %d ms\nTable: %s",
                query.getQuery(), 
                query.getDuration().toMillis(),
                query.getTable()))
            .build();
        
        alertService.sendAlert(alert);
    }
}
9.2 Performance Dashboard
java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin/cassandra")
public class CassandraAdminController {
    
    private final CassandraTemplate cassandraTemplate;
    private final ShardingManager shardingManager;
    private final MonitoringService monitoringService;
    
    @GetMapping("/metrics")
    public Mono<CassandraMetrics> getMetrics() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            CassandraMetrics metrics = new CassandraMetrics();
            
            // Get node metrics
            metrics.setNodes(getNodeMetrics());
            
            // Get table metrics
            metrics.setTables(getTableMetrics());
            
            // Get performance metrics
            metrics.setPerformance(getPerformanceMetrics());
            
            // Get sharding metrics
            metrics.setSharding(shardingManager.getShardingMetrics());
            
            return metrics;
        });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/topology")
    public Mono<TopologyInfo> getTopology() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            TopologyInfo topology = new TopologyInfo();
            
            // Get ring information
            ResultSet ringResult = cassandraTemplate.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM system.local");
            
            topology.setLocalNode(extractNodeInfo(ringResult.one()));
            
            // Get peer information
            ResultSet peersResult = cassandraTemplate.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM system.peers");
            
            List<NodeInfo> peers = new ArrayList<>();
            peersResult.forEach(row -> {
                peers.add(extractPeerInfo(row));
            });
            topology.setPeers(peers);
            
            return topology;
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/repair")
    public Mono<Void> repairTable(@RequestParam String tableName) {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            // Trigger repair for specific table
            String repairQuery = String.format(
                "REPAIR TABLE %s", tableName);
            
            cassandraTemplate.execute(repairQuery);
            
            log.info("Repair triggered for table: {}", tableName);
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/cleanup")
    public Mono<Void> cleanupNode() {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            cassandraTemplate.execute("CLEANUP");
            log.info("Node cleanup triggered");
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/flush")
    public Mono<Void> flushTable(@RequestParam String tableName) {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            String flushQuery = String.format(
                "FLUSH TABLE %s", tableName);
            
            cassandraTemplate.execute(flushQuery);
            
            log.info("Table flushed: {}", tableName);
        });
    }
}
Best Practices Summary
Data Modeling
Design for queries first: Model tables based on access patterns

Use composite partition keys: For even data distribution

Denormalize data: Cassandra is designed for denormalization

Avoid large partitions: Keep partitions under 100MB

Use appropriate clustering columns: For sorting and filtering

Sharding Strategy
Choose partition keys carefully: Ensure even distribution

Implement dynamic sharding: For handling growth

Use consistent hashing: For predictable shard placement

Monitor shard sizes: Prevent hot spots

Plan for rebalancing: Design for future scaling

Performance Optimization
Use prepared statements: For repeated queries

Implement connection pooling: Optimize connection usage

Batch operations: For high throughput

Set appropriate consistency levels: Balance consistency and performance

Use compression: Reduce network and storage footprint

Resilience & High Availability
Implement circuit breakers: Protect against cascading failures

Use retry with backoff: Handle transient failures

Plan for multi-region: Design for global distribution

Implement failover: Automatic failover to backup regions

Regular backups: And test restore procedures

Operations
Monitor key metrics: Latency, throughput, errors

Implement schema migration: Version-controlled migrations

Regular maintenance: Repair, cleanup, compaction

Capacity planning: Monitor growth trends

Security: Encryption, authentication, authorization

This comprehensive guide provides production-ready implementations for database sharding with Cassandra in microservices architecture, covering all aspects from basic configuration to advanced patterns, performance optimization, and operations.

2. Event-Driven Core Components
2.1 Event Definitions
java
// Base Event Class
@Data
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public abstract class BaseEvent {
    private String eventId;
    private String eventType;
    private String aggregateId;
    private Long version;
    private Instant timestamp;
    private String source;
    private Map<String, String> metadata;
    
    @PrePersist
    public void prePersist() {
        if (eventId == null) {
            eventId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }
        if (timestamp == null) {
            timestamp = Instant.now();
        }
    }
}

// Product Events
@Data
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class ProductCreatedEvent extends BaseEvent {
    private String productId;
    private String name;
    private String description;
    private BigDecimal price;
    private String category;
    private Integer stockQuantity;
    private Map<String, String> attributes;
    
    public ProductCreatedEvent() {
        setEventType("PRODUCT_CREATED");
    }
}

@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class ProductUpdatedEvent extends BaseEvent {
    private String productId;
    private Map<String, Object> changes;
    private UpdateType updateType;
    
    public enum UpdateType {
        PRICE, STOCK, DESCRIPTION, ATTRIBUTES
    }
}
