2.2 Event Publisher
java
@Service
@Slf4j
public class EventPublisher {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, BaseEvent> kafkaTemplate;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;
    
    @Autowired
    public EventPublisher(KafkaTemplate<String, BaseEvent> kafkaTemplate,
                          ObjectMapper objectMapper,
                          MeterRegistry meterRegistry,
                          TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate) {
        this.kafkaTemplate = kafkaTemplate;
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        this.transactionTemplate = transactionTemplate;
    }
    
    // Publish event with transaction support
    @Transactional
    public Mono<Void> publishEvent(BaseEvent event, String topic) {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            // Enrich event with metadata
            enrichEvent(event);
            
            // Record metrics
            meterRegistry.counter("events.published",
                "topic", topic,
                "type", event.getEventType()).increment();
            
            // Publish to Kafka
            kafkaTemplate.send(topic, event.getAggregateId(), event)
                .whenComplete((result, ex) -> {
                    if (ex != null) {
                        log.error("Failed to publish event: {}", event.getEventId(), ex);
                        meterRegistry.counter("events.publish.failed",
                            "topic", topic,
                            "error", ex.getClass().getSimpleName()).increment();
                        
                        // Store in dead letter queue
                        storeInDLQ(event, topic, ex);
                    } else {
                        log.debug("Event published: {} - {}", event.getEventType(), 
                            event.getEventId());
                        
                        // Store in outbox for guaranteed delivery
                        storeInOutbox(event, topic);
                    }
                });
        });
    }
    
    // Publish multiple events as a batch
    public Mono<Void> publishBatch(List<BaseEvent> events, String topic) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(events)
            .flatMap(event -> publishEvent(event, topic))
            .then();
    }
    
    // Publish with exactly-once semantics
    public Mono<SendResult<String, BaseEvent>> publishExactlyOnce(
            BaseEvent event, String topic, String transactionId) {
        
        return Mono.fromFuture(() -> {
            ProducerRecord<String, BaseEvent> record = new ProducerRecord<>(
                topic, event.getAggregateId(), event);
            
            record.headers().add("transaction-id", transactionId.getBytes());
            record.headers().add("event-id", event.getEventId().getBytes());
            
            return kafkaTemplate.send(record);
        });
    }
    
    private void enrichEvent(BaseEvent event) {
        Map<String, String> metadata = event.getMetadata();
        if (metadata == null) {
            metadata = new HashMap<>();
        }
        
        metadata.put("service", getServiceName());
        metadata.put("environment", getEnvironment());
        metadata.put("hostname", getHostname());
        metadata.put("correlationId", getCorrelationId());
        
        event.setMetadata(metadata);
        event.setSource(getServiceName());
    }
    
    private void storeInOutbox(BaseEvent event, String topic) {
        // Store in database for guaranteed delivery
        OutboxRecord record = OutboxRecord.builder()
            .eventId(event.getEventId())
            .eventType(event.getEventType())
            .topic(topic)
            .payload(serializeEvent(event))
            .status(OutboxStatus.PENDING)
            .createdAt(Instant.now())
            .build();
        
        outboxRepository.save(record);
    }
    
    private void storeInDLQ(BaseEvent event, String topic, Throwable error) {
        DeadLetterRecord record = DeadLetterRecord.builder()
            .eventId(event.getEventId())
            .eventType(event.getEventType())
            .topic(topic)
            .payload(serializeEvent(event))
            .errorMessage(error.getMessage())
            .stackTrace(getStackTrace(error))
            .timestamp(Instant.now())
            .build();
        
        dlqRepository.save(record);
    }
}
2.3 Event Consumer
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class EventConsumer {
    
    private final Map<String, EventHandler> eventHandlers;
    private final DeadLetterProcessor deadLetterProcessor;
    
    @Autowired
    public EventConsumer(List<EventHandler> handlers,
                         DeadLetterProcessor deadLetterProcessor) {
        this.eventHandlers = handlers.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(
                EventHandler::getEventType,
                Function.identity(),
                (existing, replacement) -> existing
            ));
        this.deadLetterProcessor = deadLetterProcessor;
    }
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = {
        "product-events",
        "cart-events", 
        "order-events",
        "discount-events"
    }, groupId = "event-processor-group")
    public void consumeEvent(ConsumerRecord<String, BaseEvent> record,
                             Acknowledgment acknowledgment) {
        
        BaseEvent event = record.value();
        String eventType = event.getEventType();
        
        try {
            // Process event with idempotency
            processWithIdempotency(event)
                .doOnSuccess(v -> {
                    log.debug("Event processed successfully: {} - {}", 
                        eventType, event.getEventId());
                    
                    meterRegistry.counter("events.processed",
                        "topic", record.topic(),
                        "type", eventType).increment();
                    
                    acknowledgment.acknowledge();
                })
                .doOnError(error -> {
                    log.error("Failed to process event: {}", event.getEventId(), error);
                    
                    meterRegistry.counter("events.process.failed",
                        "topic", record.topic(),
                        "type", eventType,
                        "error", error.getClass().getSimpleName()).increment();
                    
                    // Send to DLQ for retry
                    deadLetterProcessor.sendToDLQ(record, error);
                    acknowledgment.acknowledge();
                })
                .subscribe();
                
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Error consuming event", e);
            acknowledgment.acknowledge();
        }
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> processWithIdempotency(BaseEvent event) {
        String idempotencyKey = "processed:" + event.getEventId();
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .setIfAbsent(idempotencyKey, "processing", Duration.ofMinutes(5))
            .flatMap(isNew -> {
                if (Boolean.FALSE.equals(isNew)) {
                    log.info("Event already processed: {}", event.getEventId());
                    return Mono.empty();
                }
                
                EventHandler handler = eventHandlers.get(event.getEventType());
                if (handler == null) {
                    log.warn("No handler found for event type: {}", event.getEventType());
                    return Mono.empty();
                }
                
                return handler.handle(event)
                    .doFinally(signal -> {
                        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                            .set(idempotencyKey, "completed", Duration.ofDays(7))
                            .subscribe();
                    });
            });
    }
}
3. Microservices Implementation
3.1 Catalog Service
java
@SpringBootApplication
@EnableDiscoveryClient
@EnableKafka
public class CatalogServiceApplication {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(CatalogServiceApplication.class, args);
    }
}

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/catalog")
@Slf4j
public class CatalogController {
    
    private final ProductService productService;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    
    @PostMapping("/products")
    public Mono<Product> createProduct(@RequestBody CreateProductRequest request) {
        return productService.createProduct(request)
            .flatMap(product -> {
                // Publish product created event
                ProductCreatedEvent event = new ProductCreatedEvent();
                event.setProductId(product.getId());
                event.setName(product.getName());
                event.setPrice(product.getPrice());
                event.setStockQuantity(product.getStockQuantity());
                
                return eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, "product-events")
                    .thenReturn(product);
            });
    }
    
    @PutMapping("/products/{productId}/stock")
    public Mono<Product> updateStock(@PathVariable String productId,
                                      @RequestBody StockUpdateRequest request) {
        return productService.updateStock(productId, request.getQuantity())
            .flatMap(product -> {
                // Publish stock updated event
                ProductUpdatedEvent event = new ProductUpdatedEvent();
                event.setProductId(productId);
                event.setUpdateType(ProductUpdatedEvent.UpdateType.STOCK);
                event.setChanges(Map.of("stockQuantity", request.getQuantity()));
                
                return eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, "product-events")
                    .thenReturn(product);
            });
    }
    
    @PutMapping("/products/{productId}/price")
    public Mono<Product> updatePrice(@PathVariable String productId,
                                      @RequestBody PriceUpdateRequest request) {
        return productService.updatePrice(productId, request.getPrice())
            .flatMap(product -> {
                // Publish price updated event
                ProductUpdatedEvent event = new ProductUpdatedEvent();
                event.setProductId(productId);
                event.setUpdateType(ProductUpdatedEvent.UpdateType.PRICE);
                event.setChanges(Map.of("price", request.getPrice()));
                
                return eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, "product-events")
                    .thenReturn(product);
            });
    }
}

@Service
@Slf4j
public class ProductService {
    
    private final ProductRepository productRepository;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Product> redisTemplate;
    
    @Transactional
    public Mono<Product> createProduct(CreateProductRequest request) {
        Product product = Product.builder()
            .id(UUID.randomUUID().toString())
            .name(request.getName())
            .description(request.getDescription())
            .price(request.getPrice())
            .category(request.getCategory())
            .stockQuantity(request.getStockQuantity())
            .attributes(request.getAttributes())
            .createdAt(Instant.now())
            .updatedAt(Instant.now())
            .build();
        
        return productRepository.save(product)
            .doOnSuccess(p -> {
                // Cache product
                redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                    .set("product:" + p.getId(), p, Duration.ofMinutes(30))
                    .subscribe();
            });
    }
    
    public Mono<Product> getProduct(String productId) {
        // Try cache first
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .get("product:" + productId)
            .switchIfEmpty(productRepository.findById(productId))
            .doOnNext(product -> {
                // Update cache
                redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                    .set("product:" + productId, product, Duration.ofMinutes(30))
                    .subscribe();
            });
    }
    
    @EventListener
    public void handleCartItemAdded(CartItemAddedEvent event) {
        // Check inventory when item added to cart
        productRepository.findById(event.getProductId())
            .flatMap(product -> {
                if (product.getStockQuantity() < event.getQuantity()) {
                    // Publish out of stock event
                    OutOfStockEvent outOfStockEvent = new OutOfStockEvent();
                    outOfStockEvent.setProductId(event.getProductId());
                    outOfStockEvent.setRequestedQuantity(event.getQuantity());
                    outOfStockEvent.setAvailableQuantity(product.getStockQuantity());
                    
                    return eventPublisher.publishEvent(outOfStockEvent, "inventory-events");
                }
                return Mono.empty();
            })
            .subscribe();
    }
}
3.2 Shopping Cart Service
java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/cart")
@Slf4j
public class CartController {
    
    private final CartService cartService;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    private final DiscountServiceClient discountService;
    
    @PostMapping("/items")
    public Mono<Cart> addToCart(@RequestBody AddToCartRequest request) {
        return cartService.addItem(request.getUserId(), request.getProductId(), 
                                   request.getQuantity())
            .flatMap(cart -> {
                // Publish cart item added event
                CartItemAddedEvent event = new CartItemAddedEvent();
                event.setCartId(cart.getId());
                event.setUserId(cart.getUserId());
                event.setProductId(request.getProductId());
                event.setQuantity(request.getQuantity());
                event.setUnitPrice(getProductPrice(request.getProductId()));
                event.setSubtotal(event.getUnitPrice().multiply(
                    BigDecimal.valueOf(request.getQuantity())));
                
                return eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, "cart-events")
                    .thenReturn(cart);
            });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/checkout")
    public Mono<OrderResponse> checkout(@RequestBody CheckoutRequest request) {
        return cartService.getCart(request.getUserId())
            .flatMap(cart -> {
                if (cart.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                    return Mono.error(new EmptyCartException("Cart is empty"));
                }
                
                // Apply discount if applicable
                return discountService.applyDiscount(cart, request.getDiscountCode())
                    .flatMap(discountedCart -> {
                        // Create order from cart
                        OrderRequest orderRequest = OrderRequest.builder()
                            .userId(request.getUserId())
                            .items(discountedCart.getItems())
                            .totalAmount(discountedCart.getTotalAmount())
                            .shippingAddress(request.getShippingAddress())
                            .paymentMethod(request.getPaymentMethod())
                            .build();
                        
                        // Publish cart checked out event
                        CartCheckedOutEvent event = new CartCheckedOutEvent();
                        event.setCartId(cart.getId());
                        event.setUserId(cart.getUserId());
                        event.setTotalAmount(discountedCart.getTotalAmount());
                        event.setItems(cart.getItems());
                        event.setPaymentMethod(request.getPaymentMethod());
                        event.setShippingAddress(request.getShippingAddress());
                        
                        return eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, "cart-events")
                            .then(cartService.clearCart(request.getUserId()))
                            .then(orderServiceClient.createOrder(orderRequest));
                    });
            });
    }
    
    @EventHandler
    public void handleProductPriceUpdated(ProductUpdatedEvent event) {
        if (event.getUpdateType() == ProductUpdatedEvent.UpdateType.PRICE) {
            // Update cart item prices
            cartService.updateCartItemPrices(event.getProductId(), 
                (BigDecimal) event.getChanges().get("price"))
                .subscribe();
        }
    }
    
    @EventHandler
    public void handleOutOfStock(OutOfStockEvent event) {
        // Remove out of stock items from carts
        cartService.removeItemFromAllCarts(event.getProductId())
            .subscribe();
    }
}

@Service
@Slf4j
public class CartService {
    
    private final CartRepository cartRepository;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Cart> redisTemplate;
    
    public Mono<Cart> addItem(String userId, String productId, Integer quantity) {
        return getOrCreateCart(userId)
            .flatMap(cart -> {
                CartItem existingItem = cart.findItem(productId);
                if (existingItem != null) {
                    existingItem.setQuantity(existingItem.getQuantity() + quantity);
                    existingItem.setSubtotal(existingItem.getUnitPrice()
                        .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(existingItem.getQuantity())));
                } else {
                    CartItem newItem = CartItem.builder()
                        .productId(productId)
                        .quantity(quantity)
                        .unitPrice(getProductPrice(productId))
                        .subtotal(getProductPrice(productId)
                            .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(quantity)))
                        .build();
                    cart.getItems().add(newItem);
                }
                
                cart.setTotalAmount(calculateTotal(cart));
                cart.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
                
                return cartRepository.save(cart)
                    .doOnSuccess(updatedCart -> {
                        // Update cache
                        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                            .set("cart:" + userId, updatedCart, Duration.ofHours(1))
                            .subscribe();
                    });
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<Cart> getOrCreateCart(String userId) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .get("cart:" + userId)
            .switchIfEmpty(cartRepository.findByUserId(userId))
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> {
                Cart newCart = Cart.builder()
                    .id(UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                    .userId(userId)
                    .items(new ArrayList<>())
                    .totalAmount(BigDecimal.ZERO)
                    .createdAt(Instant.now())
                    .updatedAt(Instant.now())
                    .build();
                return cartRepository.save(newCart);
            }));
    }
    
    private BigDecimal calculateTotal(Cart cart) {
        return cart.getItems().stream()
            .map(CartItem::getSubtotal)
            .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
    }
}
3.3 Ordering Service
java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/orders")
@Slf4j
public class OrderController {
    
    private final OrderService orderService;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    private final SagaOrchestrator sagaOrchestrator;
    
    @PostMapping
    public Mono<Order> createOrder(@RequestBody OrderRequest request) {
        return orderService.createOrder(request)
            .flatMap(order -> {
                // Start saga for order processing
                return sagaOrchestrator.startOrderSaga(order)
                    .thenReturn(order);
            });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/{orderId}")
    public Mono<Order> getOrder(@PathVariable String orderId) {
        return orderService.getOrder(orderId);
    }
    
    @EventHandler
    public void handleCartCheckedOut(CartCheckedOutEvent event) {
        // Create order from cart checkout
        OrderRequest request = OrderRequest.builder()
            .userId(event.getUserId())
            .items(event.getItems())
            .totalAmount(event.getTotalAmount())
            .shippingAddress(event.getShippingAddress())
            .paymentMethod(event.getPaymentMethod())
            .build();
        
        createOrder(request).subscribe();
    }
    
    @EventHandler
    public void handlePaymentProcessed(OrderPaymentProcessedEvent event) {
        orderService.updatePaymentStatus(event.getOrderId(), event.getStatus())
            .flatMap(order -> {
                if (event.getStatus() == PaymentStatus.SUCCESS) {
                    // Continue order processing
                    return sagaOrchestrator.processOrderAfterPayment(order);
                } else {
                    // Cancel order
                    return sagaOrchestrator.cancelOrder(order, event.getReason());
                }
            })
            .subscribe();
    }
}

@Service
@Slf4j
public class OrderService {
    
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private final InventoryServiceClient inventoryService;
    private final PaymentServiceClient paymentService;
    private final ShippingServiceClient shippingService;
    
    @Transactional
    public Mono<Order> createOrder(OrderRequest request) {
        Order order = Order.builder()
            .id(UUID.randomUUID().toString())
            .userId(request.getUserId())
            .items(request.getItems())
            .totalAmount(request.getTotalAmount())
            .status(OrderStatus.PENDING)
            .shippingAddress(request.getShippingAddress())
            .paymentDetails(request.getPaymentDetails())
            .createdAt(Instant.now())
            .updatedAt(Instant.now())
            .build();
        
        return orderRepository.save(order)
            .flatMap(savedOrder -> {
                // Reserve inventory
                return inventoryService.reserveInventory(savedOrder)
                    .thenReturn(savedOrder);
            })
            .flatMap(orderWithInventory -> {
                // Process payment
                return paymentService.processPayment(orderWithInventory)
                    .thenReturn(orderWithInventory);
            })
            .flatMap(orderWithPayment -> {
                // Arrange shipping
                return shippingService.arrangeShipping(orderWithPayment)
                    .thenReturn(orderWithPayment);
            })
            .doOnSuccess(order -> {
                // Publish order created event
                OrderCreatedEvent event = new OrderCreatedEvent();
                event.setOrderId(order.getId());
                event.setUserId(order.getUserId());
                event.setItems(order.getItems());
                event.setTotalAmount(order.getTotalAmount());
                event.setStatus(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
                
                eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, "order-events").subscribe();
            });
    }
    
    @Transactional
    public Mono<Order> updateOrderStatus(String orderId, OrderStatus status) {
        return orderRepository.findById(orderId)
            .flatMap(order -> {
                OrderStatus oldStatus = order.getStatus();
                order.setStatus(status);
                order.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
                
                return orderRepository.save(order)
                    .doOnSuccess(updated -> {
                        // Publish status changed event
                        OrderStatusChangedEvent event = new OrderStatusChangedEvent();
                        event.setOrderId(orderId);
                        event.setOldStatus(oldStatus);
                        event.setNewStatus(status);
                        
                        eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, "order-events").subscribe();
                    });
            });
    }
}
4. Event Streaming & Processing
4.1 Kafka Streams Processing
java
@Configuration
@EnableKafkaStreams
public class KafkaStreamsConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public KStream<String, OrderEvent> orderEventStream() {
        StreamsBuilder builder = new StreamsBuilder();
        
        // Stream of order events
        KStream<String, OrderEvent> orderStream = builder
            .stream("order-events",
                Consumed.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(OrderEvent.class)));
        
        // Filter valid orders
        KStream<String, OrderEvent> validOrders = orderStream
            .filter((key, event) -> event.getTotalAmount().compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) > 0);
        
        // Aggregate order statistics by user
        KTable<String, OrderStatistics> userStatistics = validOrders
            .groupByKey()
            .aggregate(
                OrderStatistics::new,
                (userId, event, aggregate) -> {
                    aggregate.setUserId(userId);
                    aggregate.setTotalOrders(aggregate.getTotalOrders() + 1);
                    aggregate.setTotalSpent(aggregate.getTotalSpent()
                        .add(event.getTotalAmount()));
                    aggregate.setLastOrderDate(event.getTimestamp());
                    
                    if (event.getTotalAmount().compareTo(aggregate.getMaxOrderValue()) > 0) {
                        aggregate.setMaxOrderValue(event.getTotalAmount());
                    }
                    
                    return aggregate;
                },
                Materialized.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(OrderStatistics.class))
            );
        
        // Join with user information
        KStream<String, EnrichedOrder> enrichedOrders = validOrders
            .leftJoin(userStatistics,
                (order, stats) -> new EnrichedOrder(order, stats),
                Joined.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(OrderEvent.class),
                    new JsonSerde<>(OrderStatistics.class)));
        
        // Branch orders based on value
        KStream<String, EnrichedOrder>[] branches = enrichedOrders.branch(
            (key, order) -> order.getTotalAmount().compareTo(new BigDecimal("1000")) > 0,
            (key, order) -> order.getTotalAmount().compareTo(new BigDecimal("500")) > 0,
            (key, order) -> true
        );
        
        // High value orders go to premium processing
        branches[0].to("premium-orders",
            Produced.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(EnrichedOrder.class)));
        
        // Medium value orders
        branches[1].to("standard-orders",
            Produced.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(EnrichedOrder.class)));
        
        // Low value orders
        branches[2].to("economy-orders",
            Produced.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(EnrichedOrder.class)));
        
        // Windowed aggregations for real-time analytics
        KTable<Windowed<String>, OrderAggregation> hourlyOrders = validOrders
            .groupByKey()
            .windowedBy(TimeWindows.of(Duration.ofHours(1)))
            .aggregate(
                OrderAggregation::new,
                (key, event, aggregate) -> {
                    aggregate.setCount(aggregate.getCount() + 1);
                    aggregate.setTotalAmount(aggregate.getTotalAmount()
                        .add(event.getTotalAmount()));
                    return aggregate;
                },
                Materialized.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(OrderAggregation.class))
            );
        
        // Output to Kafka topic
        hourlyOrders.toStream()
            .to("hourly-order-stats",
                Produced.with(
                    WindowedSerdes.timeWindowedSerdeFrom(String.class),
                    new JsonSerde<>(OrderAggregation.class)));
        
        return orderStream;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public KStream<String, ProductEvent> productEventStream() {
        StreamsBuilder builder = new StreamsBuilder();
        
        KStream<String, ProductEvent> productStream = builder
            .stream("product-events",
                Consumed.with(Serdes.String(), new JsonSerde<>(ProductEvent.class)));
        
        // Detect popular products (count by views/purchases)
        KTable<String, Long> productPopularity = productStream
            .filter((key, event) -> event.getEventType().equals("PRODUCT_VIEWED") ||
                                    event.getEventType().equals("PRODUCT_PURCHASED"))
            .groupBy((key, event) -> event.getProductId())
            .count();
        
        // Emit popular products every 5 minutes
        productPopularity.toStream()
            .filter((key, count) -> count > 100) // Popular threshold
            .to("popular-products",
                Produced.with(Serdes.String(), Serdes.Long()));
        
        return productStream;
    }
}
4.2 Real-time Analytics
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class RealTimeAnalyticsProcessor {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, AnalyticsEvent> kafkaTemplate;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final InfluxDBClient influxDBClient;
    
    // Real-time sales metrics
    public void processOrderEvent(OrderCreatedEvent event) {
        // Update real-time metrics
        updateSalesMetrics(event);
        
        // Detect anomalies
        detectAnomalies(event);
        
        // Generate real-time recommendations
        generateRecommendations(event);
    }
    
    private void updateSalesMetrics(OrderCreatedEvent event) {
        // Update running totals in Redis
        String minuteKey = "sales:" + getMinuteBucket(event.getTimestamp());
        redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(minuteKey, 
            event.getTotalAmount().doubleValue());
        redisTemplate.expire(minuteKey, Duration.ofHours(1));
        
        // Store in InfluxDB for time-series analysis
        Point point = Point.measurement("order_metrics")
            .time(event.getTimestamp().toEpochMilli(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
            .addField("order_id", event.getOrderId())
            .addField("user_id", event.getUserId())
            .addField("total_amount", event.getTotalAmount())
            .addField("items_count", event.getItems().size())
            .addTag("status", event.getStatus().toString())
            .build();
        
        influxDBClient.write(point);
    }
    
    private void detectAnomalies(OrderCreatedEvent event) {
        // Check for unusual order patterns
        String userKey = "user:" + event.getUserId() + ":orders";
        List<OrderCreatedEvent> recentOrders = getRecentOrders(userKey, 10);
        
        if (recentOrders.size() >= 5) {
            double avgAmount = recentOrders.stream()
                .mapToDouble(o -> o.getTotalAmount().doubleValue())
                .average()
                .orElse(0);
            
            if (event.getTotalAmount().doubleValue() > avgAmount * 3) {
                // Unusually high order amount
                FraudAlert alert = FraudAlert.builder()
                    .userId(event.getUserId())
                    .orderId(event.getOrderId())
                    .amount(event.getTotalAmount())
                    .reason("UNUSUALLY_HIGH_AMOUNT")
                    .timestamp(Instant.now())
                    .build();
                
                kafkaTemplate.send("fraud-alerts", alert);
            }
        }
    }
    
    private void generateRecommendations(OrderCreatedEvent event) {
        // Generate product recommendations based on order
        Set<String> purchasedCategories = event.getItems().stream()
            .map(OrderItem::getCategory)
            .collect(Collectors.toSet());
        
        // Find similar customers
        List<String> similarUsers = findSimilarUsers(event.getUserId());
        
        // Get products purchased by similar users
        similarUsers.stream()
            .limit(10)
            .forEach(user -> {
                List<OrderItem> items = getRecentPurchases(user, 5);
                items.forEach(item -> {
                    if (!purchasedCategories.contains(item.getCategory())) {
                        // Publish recommendation
                        RecommendationEvent recEvent = RecommendationEvent.builder()
                            .userId(event.getUserId())
                            .productId(item.getProductId())
                            .score(calculateRecommendationScore(item, similarUsers.size()))
                            .reason("BOUGHT_BY_SIMILAR_USERS")
                            .build();
                        
                        kafkaTemplate.send("recommendations", recEvent);
                    }
                });
            });
    }
}
5. Serverless Functions (FAAS)
5.1 AWS Lambda Functions
java
// AWS Lambda Handler for Order Processing
public class OrderProcessorLambda implements RequestHandler<OrderEvent, OrderResult> {
    
    private final OrderService orderService;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    
    @Override
    public OrderResult handleRequest(OrderEvent event, Context context) {
        try {
            // Process order
            Order order = orderService.processOrder(event);
            
            // Publish completion event
            eventPublisher.publishEvent(
                new OrderProcessedEvent(order),
                "order-processed"
            );
            
            return OrderResult.success(order);
            
        } catch (Exception e) {
            context.getLogger().log("Error processing order: " + e.getMessage());
            
            // Publish failure event
            eventPublisher.publishEvent(
                new OrderFailedEvent(event, e.getMessage()),
                "order-failed"
            );
            
            return OrderResult.failure(e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}

// Google Cloud Function
public class CartCleanupFunction implements BackgroundFunction<CartEvent> {
    
    private final CartService cartService;
    
    @Override
    public void accept(CartEvent event, Context context) {
        // Clean up abandoned carts
        cartService.cleanupAbandonedCarts(event.getUserId())
            .subscribe(
                success -> context.getLogger().info("Cart cleaned up successfully"),
                error -> context.getLogger().severe("Error cleaning up cart: " + error)
            );
    }
}

// Azure Function
public class InventoryUpdateFunction {
    
    @FunctionName("updateInventory")
    public void updateInventory(
            @KafkaTrigger(
                name = "inventoryEvent",
                topic = "inventory-updates",
                brokerList = "kafka:9092",
                consumerGroup = "inventory-function"
            ) OrderEvent event,
            @BindingName("output") OutputBinding<String> output) {
        
        // Process inventory update
        inventoryService.updateStock(event.getProductId(), event.getQuantity());
        
        // Output result
        output.setValue("Inventory updated for order: " + event.getOrderId());
    }
}
5.2 FAAS Orchestration
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class FAASOrchestrator {
    
    private final LambdaClient lambdaClient;
    private final CloudFunctionsClient functionsClient;
    private final AzureFunctionsClient azureClient;
    
    public Mono<ProcessingResult> orchestrateServerless(
            EventType type, 
            BaseEvent event) {
        
        switch (type) {
            case ORDER_PROCESSING:
                return executeOrderProcessing(event);
                
            case INVENTORY_UPDATE:
                return executeInventoryUpdate(event);
                
            case NOTIFICATION:
                return executeNotification(event);
                
            case DATA_ENRICHMENT:
                return executeDataEnrichment(event);
                
            default:
                return Mono.error(new UnsupportedOperationException(
                    "No FAAS for event type: " + type));
        }
    }
    
    private Mono<ProcessingResult> executeOrderProcessing(BaseEvent event) {
        // AWS Lambda for order processing
        InvokeRequest request = InvokeRequest.builder()
            .functionName("order-processor")
            .payload(SdkBytes.fromUtf8String(JsonUtils.toJson(event)))
            .build();
        
        return Mono.fromFuture(lambdaClient.invoke(request))
            .map(response -> {
                if (response.statusCode() == 200) {
                    return ProcessingResult.success(response.payload().asUtf8String());
                } else {
                    return ProcessingResult.failure("Lambda execution failed");
                }
            })
            .onErrorResume(e -> {
                // Fallback to Azure Function
                return executeAzureOrderProcessing(event);
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<ProcessingResult> executeInventoryUpdate(BaseEvent event) {
        // Google Cloud Function for inventory
        HttpRequest httpRequest = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("https://us-central1-project.cloudfunctions.net/inventory-update"))
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(JsonUtils.toJson(event)))
            .build();
        
        return Mono.fromFuture(httpClient.sendAsync(httpRequest, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()))
            .map(response -> {
                if (response.statusCode() == 200) {
                    return ProcessingResult.success(response.body());
                } else {
                    return ProcessingResult.failure("Cloud Function failed");
                }
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<ProcessingResult> executeNotification(BaseEvent event) {
        // Send notifications through serverless functions
        return Flux.merge(
            sendEmailNotification(event),
            sendPushNotification(event),
            sendSMSNotification(event)
        )
        .collectList()
        .map(results -> {
            boolean allSuccess = results.stream().allMatch(ProcessingResult::isSuccess);
            return allSuccess ? 
                ProcessingResult.success("All notifications sent") :
                ProcessingResult.partialSuccess(results);
        });
    }
}
6. Data Lake Integration
6.1 Apache Iceberg Configuration
java
@Configuration
public class DataLakeConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public IcebergCatalog icebergCatalog() {
        // Configure Apache Iceberg catalog
        HadoopCatalog catalog = new HadoopCatalog(
            new Configuration(),
            "s3a://data-lake/iceberg"
        );
        
        return catalog;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public DataLakeWriter dataLakeWriter(IcebergCatalog catalog) {
        return new DataLakeWriter(catalog);
    }
}

@Component
public class DataLakeWriter {
    
    private final IcebergCatalog catalog;
    private final SparkSession spark;
    
    @Autowired
    public DataLakeWriter(IcebergCatalog catalog) {
        this.catalog = catalog;
        this.spark = SparkSession.builder()
            .appName("DataLakeWriter")
            .config("spark.sql.extensions", 
                "org.apache.iceberg.spark.extensions.IcebergSparkSessionExtensions")
            .config("spark.sql.catalog.iceberg", 
                "org.apache.iceberg.spark.SparkCatalog")
            .config("spark.sql.catalog.iceberg.type", "hadoop")
            .config("spark.sql.catalog.iceberg.warehouse", "s3a://data-lake/iceberg")
            .getOrCreate();
    }
    
    // Write events to data lake
    public void writeToDataLake(List<BaseEvent> events, String tableName) {
        // Convert events to DataFrame
        Dataset<Row> df = spark.createDataFrame(events, BaseEvent.class);
        
        // Write to Iceberg table with partitioning
        df.writeTo("iceberg." + tableName)
            .partitionedBy(
                "year(ts)", 
                "month(ts)", 
                "day(ts)",
                "event_type"
            )
            .createOrReplace();
    }
    
    // CDC streaming to data lake
    @EventListener
    public void streamEventsToDataLake(BaseEvent event) {
        // Write in micro-batches
        String tableName = event.getClass().getSimpleName().toLowerCase();
        
        Dataset<Row> df = spark.createDataFrame(
            Collections.singletonList(event), 
            event.getClass()
        );
        
        df.writeTo("iceberg." + tableName)
            .append();
    }
}

@Component
public class DataLakeQueryService {
    
    private final SparkSession spark;
    
    // Query historical data
    public Dataset<Row> queryHistoricalOrders(String userId, 
                                              LocalDate startDate,
                                              LocalDate endDate) {
        String query = String.format(
            "SELECT * FROM iceberg.order_events " +
            "WHERE user_id = '%s' " +
            "AND ts BETWEEN '%s' AND '%s' " +
            "ORDER BY ts DESC",
            userId, startDate, endDate
        );
        
        return spark.sql(query);
    }
    
    // Time-travel queries
    public Dataset<Row> queryAsOf(String tableName, Instant timestamp) {
        return spark.read()
            .format("iceberg")
            .option("snapshot-id", getSnapshotIdAsOf(tableName, timestamp))
            .load("iceberg." + tableName);
    }
    
    // Incremental queries
    public Dataset<Row> getIncrementalChanges(String tableName, 
                                               Instant fromTimestamp,
                                               Instant toTimestamp) {
        return spark.read()
            .format("iceberg")
            .option("start-snapshot-id", getSnapshotIdAsOf(tableName, fromTimestamp))
            .option("end-snapshot-id", getSnapshotIdAsOf(tableName, toTimestamp))
            .load("iceberg." + tableName);
    }
}
6.2 Delta Lake Implementation
java
@Component
public class DeltaLakeManager {
    
    private final SparkSession spark;
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        // Configure Delta Lake
        spark.conf().set("spark.sql.extensions", 
            "io.delta.sql.DeltaSparkSessionExtension");
        spark.conf().set("spark.sql.catalog.spark_catalog", 
            "org.apache.spark.sql.delta.catalog.DeltaCatalog");
    }
    
    // Write stream to Delta table
    public StreamingQuery writeStreamToDelta(
            String sourceTopic,
            String targetPath,
            CheckpointLocation checkpoint) {
        
        Dataset<Row> stream = spark
            .readStream()
            .format("kafka")
            .option("kafka.bootstrap.servers", "kafka:9092")
            .option("subscribe", sourceTopic)
            .load()
            .selectExpr("CAST(value AS STRING) as json")
            .select(from_json(col("json"), getSchema()).as("data"))
            .select("data.*");
        
        return stream
            .writeStream()
            .format("delta")
            .outputMode("append")
            .option("checkpointLocation", checkpoint.getPath())
            .option("mergeSchema", "true")
            .partitionBy("event_type", "year(ts)", "month(ts)")
            .trigger(Trigger.ProcessingTime(Duration.ofSeconds(10)))
            .start(targetPath);
    }
    
    // Upsert to Delta table
    public void upsertToDelta(String tablePath, 
                               Dataset<Row> updates,
                               String mergeCondition) {
        updates.createOrReplaceTempView("updates");
        
        String sql = String.format(
            "MERGE INTO delta.`%s` target " +
            "USING updates source " +
            "ON %s " +
            "WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET * " +
            "WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT *",
            tablePath, mergeCondition
        );
        
        spark.sql(sql);
    }
    
    // Optimize and vacuum
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 2 * * ?") // Daily at 2 AM
    public void optimizeDeltaTables() {
        // Optimize for performance
        spark.sql("OPTIMIZE delta.`s3a://data-lake/delta/orders`");
        spark.sql("OPTIMIZE delta.`s3a://data-lake/delta/products`");
        spark.sql("OPTIMIZE delta.`s3a://data-lake/delta/carts`");
        
        // Vacuum old files (retention 7 days)
        spark.sql("VACUUM delta.`s3a://data-lake/delta/orders` RETAIN 168 HOURS");
        spark.sql("VACUUM delta.`s3a://data-lake/delta/products` RETAIN 168 HOURS");
        spark.sql("VACUUM delta.`s3a://data-lake/delta/carts` RETAIN 168 HOURS");
    }
}
7. Saga Pattern & Distributed Transactions
7.1 Saga Orchestrator
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class SagaOrchestrator {
    
    private final Map<String, SagaStep> sagaSteps;
    private final SagaRepository sagaRepository;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    
    @Autowired
    public SagaOrchestrator(List<SagaStep> steps,
                            SagaRepository sagaRepository,
                            EventPublisher eventPublisher) {
        this.sagaSteps = steps.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(
                SagaStep::getName,
                Function.identity()
            ));
        this.sagaRepository = sagaRepository;
        this.eventPublisher = eventPublisher;
    }
    
    public Mono<Order> startOrderSaga(Order order) {
        Saga saga = Saga.builder()
            .id(UUID.randomUUID().toString())
            .orderId(order.getId())
            .status(SagaStatus.STARTED)
            .steps(new ArrayList<>())
            .createdAt(Instant.now())
            .build();
        
        return sagaRepository.save(saga)
            .flatMap(s -> executeStep(s, "reserve-inventory", order))
            .flatMap(s -> executeStep(s, "process-payment", order))
            .flatMap(s -> executeStep(s, "arrange-shipping", order))
            .flatMap(s -> completeSaga(s, order));
    }
    
    private Mono<Saga> executeStep(Saga saga, String stepName, Order order) {
        SagaStep step = sagaSteps.get(stepName);
        
        return step.execute(order)
            .doOnSuccess(result -> {
                saga.getSteps().add(SagaStepRecord.builder()
                    .stepName(stepName)
                    .status(SagaStepStatus.SUCCESS)
                    .result(result)
                    .timestamp(Instant.now())
                    .build());
                
                // Publish step completion event
                eventPublisher.publishEvent(
                    new SagaStepCompletedEvent(saga.getId(), stepName),
                    "saga-events"
                ).subscribe();
            })
            .doOnError(error -> {
                saga.setStatus(SagaStatus.FAILED);
                saga.setErrorMessage(error.getMessage());
                
                // Start compensation
                compensate(saga, order, stepName);
            })
            .then(sagaRepository.save(saga));
    }
    
    private Mono<Order> compensate(Saga saga, Order order, String failedStep) {
        log.info("Starting compensation for saga: {}, failed step: {}", 
            saga.getId(), failedStep);
        
        // Execute compensation in reverse order
        List<String> completedSteps = saga.getSteps().stream()
            .map(SagaStepRecord::getStepName)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        Collections.reverse(completedSteps);
        
        return Flux.fromIterable(completedSteps)
            .flatMap(stepName -> {
                SagaStep step = sagaSteps.get(stepName);
                return step.compensate(order)
                    .doOnSuccess(result -> {
                        log.info("Compensation succeeded for step: {}", stepName);
                        saga.getSteps().add(SagaStepRecord.builder()
                            .stepName(stepName + "-compensation")
                            .status(SagaStepStatus.COMPENSATED)
                            .timestamp(Instant.now())
                            .build());
                    })
                    .doOnError(error -> {
                        log.error("Compensation failed for step: {}", stepName, error);
                        saga.setStatus(SagaStatus.COMPENSATION_FAILED);
                    });
            })
            .then(sagaRepository.save(saga))
            .then(Mono.error(new SagaException(
                "Order processing failed at step: " + failedStep)));
    }
    
    private Mono<Order> completeSaga(Saga saga, Order order) {
        saga.setStatus(SagaStatus.COMPLETED);
        saga.setCompletedAt(Instant.now());
        
        return sagaRepository.save(saga)
            .thenReturn(order);
    }
}

// Saga Step Interface
public interface SagaStep {
    String getName();
    Mono<StepResult> execute(Order order);
    Mono<StepResult> compensate(Order order);
}

// Inventory Reservation Step
@Component
public class InventoryReservationStep implements SagaStep {
    
    private final InventoryServiceClient inventoryService;
    
    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return "reserve-inventory";
    }
    
    @Override
    public Mono<StepResult> execute(Order order) {
        return inventoryService.reserveInventory(order)
            .map(reservation -> StepResult.success(Map.of(
                "reservationId", reservation.getId(),
                "items", reservation.getItems()
            )));
    }
    
    @Override
    public Mono<StepResult> compensate(Order order) {
        return inventoryService.releaseReservation(order.getId())
            .map(result -> StepResult.compensated(Map.of(
                "orderId", order.getId(),
                "released", result.isReleased()
            )));
    }
}

// Payment Processing Step
@Component
public class PaymentProcessingStep implements SagaStep {
    
    private final PaymentServiceClient paymentService;
    
    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return "process-payment";
    }
    
    @Override
    public Mono<StepResult> execute(Order order) {
        return paymentService.processPayment(order)
            .map(payment -> StepResult.success(Map.of(
                "paymentId", payment.getId(),
                "transactionId", payment.getTransactionId(),
                "status", payment.getStatus()
            )));
    }
    
    @Override
    public Mono<StepResult> compensate(Order order) {
        return paymentService.refundPayment(order.getId())
            .map(refund -> StepResult.compensated(Map.of(
                "refundId", refund.getId(),
                "amount", refund.getAmount()
            )));
    }
}
8. Monitoring & Observability
8.1 Distributed Tracing
java
@Configuration
public class TracingConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public Tracer tracer() {
        JaegerTracer tracer = new JaegerTracer.Builder("event-driven-microservices")
            .withSampler(new ConstSampler(true))
            .withReporter(new RemoteReporter.Builder()
                .withSender(new HttpSender.Builder()
                    .withEndpoint("http://jaeger:14268/api/traces")
                    .build())
                .build())
            .build();
        
        GlobalTracer.register(tracer);
        return tracer;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public GlobalFilter tracingFilter(Tracer tracer) {
        return (exchange, chain) -> {
            Span span = tracer.buildSpan("http-request")
                .withTag("component", "spring-cloud-gateway")
                .withTag("http.method", exchange.getRequest().getMethodValue())
                .withTag("http.url", exchange.getRequest().getURI().toString())
                .start();
            
            try (Scope scope = tracer.activateSpan(span)) {
                return chain.filter(exchange)
                    .doFinally(signalType -> {
                        span.setTag("http.status", 
                            exchange.getResponse().getStatusCode().value());
                        span.finish();
                    });
            }
        };
    }
}

@Component
public class EventTracingInterceptor implements ProducerInterceptor<String, BaseEvent> {
    
    private final Tracer tracer;
    
    @Override
    public ProducerRecord<String, BaseEvent> onSend(ProducerRecord<String, BaseEvent> record) {
        Span span = tracer.buildSpan("kafka-publish")
            .withTag("component", "kafka-producer")
            .withTag("topic", record.topic())
            .withTag("event-type", record.value().getEventType())
            .start();
        
        // Inject trace context into headers
        tracer.inject(span.context(), Format.Builtin.TEXT_MAP, 
            new TextMapAdapter(record.headers()));
        
        record.headers().add("trace-id", span.context().traceId().getBytes());
        
        span.finish();
        return record;
    }
}
8.2 Event Dashboard
java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin/events")
public class EventDashboardController {
    
    private final EventStore eventStore;
    private final KafkaAdmin kafkaAdmin;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    
    @GetMapping("/stream")
    public Flux<Event> streamEvents(@RequestParam String topic) {
        return kafkaAdmin.createConsumer(topic)
            .consumeWithBackpressure()
            .map(this::convertToEvent)
            .share();
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/metrics")
    public Mono<EventMetrics> getEventMetrics() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            EventMetrics metrics = new EventMetrics();
            
            // Get event throughput
            metrics.setThroughput(getThroughputMetrics());
            
            // Get event latency
            metrics.setLatency(getLatencyMetrics());
            
            // Get consumer lag
            metrics.setConsumerLag(getConsumerLag());
            
            // Get error rates
            metrics.setErrorRate(getErrorRates());
            
            return metrics;
        });
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/replay")
    public Mono<Void> replayEvents(@RequestBody ReplayRequest request) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(eventStore.getEvents(request.getStartTime(), 
                                                       request.getEndTime()))
            .flatMap(event -> eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, request.getTargetTopic()))
            .then();
    }
    
    @GetMapping("/dead-letter")
    public Flux<DeadLetterRecord> getDeadLetterMessages() {
        return deadLetterRepository.findAll();
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/dead-letter/{id}/retry")
    public Mono<Void> retryDeadLetter(@PathVariable String id) {
        return deadLetterRepository.findById(id)
            .flatMap(record -> {
                BaseEvent event = deserializeEvent(record.getPayload());
                return eventPublisher.publishEvent(event, record.getOriginalTopic())
                    .then(deadLetterRepository.delete(record));
            });
    }
}

@Component
public class EventDashboardWebSocket {
    
    @Autowired
    private SimpMessagingTemplate messagingTemplate;
    
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 1000)
    public void pushRealTimeMetrics() {
        // Push metrics to dashboard
        Map<String, Object> metrics = new HashMap<>();
        metrics.put("timestamp", Instant.now());
        metrics.put("eventsPerSecond", getEventsPerSecond());
        metrics.put("avgLatency", getAverageLatency());
        metrics.put("activeConsumers", getActiveConsumers());
        
        messagingTemplate.convertAndSend("/topic/event-metrics", metrics);
    }
    
    @EventListener
    public void onEventProcessed(EventProcessedEvent event) {
        // Push real-time event to dashboard
        messagingTemplate.convertAndSend("/topic/events", event);
    }
}
Best Practices Summary
Event-Driven Design
Event Schema Evolution: Use Avro/Protobuf for schema versioning

Idempotent Consumers: Implement idempotency keys for exactly-once processing

Event Sourcing: Store events as source of truth

CQRS Pattern: Separate read and write models

Microservices Communication
Asynchronous First: Use events for inter-service communication

Saga Pattern: Handle distributed transactions

Circuit Breakers: Protect against cascading failures

Retry with Backoff: Handle transient failures

Data Management
Data Lake: Store all events for analytics

Stream Processing: Real-time aggregations and analytics

Time-Travel Queries: Use Iceberg/Delta for historical analysis

Data Retention: Implement TTL policies

Observability
Distributed Tracing: Trace events across services

Metrics: Track event throughput, latency, errors

Logging: Structured logging with correlation IDs

Alerting: Real-time anomaly detection

Performance
Batch Processing: Batch events for better throughput

Compression: Compress messages for network efficiency

Partitioning: Proper partitioning for parallel processing

Caching: Cache frequent queries

===============================================
This comprehensive implementation provides a production-ready event-driven microservices architecture with all components working together seamlessly, from client applications through microservices, serverless functions, and data lake integration.

Microservices Distributed Caching with Rate limiting

Comprehensive Guide to Distributed Caching & Rate Limiting in Microservices

1. Distributed Caching Fundamentals

1.1 Cache Architecture Overview
text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        Distributed Cache Layer                              │
