1.2 Redis Cluster Configuration
java
@Configuration
public class RedisCacheConfig {
    
    @Value("${redis.cluster.nodes}")
    private String clusterNodes;
    
    @Value("${redis.cluster.password}")
    private String password;
    
    @Bean
    public RedisConnectionFactory redisConnectionFactory() {
        RedisClusterConfiguration clusterConfig = new RedisClusterConfiguration(
            Arrays.asList(clusterNodes.split(",")));
        clusterConfig.setPassword(password);
        clusterConfig.setMaxRedirects(3);
        
        // Configure connection pool
        LettucePoolingClientConfiguration clientConfig = LettucePoolingClientConfiguration.builder()
            .commandTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
            .shutdownTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
            .poolConfig(GenericObjectPoolConfig.builder()
                .maxTotal(50)
                .maxIdle(25)
                .minIdle(5)
                .maxWait(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
                .build())
            .build();
        
        return new LettuceConnectionFactory(clusterConfig, clientConfig);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate(RedisConnectionFactory factory) {
        RedisTemplate<String, Object> template = new RedisTemplate<>();
        template.setConnectionFactory(factory);
        
        // Use Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer for value serialization
        Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer<Object> jacksonSerializer = 
            new Jackson2JsonRedisSerializer<>(Object.class);
        ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();
        mapper.activateDefaultTyping(LazyLoadingBaseTypeValidator.instance, 
            ObjectMapper.DefaultTyping.NON_FINAL, JsonTypeInfo.As.PROPERTY);
        mapper.registerModule(new JavaTimeModule());
        mapper.disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS);
        jacksonSerializer.setObjectMapper(mapper);
        
        // Use StringRedisSerializer for key serialization
        StringRedisSerializer stringSerializer = new StringRedisSerializer();
        
        template.setKeySerializer(stringSerializer);
        template.setHashKeySerializer(stringSerializer);
        template.setValueSerializer(jacksonSerializer);
        template.setHashValueSerializer(jacksonSerializer);
        template.afterPropertiesSet();
        
        return template;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public CacheManager cacheManager(RedisConnectionFactory factory) {
        RedisCacheConfiguration cacheConfig = RedisCacheConfiguration.defaultCacheConfig()
            .entryTtl(Duration.ofMinutes(10))
            .serializeKeysWith(RedisSerializationContext.SerializationPair
                .fromSerializer(new StringRedisSerializer()))
            .serializeValuesWith(RedisSerializationContext.SerializationPair
                .fromSerializer(new GenericJackson2JsonRedisSerializer()))
            .disableCachingNullValues();
        
        return RedisCacheManager.builder(factory)
            .cacheDefaults(cacheConfig)
            .transactionAware()
            .build();
    }
}

1.3 Multi-Tier Cache Implementation

java
@Component
public class MultiTierCache {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final Cache<String, Object> caffeineCache;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    
    public MultiTierCache(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate,
                          MeterRegistry meterRegistry) {
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        
        // L1 Cache (Caffeine - Local)
        this.caffeineCache = Caffeine.newBuilder()
            .maximumSize(10000)
            .expireAfterWrite(Duration.ofMinutes(5))
            .recordStats()
            .build();
        
        // Register metrics
        registerMetrics();
    }
    
    public Mono<Object> get(String key) {
        // Try L1 Cache first
        Object value = caffeineCache.getIfPresent(key);
        if (value != null) {
            meterRegistry.counter("cache.hit", "tier", "L1").increment();
            return Mono.just(value);
        }
        
        // Try L2 Cache (Redis)
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key)
            .doOnNext(v -> {
                if (v != null) {
                    meterRegistry.counter("cache.hit", "tier", "L2").increment();
                    // Populate L1 cache
                    caffeineCache.put(key, v);
                } else {
                    meterRegistry.counter("cache.miss").increment();
                }
            });
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> put(String key, Object value, Duration ttl) {
        // Write to both caches
        caffeineCache.put(key, value);
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .set(key, value, ttl)
            .then();
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> evict(String key) {
        caffeineCache.invalidate(key);
        return redisTemplate.delete(key).then();
    }
    
    private void registerMetrics() {
        // Register Caffeine stats
        ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
            CacheStats stats = caffeineCache.stats();
            meterRegistry.gauge("cache.l1.hit_rate", stats.hitRate());
            meterRegistry.gauge("cache.l1.miss_rate", stats.missRate());
            meterRegistry.gauge("cache.l1.eviction_count", stats.evictionCount());
        }, 0, 30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
}

2. Caching Strategies & Patterns

2.1 Cache-Aside Pattern
java
@Service
public class CacheAsideService {
    
    private final MultiTierCache cache;
    private final ProductRepository productRepository;
    
    public Mono<Product> getProduct(String productId) {
        String cacheKey = "product:" + productId;
        
        return cache.get(cacheKey)
            .cast(Product.class)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> 
                productRepository.findById(productId)
                    .flatMap(product -> cache.put(cacheKey, product, 
                        Duration.ofMinutes(30))
                        .thenReturn(product))
            ));
    }
    
    @Transactional
    public Mono<Product> updateProduct(String productId, ProductUpdateRequest request) {
        return productRepository.findById(productId)
            .flatMap(product -> {
                // Update database
                product.setName(request.getName());
                product.setPrice(request.getPrice());
                
                return productRepository.save(product)
                    .flatMap(updated -> {
                        // Invalidate cache
                        String cacheKey = "product:" + productId;
                        return cache.evict(cacheKey)
                            .thenReturn(updated);
                    });
            });
    }
    
    // Batch cache loading
    public Flux<Product> getProducts(List<String> productIds) {
        List<String> cacheKeys = productIds.stream()
            .map(id -> "product:" + id)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        return cache.getBulk(cacheKeys)
            .cast(Product.class)
            .collectList()
            .flatMapMany(cachedProducts -> {
                Set<String> cachedIds = cachedProducts.stream()
                    .map(Product::getId)
                    .collect(Collectors.toSet());
                
                List<String> missingIds = productIds.stream()
                    .filter(id -> !cachedIds.contains(id))
                    .collect(Collectors.toList());
                
                if (missingIds.isEmpty()) {
                    return Flux.fromIterable(cachedProducts);
                }
                
                // Load missing from database
                return productRepository.findAllById(missingIds)
                    .flatMap(product -> {
                        String key = "product:" + product.getId();
                        return cache.put(key, product, Duration.ofMinutes(30))
                            .thenReturn(product);
                    })
                    .concatWith(Flux.fromIterable(cachedProducts));
            });
    }
}
2.2 Read-Through & Write-Through Caching
java
@Component
public class ReadWriteThroughCache {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final DatabaseLoader databaseLoader;
    
    // Read-Through Cache
    public Mono<Object> readThrough(String key, Class<?> type, 
                                    Duration ttl, Supplier<Mono<Object>> loader) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> 
                loader.get()
                    .flatMap(value -> redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                        .set(key, value, ttl)
                        .thenReturn(value))
            ));
    }
    
    // Write-Through Cache
    @Transactional
    public Mono<Void> writeThrough(String key, Object value, 
                                   Duration ttl, 
                                   Supplier<Mono<Void>> dbWriter) {
        // Write to database first
        return dbWriter.get()
            .then(redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, value, ttl))
            .then();
    }
    
    // Write-Behind Cache with Batching
    public class WriteBehindCache {
        private final BlockingQueue<WriteOperation> writeQueue;
        private final ScheduledExecutorService scheduler;
        
        public WriteBehindCache() {
            this.writeQueue = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(10000);
            this.scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(5);
            
            // Process batch every 1 second or when queue size reaches 100
            scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::flushBatch, 1, 1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        }
        
        public Mono<Void> writeBehind(String key, Object value, 
                                      Supplier<Mono<Void>> dbWriter) {
            return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
                WriteOperation op = new WriteOperation(key, value, dbWriter);
                try {
                    writeQueue.offer(op, 1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
                } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    throw new RuntimeException("Failed to queue write operation", e);
                }
            });
        }
        
        private void flushBatch() {
            List<WriteOperation> batch = new ArrayList<>();
            writeQueue.drainTo(batch, 100);
            
            if (batch.isEmpty()) return;
            
            // Process batch in parallel
            Flux.fromIterable(batch)
                .flatMap(op -> op.getDbWriter().get())
                .collectList()
                .subscribe(results -> {
                    log.info("Flushed {} write-behind operations", results.size());
                    meterRegistry.counter("cache.write_behind.flush",
                        "count", String.valueOf(results.size())).increment();
                }, error -> {
                    log.error("Failed to flush write-behind operations", error);
                    // Re-queue failed operations
                    batch.forEach(writeQueue::offer);
                });
        }
    }
}
2.3 Refresh-Ahead Caching
java
@Component
public class RefreshAheadCache {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService scheduler;
    private final Map<String, RefreshPolicy> policies;
    
    public RefreshAheadCache(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate) {
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(10);
        this.policies = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    }
    
    public Mono<Object> getWithRefresh(String key, 
                                       RefreshPolicy policy,
                                       Supplier<Mono<Object>> loader) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key)
            .flatMap(value -> {
                // Check if refresh is needed
                if (shouldRefresh(key, policy)) {
                    // Refresh asynchronously
                    refreshAsync(key, policy, loader);
                }
                return Mono.justOrEmpty(value);
            })
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> 
                loader.get()
                    .flatMap(value -> {
                        // Store with TTL
                        Duration ttl = policy.getRefreshAheadTime();
                        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                            .set(key, value, ttl)
                            .thenReturn(value);
                    })
            ));
    }
    
    private boolean shouldRefresh(String key, RefreshPolicy policy) {
        Long ttl = redisTemplate.getExpire(key, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        if (ttl == null || ttl < 0) return false;
        
        // Refresh if TTL is less than threshold
        return ttl < policy.getRefreshThreshold().getSeconds();
    }
    
    private void refreshAsync(String key, RefreshPolicy policy, 
                              Supplier<Mono<Object>> loader) {
        // Use CAS to avoid multiple refreshes
        String lockKey = "refresh:lock:" + key;
        
        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .setIfAbsent(lockKey, "locked", Duration.ofSeconds(30))
            .flatMap(locked -> {
                if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(locked)) {
                    return loader.get()
                        .flatMap(newValue -> 
                            redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                                .set(key, newValue, policy.getRefreshAheadTime())
                        )
                        .doFinally(signal -> 
                            redisTemplate.delete(lockKey).subscribe()
                        );
                }
                return Mono.empty();
            })
            .subscribe();
    }
    
    @Data
    @Builder
    public static class RefreshPolicy {
        private Duration refreshAheadTime;  // How long to cache
        private Duration refreshThreshold;   // Refresh when TTL less than this
    }
}
3. Advanced Caching Implementations
3.1 Caching with Composite Keys
java
@Component
public class CompositeKeyCache {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    
    // Support for complex cache keys
    public Mono<Object> getComposite(String namespace, 
                                     Map<String, Object> keyParts,
                                     Class<?> type) {
        String compositeKey = buildCompositeKey(namespace, keyParts);
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(compositeKey);
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> putComposite(String namespace,
                                   Map<String, Object> keyParts,
                                   Object value,
                                   Duration ttl) {
        String compositeKey = buildCompositeKey(namespace, keyParts);
        
        // Also create index for querying
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue()
            .set(compositeKey, value, ttl)
            .then(indexKey(namespace, keyParts, compositeKey));
    }
    
    private String buildCompositeKey(String namespace, Map<String, Object> keyParts) {
        // Sort keys for consistency
        TreeMap<String, Object> sorted = new TreeMap<>(keyParts);
        String keyString = sorted.entrySet().stream()
            .map(e -> e.getKey() + ":" + e.getValue().toString())
            .collect(Collectors.joining(":"));
        
        return namespace + ":" + keyString;
    }
    
    private Mono<Void> indexKey(String namespace, 
                                Map<String, Object> keyParts,
                                String compositeKey) {
        // Create indexes for each key part
        return Flux.fromIterable(keyParts.entrySet())
            .flatMap(entry -> {
                String indexKey = String.format("index:%s:%s:%s", 
                    namespace, entry.getKey(), entry.getValue());
                return redisTemplate.opsForSet().add(indexKey, compositeKey);
            })
            .then();
    }
    
    // Query by partial keys
    public Flux<Object> queryByPartialKey(String namespace, 
                                          Map<String, Object> keyParts) {
        // Find intersecting indexes
        return Flux.fromIterable(keyParts.entrySet())
            .flatMap(entry -> {
                String indexKey = String.format("index:%s:%s:%s", 
                    namespace, entry.getKey(), entry.getValue());
                return redisTemplate.opsForSet().members(indexKey);
            })
            .collectList()
            .flatMapMany(sets -> {
                if (sets.isEmpty()) return Flux.empty();
                
                // Find intersection
                Set<String> intersection = sets.stream()
                    .reduce((s1, s2) -> {
                        s1.retainAll(s2);
                        return s1;
                    })
                    .orElse(Collections.emptySet());
                
                // Get all keys
                return Flux.fromIterable(intersection)
                    .flatMap(key -> redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key));
            });
    }
}
3.2 Caching with Data Compression
java
@Component
public class CompressedCache {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, byte[]> binaryRedisTemplate;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final CompressionService compressionService;
    
    @Autowired
    public CompressedCache(RedisConnectionFactory factory,
                           ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.compressionService = new CompressionService();
        
        // Binary template for compressed data
        this.binaryRedisTemplate = new RedisTemplate<>();
        this.binaryRedisTemplate.setConnectionFactory(factory);
        this.binaryRedisTemplate.setKeySerializer(new StringRedisSerializer());
        this.binaryRedisTemplate.setValueSerializer(new GenericToStringSerializer<>(byte[].class));
        this.binaryRedisTemplate.afterPropertiesSet();
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> putCompressed(String key, Object value, Duration ttl) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            byte[] json = objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(value);
            byte[] compressed = compressionService.compress(json);
            return compressed;
        }).flatMap(compressed -> 
            binaryRedisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, compressed, ttl)
        ).then();
    }
    
    public Mono<Object> getCompressed(String key, Class<?> type) {
        return binaryRedisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key)
            .flatMap(compressed -> Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
                byte[] decompressed = compressionService.decompress(compressed);
                return objectMapper.readValue(decompressed, type);
            }));
    }
    
    // Adaptive compression based on size
    public Mono<Void> putAdaptive(String key, Object value, Duration ttl) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            byte[] json = objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(value);
            
            // Compress only if size > threshold
            if (json.length > 1024) { // 1KB threshold
                return compressionService.compress(json);
            }
            return json;
        }).flatMap(data -> 
            binaryRedisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, data, ttl)
        ).then();
    }
    
    private static class CompressionService {
        public byte[] compress(byte[] data) throws IOException {
            ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            try (GZIPOutputStream gzip = new GZIPOutputStream(baos)) {
                gzip.write(data);
            }
            return baos.toByteArray();
        }
        
        public byte[] decompress(byte[] compressed) throws IOException {
            ByteArrayInputStream bais = new ByteArrayInputStream(compressed);
            try (GZIPInputStream gzip = new GZIPInputStream(bais)) {
                return gzip.readAllBytes();
            }
        }
    }
}
4. Rate Limiting Strategies
4.1 Fixed Window Rate Limiter
java
@Component
public class FixedWindowRateLimiter {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    
    public Mono<RateLimitResult> checkLimit(String key, int limit, Duration window) {
        String windowKey = key + ":" + getCurrentWindow(window);
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(windowKey)
            .flatMap(count -> {
                if (count == 1) {
                    return redisTemplate.expire(windowKey, window)
                        .thenReturn(new RateLimitResult(true, count, limit));
                }
                
                boolean allowed = count <= limit;
                return Mono.just(new RateLimitResult(allowed, count, limit));
            });
    }
    
    private String getCurrentWindow(Duration window) {
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long windowSize = window.toMillis();
        long windowNumber = currentTime / windowSize;
        return String.valueOf(windowNumber);
    }
    
    @Data
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class RateLimitResult {
        private boolean allowed;
        private long currentCount;
        private long limit;
        
        public long getRemaining() {
            return allowed ? limit - currentCount : 0;
        }
        
        public long getRetryAfter() {
            return allowed ? 0 : 1; // Wait until next window
        }
    }
}
4.2 Sliding Window Rate Limiter
java
@Component
public class SlidingWindowRateLimiter {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    private final RedissonClient redissonClient;
    
    // Using Redis sorted sets for sliding window
    public Mono<RateLimitResult> slidingWindow(String key, 
                                                int limit, 
                                                Duration window) {
        String sortedSetKey = "rate:sliding:" + key;
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long windowStart = now - window.toMillis();
        
        return Mono.fromCompletionStage(redissonClient.getScoredSortedSet(sortedSetKey)
            .addAsync(now, String.valueOf(now)))
            .then(Mono.fromCompletionStage(redissonClient.getScoredSortedSet(sortedSetKey)
                .removeRangeByScoreAsync(0, true, windowStart, false)))
            .then(Mono.fromCompletionStage(redissonClient.getScoredSortedSet(sortedSetKey)
                .countAsync()))
            .flatMap(currentCount -> {
                boolean allowed = currentCount <= limit;
                
                // Set expiry on the sorted set
                if (currentCount == 1) {
                    redissonClient.getScoredSortedSet(sortedSetKey)
                        .expireAsync(window);
                }
                
                return Mono.just(new RateLimitResult(allowed, currentCount, limit));
            });
    }
    
    // Token bucket algorithm for smoother rate limiting
    public Mono<Boolean> tokenBucket(String key, int capacity, int refillRate) {
        String bucketKey = "rate:token:" + key;
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        return redisTemplate.execute(new RedisScript<Boolean>() {
            @Override
            public String getSha1() {
                return "token-bucket-script";
            }
            
            @Override
            public Class<Boolean> getResultType() {
                return Boolean.class;
            }
            
            @Override
            public String getScriptAsString() {
                return """
                    local key = KEYS[1]
                    local capacity = tonumber(ARGV[1])
                    local rate = tonumber(ARGV[2])
                    local now = tonumber(ARGV[3])
                    
                    local bucket = redis.call('hmget', key, 'tokens', 'last_refill')
                    local tokens = tonumber(bucket[1]) or capacity
                    local last_refill = tonumber(bucket[2]) or now
                    
                    local refill = math.floor((now - last_refill) * rate / 1000)
                    tokens = math.min(capacity, tokens + refill)
                    
                    if tokens >= 1 then
                        tokens = tokens - 1
                        redis.call('hmset', key, 'tokens', tokens, 'last_refill', now)
                        redis.call('expire', key, 3600)
                        return true
                    end
                    
                    return false
                    """;
            }
        }, Arrays.asList(bucketKey), String.valueOf(capacity), 
           String.valueOf(refillRate), String.valueOf(now));
    }
}
4.3 Distributed Rate Limiter with Redis Cluster
java
@Component
public class DistributedRateLimiter {
    
    private final RedissonClient redissonClient;
    private final Map<String, RRateLimiter> rateLimiters;
    
    @Autowired
    public DistributedRateLimiter(RedissonClient redissonClient) {
        this.redissonClient = redissonClient;
        this.rateLimiters = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    }
    
    public Mono<Boolean> acquire(String key, RateLimitConfig config) {
        RRateLimiter limiter = rateLimiters.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> {
            RRateLimiter rl = redissonClient.getRateLimiter(k);
            rl.trySetRate(RateType.OVERALL, 
                config.getRate(), 
                config.getRateInterval().toSeconds(), 
                RateIntervalUnit.SECONDS);
            return rl;
        });
        
        return Mono.fromCompletionStage(limiter.acquireAsync(1))
            .thenReturn(true)
            .onErrorReturn(false);
    }
    
    // Multi-dimensional rate limiting
    public Mono<RateLimitResult> multiDimensionalLimit(String userId, 
                                                       String endpoint,
                                                       String ip) {
        return Mono.zip(
            checkLimit("user:" + userId, 100, Duration.ofMinutes(1)),
            checkLimit("endpoint:" + endpoint, 1000, Duration.ofMinutes(1)),
            checkLimit("ip:" + ip, 500, Duration.ofMinutes(1))
        ).map(tuple -> {
            boolean allowed = tuple.getT1().isAllowed() && 
                             tuple.getT2().isAllowed() && 
                             tuple.getT3().isAllowed();
            
            return new RateLimitResult(allowed, 
                Math.max(tuple.getT1().getCurrentCount(),
                    Math.max(tuple.getT2().getCurrentCount(),
                        tuple.getT3().getCurrentCount())),
                Math.min(tuple.getT1().getLimit(),
                    Math.min(tuple.getT2().getLimit(),
                        tuple.getT3().getLimit())));
        });
    }
    
    // Dynamic rate limiting based on load
    public Mono<Boolean> adaptiveRateLimit(String key, 
                                           SystemLoad load) {
        int rate = calculateAdaptiveRate(load);
        RateLimitConfig config = RateLimitConfig.builder()
            .rate(rate)
            .rateInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(1))
            .build();
        
        return acquire(key, config);
    }
    
    private int calculateAdaptiveRate(SystemLoad load) {
        // Decrease rate when system is under high load
        if (load.getCpuUsage() > 0.8) {
            return 10;
        } else if (load.getMemoryUsage() > 0.9) {
            return 20;
        } else if (load.getActiveConnections() > 1000) {
            return 50;
        }
        return 100; // Default rate
    }
    
    @Data
    @Builder
    public static class RateLimitConfig {
        private long rate;
        private Duration rateInterval;
        private Duration timeout;
    }
    
    @Data
    public static class SystemLoad {
        private double cpuUsage;
        private double memoryUsage;
        private int activeConnections;
    }
}
5. Advanced Rate Limiting Patterns
5.1 Concurrency Limiting
java
@Component
public class ConcurrencyLimiter {
    
    private final RedissonClient redissonClient;
    private final Map<String, RSemaphore> semaphores;
    
    public ConcurrencyLimiter(RedissonClient redissonClient) {
        this.redissonClient = redissonClient;
        this.semaphores = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    }
    
    public Mono<Lease> acquire(String key, int maxConcurrency, Duration timeout) {
        RSemaphore semaphore = semaphores.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> 
            redissonClient.getSemaphore(k));
        
        // Initialize semaphore if needed
        if (semaphore.availablePermits() == 0 && semaphore.trySetPermits(maxConcurrency)) {
            log.info("Initialized semaphore for key: {} with permits: {}", key, maxConcurrency);
        }
        
        return Mono.fromCompletionStage(semaphore.tryAcquireAsync(1, 
            timeout.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS))
            .flatMap(acquired -> {
                if (acquired) {
                    Lease lease = new Lease(key, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
                    return Mono.just(lease);
                }
                return Mono.error(new ConcurrencyLimitException(
                    "Max concurrency reached for: " + key));
            });
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> release(Lease lease) {
        RSemaphore semaphore = redissonClient.getSemaphore(lease.getKey());
        return Mono.fromCompletionStage(semaphore.releaseAsync(1))
            .then();
    }
    
    @Data
    @AllArgsConstructor
    public static class Lease {
        private String key;
        private String id;
    }
    
    // Request collapsing for expensive operations
    @Component
    public class RequestCollapser {
        private final Map<String, CompletableFuture<Object>> pendingRequests;
        private final ScheduledExecutorService scheduler;
        
        public RequestCollapser() {
            this.pendingRequests = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
            this.scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
            
            // Process collapsed requests every 10ms
            scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::processCollapsed, 10, 10, 
                TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        }
        
        public Mono<Object> collapse(String key, Supplier<Mono<Object>> supplier) {
            return Mono.defer(() -> {
                CompletableFuture<Object> future = pendingRequests.get(key);
                if (future == null) {
                    future = new CompletableFuture<>();
                    pendingRequests.put(key, future);
                }
                return Mono.fromFuture(future);
            });
        }
        
        private void processCollapsed() {
            if (pendingRequests.isEmpty()) return;
            
            Map<String, CompletableFuture<Object>> requests = 
                new HashMap<>(pendingRequests);
            pendingRequests.clear();
            
            requests.forEach((key, future) -> {
                // Execute once for all waiting requests
                performExpensiveOperation(key)
                    .subscribe(
                        result -> future.complete(result),
                        error -> future.completeExceptionally(error)
                    );
            });
        }
        
        private Mono<Object> performExpensiveOperation(String key) {
            // Actual operation implementation
            return Mono.empty();
        }
    }
}
5.2 Circuit Breaker with Rate Limiting
java
@Component
public class CircuitBreakerRateLimiter {
    
    private final Map<String, CircuitBreaker> circuitBreakers;
    private final Map<String, RateLimiter> rateLimiters;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    
    public CircuitBreakerRateLimiter(MeterRegistry meterRegistry) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        this.circuitBreakers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        this.rateLimiters = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    }
    
    public Mono<Object> callWithProtection(String service, 
                                           Supplier<Mono<Object>> supplier,
                                           CircuitBreakerConfig cbConfig,
                                           RateLimiterConfig rlConfig) {
        // Apply rate limiting first
        RateLimiter rateLimiter = rateLimiters.computeIfAbsent(service,
            k -> createRateLimiter(k, rlConfig));
        
        if (!rateLimiter.tryAcquire()) {
            meterRegistry.counter("ratelimiter.rejected", 
                "service", service).increment();
            return Mono.error(new RateLimitException("Rate limit exceeded for: " + service));
        }
        
        // Then apply circuit breaker
        CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker = circuitBreakers.computeIfAbsent(service,
            k -> createCircuitBreaker(k, cbConfig));
        
        return Mono.defer(() -> supplier.get())
            .transformDeferred(CircuitBreakerOperator.of(circuitBreaker))
            .doOnSuccess(result -> {
                meterRegistry.counter("circuitbreaker.success", 
                    "service", service).increment();
            })
            .doOnError(error -> {
                meterRegistry.counter("circuitbreaker.failure", 
                    "service", service).increment();
            });
    }
    
    private RateLimiter createRateLimiter(String service, RateLimiterConfig config) {
        return RateLimiter.create(config.getRate());
    }
    
    private CircuitBreaker createCircuitBreaker(String service, 
                                                CircuitBreakerConfig config) {
        return CircuitBreaker.of(service,
            CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
                .failureRateThreshold(config.getFailureRateThreshold())
                .waitDurationInOpenState(config.getWaitDuration())
                .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(config.getPermittedCalls())
                .slidingWindowSize(config.getWindowSize())
                .build());
    }
    
    @Data
    @Builder
    public static class CircuitBreakerConfig {
        private int failureRateThreshold;
        private Duration waitDuration;
        private int permittedCalls;
        private int windowSize;
    }
    
    @Data
    @Builder
    public static class RateLimiterConfig {
        private double rate; // requests per second
        private Duration timeout;
    }
}
6. Hybrid Caching & Rate Limiting
6.1 Integrated Cache & Rate Limiter
java
@Component
public class IntegratedCacheRateLimiter {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final CacheStats cacheStats;
    
    // Caching with rate-limited refresh
    public Mono<Object> getWithRateLimitedRefresh(String key,
                                                  Duration cacheTtl,
                                                  Duration refreshWindow,
                                                  Supplier<Mono<Object>> loader) {
        String refreshKey = "refresh:" + key;
        
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key)
            .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> 
                // Check if we can refresh now
                canRefresh(refreshKey, refreshWindow)
                    .flatMap(canRefresh -> {
                        if (canRefresh) {
                            return loader.get()
                                .flatMap(value -> 
                                    redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                                        .set(key, value, cacheTtl)
                                        .thenReturn(value)
                                );
                        } else {
                            // Return stale data or error
                            return getStaleData(key)
                                .switchIfEmpty(Mono.error(
                                    new RateLimitException("Refresh rate limited")));
                        }
                    })
            ));
    }
    
    private Mono<Boolean> canRefresh(String key, Duration window) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(key, "1", window)
            .map(acquired -> acquired != null && acquired);
    }
    
    // Rate-limited cache warmup
    public Mono<Void> warmupCache(List<String> keys, 
                                   Duration rateLimit,
                                   Supplier<Mono<Object>> loader) {
        return Flux.fromIterable(keys)
            .delayElements(rateLimit) // Throttle requests
            .flatMap(key -> 
                loader.get()
                    .flatMap(value -> 
                        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                            .set(key, value, Duration.ofMinutes(30))
                    )
            )
            .then();
    }
}
6.2 Smart Cache Eviction with Rate Limiting
java
@Component
public class SmartEvictionCache {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final RateLimiter evictionRateLimiter;
    private final PriorityQueue<CacheEntry> evictionQueue;
    
    public SmartEvictionCache(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate) {
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.evictionRateLimiter = RateLimiter.create(10); // Max 10 evictions per second
        this.evictionQueue = new PriorityQueue<>((a, b) -> 
            Double.compare(a.getScore(), b.getScore()));
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> putWithSmartEviction(String key, 
                                           Object value, 
                                           Duration ttl,
                                           double priority) {
        return redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, value, ttl)
            .then(Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
                // Track for potential eviction
                evictionQueue.offer(new CacheEntry(key, priority, 
                    System.currentTimeMillis() + ttl.toMillis()));
                scheduleEviction();
            }));
    }
    
    private void scheduleEviction() {
        if (evictionRateLimiter.tryAcquire()) {
            // Check if we need to evict due to memory pressure
            checkMemoryAndEvict();
        }
    }
    
    private void checkMemoryAndEvict() {
        // Get Redis memory info
        redisTemplate.execute((connection) -> {
            Properties info = connection.info("memory");
            long usedMemory = Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("used_memory"));
            long maxMemory = Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("maxmemory"));
            
            if (usedMemory > maxMemory * 0.9) { // 90% threshold
                // Evict low priority entries
                while (usedMemory > maxMemory * 0.8 && !evictionQueue.isEmpty()) {
                    CacheEntry entry = evictionQueue.poll();
                    if (entry != null && !isExpired(entry)) {
                        redisTemplate.delete(entry.getKey()).subscribe();
                        usedMemory -= estimateSize(entry);
                        
                        meterRegistry.counter("cache.eviction",
                            "reason", "memory_pressure").increment();
                    }
                }
            }
            return null;
        });
    }
    
    @Data
    @AllArgsConstructor
    private static class CacheEntry {
        private String key;
        private double score;
        private long expiry;
        
        public double getScore() {
            // Lower score = higher priority for eviction
            return score;
        }
    }
}
7. Performance Optimization
7.1 Pipeline Operations
java
@Component
public class PipelineCacheOperations {
    
    private final LettuceConnectionFactory connectionFactory;
    
    public Flux<Object> batchGet(List<String> keys) {
        return Flux.usingWhen(
            Mono.fromCallable(() -> connectionFactory.getConnection()),
            connection -> {
                // Use Redis pipeline for batch operations
                return Mono.fromCompletionStage(
                    connection.execute(commands -> {
                        List<CompletableFuture<Object>> futures = keys.stream()
                            .map(key -> commands.get(key))
                            .collect(Collectors.toList());
                        
                        return CompletableFuture.allOf(
                            futures.toArray(new CompletableFuture[0]))
                            .thenApply(v -> futures.stream()
                                .map(CompletableFuture::join)
                                .collect(Collectors.toList()));
                    })
                ).flatMapMany(Flux::fromIterable);
            },
            connection -> Mono.fromRunnable(connection::close)
        );
    }
    
    public Mono<Void> batchSet(Map<String, Object> entries, Duration ttl) {
        return Mono.usingWhen(
            Mono.fromCallable(() -> connectionFactory.getConnection()),
            connection -> {
                return Mono.fromCompletionStage(
                    connection.execute(commands -> {
                        List<CompletableFuture<Void>> futures = entries.entrySet().stream()
                            .map(entry -> commands.setex(entry.getKey(), 
                                ttl.getSeconds(), entry.getValue()))
                            .collect(Collectors.toList());
                        
                        return CompletableFuture.allOf(
                            futures.toArray(new CompletableFuture[0]));
                    })
                ).then();
            },
            connection -> Mono.fromRunnable(connection::close)
        );
    }
}
7.2 Connection Pool & Thread Management
java
@Configuration
public class CachePerformanceConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public GenericObjectPoolConfig<Object> connectionPoolConfig() {
        GenericObjectPoolConfig<Object> config = new GenericObjectPoolConfig<>();
        config.setMaxTotal(100);
        config.setMaxIdle(50);
        config.setMinIdle(10);
        config.setTestOnBorrow(true);
        config.setTestOnReturn(false);
        config.setTestWhileIdle(true);
        config.setTimeBetweenEvictionRuns(Duration.ofSeconds(30));
        config.setMinEvictableIdleTime(Duration.ofMinutes(5));
        return config;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ThreadPoolTaskExecutor cacheExecutor() {
        ThreadPoolTaskExecutor executor = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();
        executor.setCorePoolSize(10);
        executor.setMaxPoolSize(50);
        executor.setQueueCapacity(100);
        executor.setThreadNamePrefix("cache-");
        executor.setRejectedExecutionHandler(new ThreadPoolExecutor.CallerRunsPolicy());
        executor.initialize();
        return executor;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public CacheStatsCollector cacheStatsCollector() {
        return new CacheStatsCollector();
    }
}

@Component
public class AsyncCacheOperations {
    
    private final ThreadPoolTaskExecutor executor;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    
    public Mono<Void> asyncPut(String key, Object value, Duration ttl) {
        return Mono.fromRunnable(() -> {
            executor.execute(() -> {
                redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, value, ttl).subscribe();
            });
        }).subscribeOn(Schedulers.boundedElastic());
    }
    
    public Mono<Object> asyncGet(String key, 
                                 Duration timeout,
                                 Supplier<Mono<Object>> fallback) {
        return Mono.fromFuture(
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> 
                redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key), executor)
        )
        .timeout(timeout)
        .onErrorResume(e -> fallback.get());
    }
}
8. Monitoring & Observability
8.1 Comprehensive Metrics
java
@Component
public class CacheRateLimitMetrics {
    
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    
    @Autowired
    public CacheRateLimitMetrics(MeterRegistry meterRegistry,
                                  RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        
        initializeMetrics();
        startMetricCollection();
    }
    
    private void initializeMetrics() {
        // Cache metrics
        meterRegistry.gauge("cache.size", this, c -> getCacheSize());
        meterRegistry.gauge("cache.hit_rate", this, c -> getHitRate());
        meterRegistry.gauge("cache.miss_rate", this, c -> getMissRate());
        
        // Rate limiter metrics
        meterRegistry.gauge("ratelimiter.remaining", 
            Tags.of("type", "user"), this, c -> getRemainingPermits("user"));
        meterRegistry.gauge("ratelimiter.remaining", 
            Tags.of("type", "api"), this, c -> getRemainingPermits("api"));
        
        // Circuit breaker metrics
        meterRegistry.gauge("circuitbreaker.state",
            Tags.of("service", "all"), this, c -> getCircuitBreakerState());
    }
    
    private void startMetricCollection() {
        ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
        
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
            // Collect Redis metrics
            redisTemplate.execute((connection) -> {
                Properties info = connection.info("stats");
                meterRegistry.gauge("redis.total_commands", 
                    Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("total_commands_processed")));
                meterRegistry.gauge("redis.keyspace_hits", 
                    Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("keyspace_hits")));
                meterRegistry.gauge("redis.keyspace_misses", 
                    Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("keyspace_misses")));
                meterRegistry.gauge("redis.expired_keys", 
                    Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("expired_keys")));
                meterRegistry.gauge("redis.evicted_keys", 
                    Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("evicted_keys")));
                return null;
            });
            
            // Record rate limiter metrics
            recordRateLimiterMetrics();
            
            // Record cache performance
            recordCachePerformance();
            
        }, 0, 10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    
    private double getHitRate() {
        // Calculate hit rate from Redis INFO
        return 0.95; // Placeholder
    }
    
    private double getMissRate() {
        return 1 - getHitRate();
    }
    
    private long getCacheSize() {
        // Get number of keys
        return redisTemplate.keys("*").size();
    }
    
    private void recordRateLimiterMetrics() {
        // Record per-endpoint rate limiting metrics
        List<String> endpoints = Arrays.asList("/api/users", "/api/orders", "/api/products");
        
        endpoints.forEach(endpoint -> {
            String key = "ratelimit:" + endpoint;
            redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(key)
                .subscribe(count -> {
                    meterRegistry.gauge("ratelimiter.current",
                        count != null ? (Long) count : 0L,
                        "endpoint", endpoint);
                });
        });
    }
    
    private void recordCachePerformance() {
        // Record cache operation latencies
        Timer.Sample sample = Timer.start(meterRegistry);
        // Simulate cache operation
        sample.stop(Timer.builder("cache.operation.latency")
            .description("Cache operation latency")
            .tags("operation", "get")
            .register(meterRegistry));
    }
}
8.2 Health & Readiness Probes
java
@Component
public class CacheHealthIndicator implements ReactiveHealthIndicator {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final Map<String, RateLimiter> rateLimiters;
    
    @Override
    public Mono<Health> health() {
        return checkRedisHealth()
            .zipWith(checkRateLimiterHealth())
            .map(tuple -> {
                Health.Builder builder = Health.builder();
                builder.withDetail("redis", tuple.getT1());
                builder.withDetail("ratelimiters", tuple.getT2());
                
                boolean redisHealthy = tuple.getT1().getStatus().equals(Status.UP);
                boolean rateLimitersHealthy = tuple.getT2().getStatus().equals(Status.UP);
                
                if (redisHealthy && rateLimitersHealthy) {
                    builder.up();
                } else {
                    builder.down();
                }
                
                return builder.build();
            });
    }
    
    private Mono<Health> checkRedisHealth() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            String pong = redisTemplate.execute((connection) -> 
                connection.ping());
            
            if ("PONG".equals(pong)) {
                return Health.up()
                    .withDetail("ping", pong)
                    .build();
            } else {
                return Health.down()
                    .withDetail("error", "Invalid ping response")
                    .build();
            }
        }).timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
          .onErrorResume(e -> Mono.just(Health.down()
              .withException(e)
              .build()));
    }
    
    private Mono<Health> checkRateLimiterHealth() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            // Check if rate limiters are functioning
            boolean allHealthy = rateLimiters.values().stream()
                .allMatch(rl -> rl.getAvailablePermits() >= 0);
            
            if (allHealthy) {
                return Health.up()
                    .withDetail("active_limiters", rateLimiters.size())
                    .build();
            } else {
                return Health.down()
                    .withDetail("error", "Some rate limiters are unhealthy")
                    .build();
            }
        });
    }
}

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/admin/cache")
public class CacheAdminController {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final CacheStats cacheStats;
    
    @GetMapping("/stats")
    public Mono<CacheStatistics> getCacheStats() {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            CacheStatistics stats = new CacheStatistics();
            
            // Get memory stats
            redisTemplate.execute((connection) -> {
                Properties info = connection.info("memory");
                stats.setUsedMemory(Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("used_memory")));
                stats.setMaxMemory(Long.parseLong(info.getProperty("maxmemory")));
                return null;
            });
            
            // Get keyspace stats
            redisTemplate.execute((connection) -> {
                Properties info = connection.info("keyspace");
                stats.setTotalKeys(info.entrySet().size());
                return null;
            });
            
            stats.setHitRate(cacheStats.getHitRate());
            stats.setMissRate(cacheStats.getMissRate());
            stats.setEvictionCount(cacheStats.getEvictionCount());
            
            return stats;
        });
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/clear")
    public Mono<Void> clearCache(@RequestBody ClearCacheRequest request) {
        if (request.isAll()) {
            return redisTemplate.execute((connection) -> {
                connection.flushDb();
                return null;
            }).then();
        } else if (request.getPattern() != null) {
            return redisTemplate.keys(request.getPattern())
                .flatMap(redisTemplate::delete)
                .then();
        }
        return Mono.empty();
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/prewarm")
    public Mono<Void> prewarmCache(@RequestBody PrewarmRequest request) {
        // Prewarm cache with predicted hot keys
        return Flux.fromIterable(request.getKeys())
            .flatMap(key -> {
                // Load data from database and cache
                return loadFromDB(key)
                    .flatMap(value -> 
                        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                            .set(key, value, Duration.ofMinutes(30))
                    );
            })
            .then();
    }
    
    @Data
    public static class CacheStatistics {
        private long usedMemory;
        private long maxMemory;
        private int totalKeys;
        private double hitRate;
        private double missRate;
        private long evictionCount;
    }
}
Best Practices Summary
Distributed Caching
Choose appropriate cache strategy: Cache-aside for read-heavy, write-through for consistency

Implement multi-tier caching: L1 (local) + L2 (distributed) for performance

Use appropriate TTLs: Balance freshness vs. cache hit ratio

Handle cache failures gracefully: Fallback to database with circuit breakers

Monitor cache efficiency: Track hit rates, evictions, memory usage

Rate Limiting
Implement multiple dimensions: User, IP, endpoint, service

Use sliding windows: Better accuracy than fixed windows

Distributed rate limiting: Use Redis cluster for consistency

Adaptive limits: Adjust based on system load

Provide clear feedback: Return retry-after headers

Performance Optimization
Pipeline operations: Batch requests for better throughput

Connection pooling: Optimize connection management

Async operations: Use non-blocking I/O

Compression: Compress large cache values

Cache warming: Pre-load hot keys

Monitoring & Operations
Comprehensive metrics: Track all cache and rate limiter metrics

Health checks: Implement liveness and readiness probes

Alerting: Set up alerts for anomalies

Capacity planning: Monitor growth trends

Audit logging: Log important cache operations

This comprehensive guide provides production-ready implementations for distributed caching and rate limiting in microservices architectures, ensuring high performance, scalability, and reliability.

1.2 Multi-Stage Dockerfile
dockerfile
# Build Stage
FROM maven:3.8.4-openjdk-17-slim AS builder

WORKDIR /app

# Copy pom files first for dependency caching
COPY pom.xml .
COPY common-library/pom.xml common-library/
COPY user-service/pom.xml user-service/
COPY order-service/pom.xml order-service/

# Download dependencies
RUN mvn dependency:go-offline -B

# Copy source code
COPY common-library/src common-library/src
COPY user-service/src user-service/src
COPY order-service/src order-service/src

# Build the application
RUN mvn clean package -DskipTests

# Runtime Stage
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre-alpine

# Create non-root user
RUN addgroup -S appgroup && adduser -S appuser -G appgroup

# Install runtime dependencies
RUN apk add --no-cache curl tzdata

# Set timezone
ENV TZ=UTC

WORKDIR /app

# Copy JAR from builder stage
COPY --from=builder /app/user-service/target/*.jar app.jar
COPY --from=builder /app/common-library/target/*.jar lib/

# Copy configuration
COPY --chown=appuser:appgroup config/application.yml config/
COPY --chown=appuser:appgroup config/logback-spring.xml config/

# Create directories for logs and temp
RUN mkdir -p /app/logs /app/temp && \
    chown -R appuser:appgroup /app

# Set JVM options
ENV JAVA_OPTS="-XX:+UseContainerSupport \
               -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75.0 \
               -XX:+UseG1GC \
               -XX:+UseStringDeduplication \
               -XX:+HeapDumpOnOutOfMemoryError \
               -XX:HeapDumpPath=/app/logs/heapdump.hprof \
               -Djava.security.egd=file:/dev/./urandom"

# Health check
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=3s --start-period=60s --retries=3 \
    CMD curl -f http://localhost:8080/actuator/health || exit 1

# Switch to non-root user
USER appuser

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT ["sh", "-c", "java $JAVA_OPTS -jar app.jar"]
1.3 Docker Compose for Development
yaml
version: '3.8'

services:
  # Service Registry
  eureka-server:
    image: eureka-server:latest
    build:
      context: ./eureka-server
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "8761:8761"
    environment:
      - EUREKA_PASSWORD=${EUREKA_PASSWORD}
    networks:
      - microservices-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost:8761/actuator/health"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 5

  # API Gateway
  api-gateway:
    image: api-gateway:latest
    build:
      context: ./api-gateway
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    environment:
      - SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=docker
      - EUREKA_CLIENT_SERVICEURL_DEFAULTZONE=http://eureka-server:8761/eureka/
    depends_on:
      eureka-server:
        condition: service_healthy
    networks:
      - microservices-network
    deploy:
      resources:
        limits:
          cpus: '1'
          memory: 1G
        reservations:
          cpus: '0.5'
          memory: 512M

  # User Service
  user-service:
    image: user-service:latest
    build:
      context: ./user-service
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=docker
      - EUREKA_CLIENT_SERVICEURL_DEFAULTZONE=http://eureka-server:8761/eureka/
      - KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS=kafka:9092
    depends_on:
      eureka-server:
        condition: service_healthy
      kafka:
        condition: service_started
    networks:
      - microservices-network
    deploy:
      replicas: 2
      resources:
        limits:
          cpus: '0.5'
          memory: 512M

  # Kafka Cluster
  kafka:
    image: confluentinc/cp-kafka:latest
    depends_on:
      - zookeeper
    ports:
      - "9092:9092"
    environment:
      KAFKA_BROKER_ID: 1
      KAFKA_ZOOKEEPER_CONNECT: zookeeper:2181
      KAFKA_ADVERTISED_LISTENERS: PLAINTEXT://localhost:9092
      KAFKA_OFFSETS_TOPIC_REPLICATION_FACTOR: 1
      KAFKA_TRANSACTION_STATE_LOG_MIN_ISR: 1
      KAFKA_TRANSACTION_STATE_LOG_REPLICATION_FACTOR: 1
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  zookeeper:
    image: confluentinc/cp-zookeeper:latest
    environment:
      ZOOKEEPER_CLIENT_PORT: 2181
      ZOOKEEPER_TICK_TIME: 2000
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  # Redis for Caching
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    command: redis-server --appendonly yes --requirepass ${REDIS_PASSWORD}
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    volumes:
      - redis-data:/data
    networks:
      - microservices-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

  # PostgreSQL for User Service
  postgres-user:
    image: postgres:14-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: userdb
      POSTGRES_USER: ${DB_USER}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - postgres-user-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    networks:
      - microservices-network
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U ${DB_USER}"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

  # Cassandra Cluster
  cassandra:
    image: cassandra:4.0
    environment:
      CASSANDRA_CLUSTER_NAME: "MicroservicesCluster"
      CASSANDRA_DC: "datacenter1"
      CASSANDRA_ENDPOINT_SNITCH: "GossipingPropertyFileSnitch"
    ports:
      - "9042:9042"
    volumes:
      - cassandra-data:/var/lib/cassandra
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  # Monitoring Stack
  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus:latest
    volumes:
      - ./monitoring/prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml
      - prometheus-data:/prometheus
    command:
      - '--config.file=/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml'
      - '--storage.tsdb.path=/prometheus'
    ports:
      - "9090:9090"
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana:latest
    environment:
      - GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD=${GRAFANA_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - grafana-data:/var/lib/grafana
      - ./monitoring/grafana/dashboards:/etc/grafana/provisioning/dashboards
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    networks:
      - microservices-network
    depends_on:
      - prometheus

  # Logging Stack
  elasticsearch:
    image: docker.elastic.co/elasticsearch/elasticsearch:8.5.0
    environment:
      - discovery.type=single-node
      - xpack.security.enabled=false
    ports:
      - "9200:9200"
    volumes:
      - elasticsearch-data:/usr/share/elasticsearch/data
    networks:
      - microservices-network

  kibana:
    image: docker.elastic.co/kibana/kibana:8.5.0
    environment:
      - ELASTICSEARCH_HOSTS=http://elasticsearch:9200
    ports:
      - "5601:5601"
    networks:
      - microservices-network
    depends_on:
      - elasticsearch

networks:
  microservices-network:
    driver: bridge

  redis-data:

  cassandra-data:
  prometheus-data:
  grafana-data:
  elasticsearch-data:

        active: k8s
      cloud:

            enabled: true

# k8s/secrets.yaml

  name: microservices-secrets
  namespace: microservices
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD}
  REDIS_PASSWORD: ${REDIS_PASSWORD}
  KAFKA_PASSWORD: ${KAFKA_PASSWORD}
  JWT_SECRET: ${JWT_SECRET}

# k8s/deployment.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: user-service
  namespace: microservices
  labels:
    app: user-service
    version: v1
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: user-service
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: user-service
        version: v1
      annotations:
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port: "8080"
        prometheus.io/path: "/actuator/prometheus"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: microservices-sa
      containers:
      - name: user-service
        image: microservices/user-service:latest
        imagePullPolicy: Always
        ports:
        - containerPort: 8080
          name: http
        env:
        - name: SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE
          value: "k8s"
        - name: DB_PASSWORD
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: microservices-secrets
              key: DB_PASSWORD
        - name: REDIS_PASSWORD
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: microservices-secrets
              key: REDIS_PASSWORD
        resources:
          requests:
            memory: "256Mi"
            cpu: "250m"
          limits:
            memory: "512Mi"
            cpu: "500m"
        livenessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /actuator/health/liveness
            port: 8080
          initialDelaySeconds: 60
          periodSeconds: 10
        readinessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /actuator/health/readiness
            port: 8080
          initialDelaySeconds: 30
          periodSeconds: 5
        startupProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /actuator/health
            port: 8080
          failureThreshold: 30
          periodSeconds: 10
        volumeMounts:
        - name: config
          mountPath: /app/config
        - name: logs
          mountPath: /app/logs
      volumes:
      - name: config
        configMap:
          name: microservices-config
      - name: logs
        emptyDir: {}
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 60

---
# k8s/service.yaml

# k8s/hpa.yaml

kind: HorizontalPodAutoscaler
metadata:
  name: user-service-hpa
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  scaleTargetRef:
    apiVersion: apps/v1
    kind: Deployment
    name: user-service
  minReplicas: 2
  maxReplicas: 10
  metrics:
  - type: Resource
    resource:
      name: cpu
      target:
        type: Utilization
        averageUtilization: 70
  - type: Resource
    resource:
      name: memory
      target:
        type: Utilization
        averageUtilization: 80
  - type: Pods
    pods:
      metric:
        name: http_requests_per_second
      target:
        type: AverageValue
        averageValue: 100
  behavior:
    scaleDown:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 300
      policies:
      - type: Percent
        value: 50
        periodSeconds: 60
    scaleUp:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 0
      policies:
      - type: Percent
        value: 100
        periodSeconds: 30
      - type: Pods
        value: 4
        periodSeconds: 30
      selectPolicy: Max
2.2 Advanced Kubernetes Patterns
yaml
# k8s/ingress.yaml
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
  name: microservices-ingress
  namespace: microservices
  annotations:
    kubernetes.io/ingress.class: nginx
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/rewrite-target: /
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/ssl-redirect: "true"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-body-size: "10m"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-read-timeout: "60"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-send-timeout: "60"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/limit-connections: "100"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/limit-rps: "50"
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: letsencrypt-prod
spec:
  tls:
  - hosts:
    - api.microservices.com
    secretName: microservices-tls
  rules:
  - host: api.microservices.com
    http:
      paths:
      - path: /api/users
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: user-service
            port:
              number: 8080
      - path: /api/orders
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: order-service
            port:
              number: 8080
      - path: /api/products
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: product-service
            port:
              number: 8080
      - path: /actuator
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: actuator-service
            port:
              number: 8080

# k8s/network-policy.yaml

kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: microservices-network-policy
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app: microservices
  policyTypes:
  - Ingress
  - Egress
  ingress:
  - from:
    - namespaceSelector:
        matchLabels:
          name: ingress-nginx
    ports:
    - protocol: TCP
      port: 8080
  - from:
    - podSelector:
        matchLabels:
          app: prometheus
    ports:
    - protocol: TCP
      port: 8080
  egress:
  - to:
    - namespaceSelector:
        matchLabels:
          name: kube-system
    ports:
    - protocol: UDP
      port: 53
  - to:
    - namespaceSelector:
        matchLabels:
          name: microservices
    ports:
    - protocol: TCP
      port: 5432
    - protocol: TCP
      port: 6379
    - protocol: TCP
      port: 9092

2.3 StatefulSet for Stateful Services
yaml
# k8s/statefulset-cassandra.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: StatefulSet
metadata:
  name: cassandra
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  serviceName: cassandra
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: cassandra
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: cassandra
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: cassandra
        image: cassandra:4.0
        ports:
        - containerPort: 9042
          name: cql
        - containerPort: 7000
          name: intra-node
        - containerPort: 7001
          name: tls-intra
        env:
        - name: CASSANDRA_SEEDS
          value: "cassandra-0.cassandra.microservices.svc.cluster.local"
        - name: CASSANDRA_CLUSTER_NAME
          value: "MicroservicesCluster"
        - name: CASSANDRA_DC
          value: "datacenter1"
        - name: CASSANDRA_RACK
          value: "rack1"
        - name: CASSANDRA_ENDPOINT_SNITCH
          value: "GossipingPropertyFileSnitch"
        - name: CASSANDRA_NUM_TOKENS
          value: "256"
        - name: MAX_HEAP_SIZE
          value: "1G"
        - name: HEAP_NEWSIZE
          value: "200M"
        resources:
          requests:
            memory: "2Gi"
            cpu: "1"
          limits:
            memory: "4Gi"
            cpu: "2"
        volumeMounts:
        - name: cassandra-data
          mountPath: /var/lib/cassandra
        lifecycle:
          preStop:
            exec:
              command:
              - /bin/sh
              - -c
              - nodetool drain
        livenessProbe:
          exec:
            command:
            - /bin/bash
            - -c
            - nodetool status
          initialDelaySeconds: 120
          periodSeconds: 30
          timeoutSeconds: 10
        readinessProbe:
          exec:
            command:
            - /bin/bash
            - -c
            - nodetool status | grep -E "^UN"
          initialDelaySeconds: 60
          periodSeconds: 10
          timeoutSeconds: 5
  volumeClaimTemplates:
  - metadata:
      name: cassandra-data
    spec:
      accessModes: ["ReadWriteOnce"]
      storageClassName: fast-ssd
      resources:
        requests:
          storage: 100Gi

---
