3. Service Mesh Integration
3.1 Istio Service Mesh Configuration
yaml
# istio/virtual-service.yaml
apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: VirtualService
metadata:
  name: user-service-vs
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  hosts:
  - user-service
  http:
  - match:
    - headers:
        version:
          exact: v2
    route:
    - destination:
        host: user-service
        subset: v2
      weight: 100
  - route:
    - destination:
        host: user-service
        subset: v1
      weight: 90
    - destination:
        host: user-service
        subset: v2
      weight: 10
    retries:
      attempts: 3
      perTryTimeout: 2s
      retryOn: gateway-error,connect-failure,refused-stream
    fault:
      delay:
        percentage:
          value: 5
        fixedDelay: 2s
      abort:
        percentage:
          value: 1
        httpStatus: 500
    timeout: 10s

---
# istio/destination-rule.yaml
apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: DestinationRule
metadata:
  name: user-service-dr
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  host: user-service
  trafficPolicy:
    connectionPool:
      tcp:
        maxConnections: 100
      http:
        http1MaxPendingRequests: 50
        http2MaxRequests: 200
        maxRequestsPerConnection: 10
    loadBalancer:
      simple: LEAST_REQUEST
    outlierDetection:
      consecutive5xxErrors: 5
      interval: 30s
      baseEjectionTime: 30s
      maxEjectionPercent: 50
    tls:
      mode: ISTIO_MUTUAL
  subsets:
  - name: v1
    labels:
      version: v1
    trafficPolicy:
      connectionPool:
        tcp:
          maxConnections: 50
  - name: v2
    labels:
      version: v2
    trafficPolicy:
      connectionPool:
        tcp:
          maxConnections: 150

---
# istio/peer-authentication.yaml
apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: PeerAuthentication
metadata:
  name: default
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  mtls:
    mode: STRICT

---
# istio/authorization-policy.yaml
apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: AuthorizationPolicy
metadata:
  name: microservices-authz
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: microservices
  action: ALLOW
  rules:
  - from:
    - source:
        principals: ["cluster.local/ns/microservices/sa/user-service"]
    to:
    - operation:
        methods: ["GET", "POST"]
        paths: ["/api/users/*"]
  - from:
    - source:
        namespaces: ["ingress-nginx"]
    to:
    - operation:
        methods: ["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE"]

---
# istio/gateway.yaml
apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: Gateway
metadata:
  name: microservices-gateway
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  selector:
    istio: ingressgateway
  servers:
  - port:
      number: 443
      name: https
      protocol: HTTPS
    tls:
      mode: SIMPLE
      credentialName: microservices-tls
    hosts:
    - "api.microservices.com"
  - port:
      number: 80
      name: http
      protocol: HTTP
    hosts:
    - "api.microservices.com"
    tls:
      httpsRedirect: true
3.2 Linkerd Service Mesh
yaml
# linkerd/service-profile.yaml
apiVersion: linkerd.io/v1alpha2
kind: ServiceProfile
metadata:
  name: user-service.microservices.svc.cluster.local
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  routes:
  - name: GET /api/users
    condition:
      method: GET
      pathRegex: /api/users/\d+
  - name: POST /api/users
    condition:
      method: POST
      pathRegex: /api/users
    isRetryable: true
  - name: GET /actuator/health
    condition:
      method: GET
      pathRegex: /actuator/health
    isFailure: false
  retryBudget:
    minRetriesPerSecond: 10
    retryRatio: 0.2
    ttl: 10s

---
# linkerd/authorization-policy.yaml
apiVersion: policy.linkerd.io/v1beta1
kind: AuthorizationPolicy
metadata:
  name: user-service-authz
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  targetRef:
    group: policy.linkerd.io
    kind: Server
    name: user-service
  requiredAuthenticationRefs:
    - name: mtls
      kind: MeshTLSAuthentication
  rules:
  - from:
    - serviceAccount:
        namespace: ingress-nginx
        name: default
4. CI/CD & GitOps
4.1 GitHub Actions Workflow
yaml
# .github/workflows/build-and-deploy.yml
name: Build and Deploy Microservices

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, develop]
    paths-ignore:
      - '**.md'
      - 'docs/**'
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]

env:
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io
  IMAGE_NAME: ${{ github.repository }}

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v3
    
    - name: Set up JDK 17
      uses: actions/setup-java@v3
      with:
        java-version: '17'
        distribution: 'temurin'
        cache: maven
    
    - name: Run tests
      run: mvn clean test
      
    - name: Generate test report
      uses: dorny/test-reporter@v1
      if: success() || failure()
      with:
        name: Maven Tests
        path: target/surefire-reports/*.xml
        reporter: java-junit

  build:
    needs: test
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    if: github.event_name == 'push'
    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v3
    
    - name: Set up Docker Buildx
      uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v2
    
    - name: Log in to Container Registry
      uses: docker/login-action@v2
      with:
        registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
        username: ${{ github.actor }}
        password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    
    - name: Build and push images
      run: |
        SERVICES=("user-service" "order-service" "product-service" "api-gateway")
        for SERVICE in "${SERVICES[@]}"; do
          docker buildx build \
            --platform linux/amd64,linux/arm64 \
            --tag ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ github.repository }}/$SERVICE:${{ github.sha }} \
            --tag ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ github.repository }}/$SERVICE:latest \
            --file $SERVICE/Dockerfile \
            --push \
            .
        done
    
    - name: Trivy vulnerability scan
      uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@master
      with:
        image-ref: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ github.repository }}/user-service:${{ github.sha }}
        format: 'sarif'
        output: 'trivy-results.sarif'
    
    - name: Upload Trivy results
      uses: github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v2
      with:
        sarif_file: 'trivy-results.sarif'

  deploy:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v3
    
    - name: Configure kubectl
      uses: azure/setup-kubectl@v3
      with:
        version: 'latest'
    
    - name: Set up kubeconfig
      run: |
        mkdir -p $HOME/.kube
        echo "${{ secrets.KUBECONFIG }}" | base64 --decode > $HOME/.kube/config
    
    - name: Deploy to Kubernetes
      run: |
        # Update image tags
        sed -i "s|image: microservices/user-service:.*|image: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ github.repository }}/user-service:${{ github.sha }}|g" k8s/deployment.yaml
        
        # Apply manifests
        kubectl apply -f k8s/namespace.yaml
        kubectl apply -f k8s/configmap.yaml
        kubectl apply -f k8s/secrets.yaml
        kubectl apply -f k8s/deployment.yaml
        kubectl apply -f k8s/service.yaml
        kubectl apply -f k8s/hpa.yaml
        kubectl apply -f k8s/ingress.yaml
    
    - name: Wait for rollout
      run: |
        kubectl rollout status deployment/user-service -n microservices --timeout=5m
        kubectl rollout status deployment/order-service -n microservices --timeout=5m
    
    - name: Smoke test
      run: |
        kubectl run smoke-test --image=curlimages/curl -i --rm --restart=Never -- \
          curl -f http://user-service.microservices:8080/actuator/health

  canary:
    needs: deploy
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
    - name: Canary deployment
      run: |
        # Update canary deployment
        kubectl apply -f k8s/canary-deployment.yaml
        
        # Split traffic 10% to canary
        kubectl patch virtualservice user-service-vs -n microservices --type='json' \
          -p='[{"op": "replace", "path": "/spec/http/0/route", "value": [
            {"destination": {"host": "user-service", "subset": "v1"}, "weight": 90},
            {"destination": {"host": "user-service", "subset": "v2"}, "weight": 10}
          ]}]'
        
        # Wait for metrics
        sleep 300
        
        # Analyze metrics
        kubectl run metrics-check --image=curlimages/curl -i --rm --restart=Never -- \
          curl -s http://prometheus.monitoring:9090/api/v1/query?query=istio_requests_total
        
        # Promote if successful
        kubectl patch virtualservice user-service-vs -n microservices --type='json' \
          -p='[{"op": "replace", "path": "/spec/http/0/route", "value": [
            {"destination": {"host": "user-service", "subset": "v2"}, "weight": 100}
          ]}]'
4.2 ArgoCD GitOps Configuration
yaml
# argocd/application.yaml
apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
kind: Application
metadata:
  name: microservices
  namespace: argocd
  finalizers:
    - resources-finalizer.argocd.argoproj.io
spec:
  project: default
  source:
    repoURL: https://github.com/your-org/microservices-config
    targetRevision: main
    path: k8s/overlays/production
    helm:
      valueFiles:
        - values-prod.yaml
  destination:
    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
    namespace: microservices
  syncPolicy:
    automated:
      prune: true
      selfHeal: true
      allowEmpty: false
    syncOptions:
      - CreateNamespace=true
      - PruneLast=true
    retry:
      limit: 5
      backoff:
        duration: 5s
        factor: 2
        maxDuration: 3m

---
# argocd/applicationset.yaml

  name: microservices-appset
  namespace: argocd
spec:
  generators:
  - list:
      elements:
      - cluster: dev
        url: https://dev-k8s.example.com
      - cluster: staging
        url: https://staging-k8s.example.com
      - cluster: prod
        url: https://prod-k8s.example.com
  template:
    metadata:
      name: '{{cluster}}-microservices'
    spec:
      project: default
      source:
        repoURL: https://github.com/your-org/microservices-config
        targetRevision: '{{cluster}}'
        path: k8s/overlays/{{cluster}}
      destination:
        server: '{{url}}'
        namespace: microservices
      syncPolicy:
        automated:
          prune: true
          selfHeal: true
5. Security & Compliance
5.1 Pod Security Policies
yaml
# security/pod-security-policy.yaml
apiVersion: policy/v1beta1
kind: PodSecurityPolicy
metadata:
  name: microservices-psp
  annotations:
    seccomp.security.alpha.kubernetes.io/allowedProfileNames: 'runtime/default'
    seccomp.security.alpha.kubernetes.io/defaultProfileName: 'runtime/default'
spec:
  privileged: false
  allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
  requiredDropCapabilities:
    - ALL
  volumes:
    - 'configMap'
    - 'secret'
    - 'emptyDir'
    - 'persistentVolumeClaim'
  hostNetwork: false
  hostIPC: false
  hostPID: false
  runAsUser:
    rule: 'MustRunAsNonRoot'
  seLinux:
    rule: 'RunAsAny'
  supplementalGroups:
    rule: 'MustRunAs'
    ranges:
      - min: 1000
        max: 65535
  fsGroup:
    rule: 'MustRunAs'
    ranges:
      - min: 1000
        max: 65535
  readOnlyRootFilesystem: true

---
# security/opa-policies.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: opa-policies
  namespace: gatekeeper-system
data:
  deny-privileged-containers.rego: |
    package k8s.deny_privileged_containers
    
    violation[{"msg": msg}] {
      container := input.review.object.spec.containers[_]
      container.securityContext.privileged == true
      msg := sprintf("Privileged container '%v' is not allowed", [container.name])
    }
    
    violation[{"msg": msg}] {
      container := input.review.object.spec.containers[_]
      not container.securityContext.runAsNonRoot == true
      msg := sprintf("Container '%v' must run as non-root", [container.name])
    }
  
  deny-latest-image-tag.rego: |
    package k8s.deny_latest_image_tag
    
    violation[{"msg": msg}] {
      container := input.review.object.spec.containers[_]
      endswith(container.image, ":latest")
      msg := sprintf("Image '%v' uses 'latest' tag which is not allowed", [container.image])
    }
5.2 Runtime Security with Falco
yaml
# falco/falco-config.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: falco-config
  namespace: falco
data:
  falco_rules.local.yaml: |
    - rule: Microservice Unauthorized Process
      desc: Detect unauthorized process execution in containers
      condition: >
        container and
        proc.name in (wget, curl, nc, netcat, socat, ssh, telnet) and
        not proc.name in (sh, bash, dash)
      output: >
        Unauthorized process (%proc.name) executed in container
        (user=%user.name container_id=%container.id image=%container.image)
      priority: WARNING
    
    - rule: Microservice Network Scan
      desc: Detect network scanning from microservices
      condition: >
        container and
        evt.type in (connect, accept) and
        fd.sip in (0.0.0.0, 255.255.255.255) and
        fd.sport in (1-1024)
      output: >
        Network scan detected from container (container_id=%container.id
        image=%container.image proc=%proc.name)
      priority: CRITICAL
    
    - rule: Microservice File System Modification
      desc: Detect unauthorized file system modifications
      condition: >
        container and
        evt.type in (open, write, rename) and
        fd.name startswith /app/config and
        not proc.name in (java, node, python)
      output: >
        Unauthorized file modification in config directory
        (file=%fd.name container=%container.id)
      priority: HIGH

# monitoring/service-monitor.yaml

kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: microservices-servicemonitor
  namespace: monitoring
  labels:
    app: microservices
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: microservices
  endpoints:
  - port: http
    path: /actuator/prometheus
    interval: 30s
    scrapeTimeout: 10s
    relabelings:
    - sourceLabels: [__meta_kubernetes_pod_label_app]
      targetLabel: service
    - sourceLabels: [__meta_kubernetes_namespace]
      targetLabel: namespace
    - sourceLabels: [__meta_kubernetes_pod_node_name]
      targetLabel: node
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames:
    - microservices

---
# monitoring/prometheus-rules.yaml
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: PrometheusRule
metadata:
  name: microservices-alerts
  namespace: monitoring
  labels:
    app: microservices
spec:
  groups:
  - name: microservices
    interval: 30s
    rules:
    - alert: HighErrorRate
      expr: |
        sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count{status=~"5.."}[5m])) 
        / sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_count[5m])) > 0.05
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: critical
        service: "{{ $labels.service }}"
      annotations:
        summary: "High error rate for {{ $labels.service }}"
        description: "Error rate is {{ $value | humanizePercentage }} for {{ $labels.service }}"
    
    - alert: HighResponseTime
      expr: |
        histogram_quantile(0.95, 
          sum(rate(http_server_requests_seconds_bucket[5m])) by (le, service)) > 1
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: warning
        service: "{{ $labels.service }}"
      annotations:
        summary: "High response time for {{ $labels.service }}"
        description: "95th percentile response time is {{ $value }}s"
    
    - alert: HighMemoryUsage
      expr: |
        (container_memory_working_set_bytes{container!=""} / 
        container_spec_memory_limit_bytes{container!=""}) > 0.9
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: warning
      annotations:
        summary: "High memory usage for {{ $labels.container }}"
        description: "Memory usage is {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}"
    
    - alert: PodCrashLooping
      expr: |
        rate(kube_pod_container_status_restarts_total[15m]) > 0
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: critical
      annotations:
        summary: "Pod {{ $labels.pod }} is crash looping"
        description: "Container {{ $labels.container }} has restarted {{ $value }} times"
6.2 Logging with EFK Stack
yaml
# logging/fluent-bit-daemonset.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: DaemonSet
metadata:
  name: fluent-bit
  namespace: logging
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      name: fluent-bit
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        name: fluent-bit
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: fluent-bit
      containers:
      - name: fluent-bit
        image: fluent/fluent-bit:latest
        volumeMounts:
        - name: varlog
          mountPath: /var/log
        - name: dockercontainers
          mountPath: /var/lib/docker/containers
          readOnly: true
        - name: fluent-bit-config
          mountPath: /fluent-bit/etc/
        env:
        - name: NODE_NAME
          valueFrom:
            fieldRef:
              fieldPath: spec.nodeName
      volumes:
      - name: varlog
        hostPath:
          path: /var/log
      - name: dockercontainers
        hostPath:
          path: /var/lib/docker/containers
      - name: fluent-bit-config
        configMap:
          name: fluent-bit-config

---
# logging/fluent-bit-config.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: fluent-bit-config
  namespace: logging
data:
  fluent-bit.conf: |
    [SERVICE]
        Flush         1
        Log_Level     info
        Daemon        off
        Parsers_File  parsers.conf
    
    [INPUT]
        Name              tail
        Path              /var/log/containers/*.log
        Parser            docker
        Tag               kube.*
        Mem_Buf_Limit     50MB
        Skip_Long_Lines   On
    
    [FILTER]
        Name                kubernetes
        Match               kube.*
        Kube_URL            https://kubernetes.default.svc:443
        Kube_Tag_Prefix     kube.var.log.containers.
        Merge_Log           On
        Merge_Log_Key       log_processed
        K8S-Logging.Parser  On
        K8S-Logging.Exclude On
    
    [FILTER]
        Name                modify
        Match               *
        Add                 environment production
        Add                 cluster microservices
    
    [OUTPUT]
        Name                es
        Match               *
        Host                elasticsearch.logging
        Port                9200
        Index               microservices-logs-%Y.%m.%d
        Type                _doc
        Retry_Limit         False
        Logstash_Format     On
        Logstash_Prefix     microservices
        Replace_Dots        On

  parsers.conf: |
    [PARSER]
        Name        docker
        Format      json
        Time_Key    time
        Time_Format %Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%L
        Time_Keep   On
7. Advanced Container Patterns
7.1 Sidecar Pattern
yaml
# patterns/sidecar-pattern.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: service-with-sidecar
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  replicas: 3
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: service-with-sidecar
    spec:
      containers:
      # Main Application Container
      - name: app
        image: microservices/app:latest
        ports:
        - containerPort: 8080
        env:
        - name: LOG_LEVEL
          value: "INFO"
        volumeMounts:
        - name: shared-logs
          mountPath: /var/log/app
        
      # Logging Sidecar
      - name: log-shipper
        image: fluent/fluent-bit:latest
        env:
        - name: FLUENT_ELASTICSEARCH_HOST
          value: "elasticsearch.logging"
        volumeMounts:
        - name: shared-logs
          mountPath: /var/log/app
          readOnly: true
        - name: fluent-bit-config
          mountPath: /fluent-bit/etc/
      
      # Metrics Sidecar
      - name: metrics-exporter
        image: prom/statsd-exporter:latest
        args: ["--statsd.mapping-config=/etc/statsd/mappings.yml"]
        ports:
        - containerPort: 9102
          name: metrics
        volumeMounts:
        - name: statsd-config
          mountPath: /etc/statsd
      
      # Security Sidecar (Istio)
      - name: istio-proxy
        image: istio/proxyv2:latest
        args:
        - proxy
        - sidecar
        - --domain
        - $(POD_NAMESPACE).svc.cluster.local
        - --serviceCluster
        - service-with-sidecar
        - --proxyLogLevel=warning
        env:
        - name: POD_NAMESPACE
          valueFrom:
            fieldRef:
              fieldPath: metadata.namespace
        - name: INSTANCE_IP
          valueFrom:
            fieldRef:
              fieldPath: status.podIP
        - name: SERVICE_ACCOUNT
          valueFrom:
            fieldRef:
              fieldPath: spec.serviceAccountName
        ports:
        - containerPort: 15001
          name: envoy
        - containerPort: 15090
          name: envoy-stats
      
      volumes:
      - name: shared-logs
        emptyDir: {}
      - name: fluent-bit-config
        configMap:
          name: fluent-bit-config
      - name: statsd-config
        configMap:
          name: statsd-mappings
7.2 Ambassador Pattern
yaml
# patterns/ambassador-pattern.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: database-ambassador
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  replicas: 2
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: database-ambassador
    spec:
      containers:
      # Database Proxy (Ambassador)
      - name: db-proxy
        image: bitnami/pgpool:latest
        env:
        - name: PGPOOL_BACKEND_NODES
          value: "postgres-0.postgres:5432,postgres-1.postgres:5432,postgres-2.postgres:5432"
        - name: PGPOOL_NUM_INIT_CHILDREN
          value: "32"
        - name: PGPOOL_MAX_POOL
          value: "100"
        - name: PGPOOL_CHILD_LIFE_TIME
          value: "300"
        - name: PGPOOL_CONNECTION_CACHE
          value: "on"
        ports:
        - containerPort: 5432
          name: postgresql
        - containerPort: 9898
          name: admin
        livenessProbe:
          exec:
            command:
            - /bin/bash
            - -c
            - "pcp_pool_status -h localhost -p 9898 -U admin"
          initialDelaySeconds: 30
          periodSeconds: 10
        resources:
          requests:
            memory: "512Mi"
            cpu: "500m"
          limits:
            memory: "1Gi"
            cpu: "1000m"
      
      # Connection Pooler Sidecar
      - name: connection-pooler
        image: pgbouncer/pgbouncer:latest
        volumeMounts:
        - name: pgbouncer-config
          mountPath: /etc/pgbouncer
        ports:
        - containerPort: 6432
          name: pooled
      
      volumes:
      - name: pgbouncer-config
        configMap:
          name: pgbouncer-config

---
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: pgbouncer-config
  namespace: microservices
data:
  pgbouncer.ini: |
    [databases]
    * = host=localhost port=5432
    
    [pgbouncer]
    listen_addr = 0.0.0.0
    listen_port = 6432
    auth_type = md5
    auth_file = /etc/pgbouncer/userlist.txt
    pool_mode = transaction
    default_pool_size = 20
    max_client_conn = 1000
    idle_transaction_timeout = 30
    query_timeout = 60
    client_idle_timeout = 600
    server_idle_timeout = 600
  
  userlist.txt: |
    "appuser" "md5hashedpassword"
7.3 Init Container Pattern
yaml
# patterns/init-container-pattern.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: service-with-init
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  replicas: 3
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: service-with-init
    spec:
      initContainers:
      # Database Migration
      - name: db-migration
        image: microservices/flyway-migrations:latest
        env:
        - name: DB_URL
          value: "jdbc:postgresql://postgres-user:5432/userdb"
        - name: DB_USER
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: microservices-secrets
              key: DB_USER
        - name: DB_PASSWORD
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: microservices-secrets
              key: DB_PASSWORD
        volumeMounts:
        - name: migration-scripts
          mountPath: /flyway/sql
      
      # Schema Validation
      - name: schema-validator
        image: microservices/schema-validator:latest
        command:
        - /bin/sh
        - -c
        - |
          echo "Validating schema..."
          schema-validator --path /schemas --validate
        volumeMounts:
        - name: schemas
          mountPath: /schemas
      
      # Dependency Check
      - name: dependency-check
        image: curlimages/curl:latest
        command:
        - /bin/sh
        - -c
        - |
          until curl -f http://redis:6379; do
            echo "Waiting for Redis..."
            sleep 5
          done
          until curl -f http://kafka:9092; do
            echo "Waiting for Kafka..."
            sleep 5
          done
          echo "All dependencies ready"
      
      # Config Fetch
      - name: config-fetcher
        image: microservices/config-fetcher:latest
        env:
        - name: CONFIG_SERVER_URL
          value: "http://config-server:8888"
        volumeMounts:
        - name: app-config
          mountPath: /app/config
      
      containers:
      - name: app
        image: microservices/app:latest
        volumeMounts:
        - name: app-config
          mountPath: /app/config
          readOnly: true
        - name: shared-data
          mountPath: /app/data
      
      volumes:
      - name: migration-scripts
        configMap:
          name: migration-scripts
      - name: schemas
        configMap:
          name: schemas
      - name: app-config
        emptyDir: {}
      - name: shared-data
        emptyDir: {}
8. Best Practices & Optimization
8.1 Resource Optimization
yaml
# optimization/resource-quotas.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ResourceQuota
metadata:
  name: microservices-quota
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  hard:
    requests.cpu: "20"
    requests.memory: 40Gi
    limits.cpu: "40"
    limits.memory: 80Gi
    persistentvolumeclaims: "10"
    pods: "50"
    services: "20"
    secrets: "100"
    configmaps: "100"

---
# optimization/limit-range.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: LimitRange
metadata:
  name: microservices-limits
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  limits:
  - max:
      cpu: "2"
      memory: 2Gi
    min:
      cpu: "100m"
      memory: 128Mi
    default:
      cpu: "500m"
      memory: 512Mi
    defaultRequest:
      cpu: "250m"
      memory: 256Mi
    type: Container

---
# optimization/vertical-pod-autoscaler.yaml
apiVersion: autoscaling.k8s.io/v1
kind: VerticalPodAutoscaler
metadata:
  name: user-service-vpa
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  targetRef:
    apiVersion: "apps/v1"
    kind: Deployment
    name: user-service
  updatePolicy:
    updateMode: "Auto"
  resourcePolicy:
    containerPolicies:
    - containerName: user-service
      minAllowed:
        cpu: "250m"
        memory: "256Mi"
      maxAllowed:
        cpu: "2"
        memory: "2Gi"
      controlledResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
8.2 Cost Optimization
yaml
# optimization/spot-instances.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: user-service
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      nodeSelector:
        node-type: spot
      tolerations:
      - key: "spotInstance"
        operator: "Equal"
        value: "true"
        effect: "NoSchedule"
      - key: "spotInstance"
        operator: "Equal"
        value: "true"
        effect: "PreferNoSchedule"

---
# optimization/cluster-autoscaler.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: cluster-autoscaler-status
  namespace: kube-system
data:
  cluster-autoscaler: |
    expanders:
      - priority
    expander-priority:
      - "spot"
      - "on-demand"
    balance-similar-node-groups: true
    skip-nodes-with-local-storage: false
    skip-nodes-with-system-pods: true
    max-nodes-total: 100
    cores-total: 0:200
    memory-total: 0:800Gi
Best Practices Summary
Container Best Practices
Use multi-stage builds: Optimize image size

Run as non-root: Enhance security

Use specific tags: Avoid :latest

Implement health checks: Liveness and readiness probes

Set resource limits: Prevent resource exhaustion

Kubernetes Best Practices
Use namespaces: For environment isolation

Implement RBAC: Least privilege principle

Use network policies: For micro-segmentation

Implement pod disruption budgets: For high availability

Use horizontal pod autoscaler: For automatic scaling

Service Mesh Best Practices
Implement mTLS: For service-to-service encryption

Use circuit breakers: For resilience

Implement retry policies: With exponential backoff

Use traffic splitting: For canary deployments

Monitor service mesh: Track SLOs and SLIs

CI/CD Best Practices
Implement GitOps: Declarative infrastructure

Use multi-stage pipelines: Build, test, deploy

Implement canary deployments: Gradual rollout

Use image scanning: Vulnerability detection

Automate rollbacks: On deployment failure

Security Best Practices
Use pod security policies: Restrict privileged containers

Implement OPA policies: Fine-grained authorization

Use secrets management: Vault, AWS Secrets Manager

Scan images: For vulnerabilities

Audit all changes: Maintain audit logs

Monitoring Best Practices
Collect metrics: CPU, memory, network, disk

Implement distributed tracing: For request flows

Centralize logging: EFK or Loki

Set up alerts: For anomalies and failures

Create dashboards: For visualization

This comprehensive guide provides production-ready implementations for containerizing microservices and orchestrating them with Kubernetes, including advanced patterns, security practices, and operational excellence.

├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                             │
│                         ┌──────────────────────┐                           │
│                    ┌───►│      CLOSED         │                           │

│                    │               │                                       │

│                    │               │ Exceeded                              │

│                    │    ┌──────────────────────┐                           │
│                    │    │      OPEN           │                           │
│                    │    │   (Circuit Open)    │                           │
│                    │    └──────────┬───────────┘                           │

│                    │               │ (Wait Duration)                       │

│                    │     │                   │                            │
│                    │     │ Success          │ Failure                     │
│                    │     │                  │                            │
│                    │     ▼                  ▼                            │
│                    │  ┌──────────┐      ┌──────────┐                      │

│                    APPLICATION ARCHITECTURE                                 │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                             │

│  │                        Client Application                            │  │
│  └───────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                  │                                         │
│                                  ▼                                         │
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │                    Circuit Breaker Proxy                             │  │
│  │  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │  │
│  │  │  State: CLOSED          Failure Rate: 5%                      │  │  │
│  │  │  Success Calls: 95       Failed Calls: 5                      │  │  │
│  │  │  Total Calls: 100        Call Duration: 150ms                 │  │  │
│  │  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │  │
│  └───────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                  │                                         │
│                    ┌─────────────┴─────────────┐                          │
│                    │                           │                          │
│                    ▼                           ▼                          │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────────────────┐        │
│  │    Successful Response      │ │    Fallback Response        │        │
│  │    ┌───────────────────┐    │ │    ┌───────────────────┐    │        │
│  │    │ Downstream Service │    │ │    │   Default Value  │    │        │
│  │    │     Response      │    │ │    │   Cache Response │    │        │
│  │    └───────────────────┘    │ │    │   Error Message  │    │        │
│  └─────────────────────────────┘ └─────────────────────────────┘        │
│                                                                             │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
2. Resilience4j Circuit Breaker States
2.1 Complete State Implementation
java
package com.example.resilience.circuitbreaker;

import io.github.resilience4j.circuitbreaker.CircuitBreaker;
import io.github.resilience4j.circuitbreaker.CircuitBreakerConfig;
import io.github.resilience4j.circuitbreaker.CircuitBreakerRegistry;
import io.github.resilience4j.circuitbreaker.event.*;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import javax.annotation.PostConstruct;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

@Slf4j
@Component
public class CircuitBreakerStateManager {
    
    private CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker;
    private CircuitBreakerRegistry registry;
    
    // State transition counters
    private AtomicInteger closedToOpenTransitions = new AtomicInteger(0);
    private AtomicInteger openToHalfOpenTransitions = new AtomicInteger(0);
    private AtomicInteger halfOpenToClosedTransitions = new AtomicInteger(0);
    private AtomicInteger halfOpenToOpenTransitions = new AtomicInteger(0);
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        // Create circuit breaker configuration
        CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            .failureRateThreshold(50)                    // 50% failure rate threshold
            .slowCallRateThreshold(50)                   // 50% slow call threshold
            .slowCallDurationThreshold(Duration.ofSeconds(2)) // 2 seconds slow call threshold
            .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(5)    // 5 calls allowed in half-open
            .maxWaitDurationInHalfOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(30)) // Max time in half-open
            .slidingWindowType(CircuitBreakerConfig.SlidingWindowType.COUNT_BASED)
            .slidingWindowSize(20)                       // 20 calls in sliding window
            .minimumNumberOfCalls(10)                    // Minimum calls before calculating
            .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(60)) // 60 seconds in open state
            .automaticTransitionFromOpenToHalfOpenEnabled(true)
            .recordExceptions(Throwable.class)           // Record all exceptions as failures
            .ignoreExceptions(IllegalArgumentException.class) // Ignore these exceptions
            .build();
        
        // Create circuit breaker registry
        registry = CircuitBreakerRegistry.of(config);
        
        // Create circuit breaker
        circuitBreaker = registry.circuitBreaker("user-service");
        
        // Register event listeners for state transitions
        registerEventListeners();
        
        log.info("Circuit Breaker initialized with configuration: {}", config);
    }
    
    private void registerEventListeners() {
        // Listen for state transitions
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.StateTransition transition = event.getStateTransition();
                log.info("Circuit Breaker State Transition: {} -> {}", 
                    transition.getFromState(), transition.getToState());
                
                // Track transitions
                switch (transition.getToState()) {
                    case OPEN:
                        closedToOpenTransitions.incrementAndGet();
                        log.warn("Circuit Breaker OPENED! Failure threshold exceeded.");
                        break;
                    case HALF_OPEN:
                        openToHalfOpenTransitions.incrementAndGet();
                        log.info("Circuit Breaker HALF_OPEN - Testing if service recovered");
                        break;
                    case CLOSED:
                        if (transition.getFromState() == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                            halfOpenToClosedTransitions.incrementAndGet();
                            log.info("Circuit Breaker CLOSED - Service recovered successfully!");
                        }
                        break;
                }
            });
        
        // Listen for failure events
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onFailureEvent(event -> {
                log.error("Circuit Breaker Failure Event - Duration: {}ms, Exception: {}", 
                    event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis(), 
                    event.getCreationTime());
                
                // Record metrics
                recordFailureMetrics(event);
            });
        
        // Listen for success events
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onSuccessEvent(event -> {
                log.debug("Circuit Breaker Success Event - Duration: {}ms", 
                    event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis());
                
                // Record metrics
                recordSuccessMetrics(event);
            });
        
        // Listen for call not permitted events (when circuit is open)
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onCallNotPermittedEvent(event -> {
                log.warn("Circuit Breaker Call NOT PERMITTED - Circuit is OPEN");
                
                // Track rejected calls
                meterRegistry.counter("circuitbreaker.rejected.calls",
                    "circuit", circuitBreaker.getName()).increment();
            });
        
        // Listen for state transition events
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                // Update metrics for state changes
                meterRegistry.gauge("circuitbreaker.state",
                    circuitBreaker.getState().ordinal(),
                    "circuit", circuitBreaker.getName());
            });
    }
    
    // Method to check circuit breaker state
    public CircuitBreaker.State getCurrentState() {
        return circuitBreaker.getState();
    }
    
    // Method to get metrics
    public CircuitBreakerMetrics getMetrics() {
        CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = circuitBreaker.getMetrics();
        
        return CircuitBreakerMetrics.builder()
            .state(circuitBreaker.getState())
            .failureRate(metrics.getFailureRate())
            .slowCallRate(metrics.getSlowCallRate())
            .totalCalls(metrics.getNumberOfSuccessfulCalls() + metrics.getNumberOfFailedCalls())
            .successfulCalls(metrics.getNumberOfSuccessfulCalls())
            .failedCalls(metrics.getNumberOfFailedCalls())
            .slowCalls(metrics.getNumberOfSlowCalls())
            .notPermittedCalls(metrics.getNumberOfNotPermittedCalls())
            .closedToOpenTransitions(closedToOpenTransitions.get())
            .openToHalfOpenTransitions(openToHalfOpenTransitions.get())
            .halfOpenToClosedTransitions(halfOpenToClosedTransitions.get())
            .halfOpenToOpenTransitions(halfOpenToOpenTransitions.get())
            .build();
    }
    
    @Data
    @Builder
    public static class CircuitBreakerMetrics {
        private CircuitBreaker.State state;
        private Float failureRate;
        private Float slowCallRate;
        private Long totalCalls;
        private Long successfulCalls;
        private Long failedCalls;
        private Long slowCalls;
        private Long notPermittedCalls;
        private Integer closedToOpenTransitions;
        private Integer openToHalfOpenTransitions;
        private Integer halfOpenToClosedTransitions;
        private Integer halfOpenToOpenTransitions;
    }
}
